📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在第29天,IVX-A12和RSVPreF3施用增加了RSV A中和抗体(nAb)水平,几何平均倍数上升(GMFR)分别为3.4和6.5,以及RSV B nAb水平,GMFR分别为2.9和5.4。IVX-A12(而非 RSVPreF3)也增加了 hMPV A (GMFR 3.0) 和 hMPV B (GMFR 2.9) nAb 水平。
  • 在1012名因急性呼吸道感染(ARI)住院的婴儿中,有655名患有下呼吸道疾病(LRTD),其中386名病例婴儿中有114名(30%)和269名对照婴儿中有171名(64%)出生于呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)preF疫苗接种的母亲。疫苗对RSV-LRTD住院的保护效果为77.8%(95%置信区间,66.4%-85.3%),在出生至6个月龄的婴儿中观察到更高的保护效果。
  • RSV 抗原检测的敏感性范围为 20.6%(腺病毒)至 79.7%(hMPV),而所有六个目标的特异性均超过 99%。对于 RSV,抗原检测阳性率随病毒载量急剧增加,病毒载量≥10^6–10^7 拷贝/100 μL 时接近 90–100%,病毒载量≤10^4–10^5 拷贝/100 μL 时下降到 50% 以下。
  • 该审查支持扩大对患有严重RSV疾病并进入第二个RSV季节的8至19个月婴儿和儿童RSV免疫的高风险标准。这表明对于这个特定年龄组需要有针对性的免疫策略的共识。 [10.1542/peds.2026-079047]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • At Day 29, IVX-A12 and RSVPreF3 administration increased RSV A neutralizing antibody (nAb) levels with geometric mean fold rise (GMFR) of 3.4 and 6.5, and RSV B nAb levels with GMFR of 2.9 and 5.4, respectively.
  • Of 1012 infants hospitalized with ARI, 655 had lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD), with 114 of 386 case infants (30%) and 171 of 269 control infants (64%) born to RSVpreF-vaccinated mothers.
  • RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes, with VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations at 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%), and varying by age and residency duration. [10.1001/jamainternmed.2026.3841].
  • Sensitivity of the antigen tests for RSV ranged from 20.6% (adenovirus) to 79.7% (hMPV), while specificity exceeded 99% for all six targets. [10.1128/jcm.00964-26].

  • 韩国的一些产后护理中心需要RSV 疫苗接种才能入院,济州岛的一家产后护理中心于2026年9月出现RSV感染暴发,导致暂时关闭。韩国疾病管理本部报告称,去年产后护理中心发生 22 例 RSV 群感染,创历史新高,今年上半年则有 15 例。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Respiratory syncytial virus news developments.
  • The CDC published an article titled 'Illness Levels Over Time | Respiratory Illnesses' discussing respiratory illnesses, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). [news-4265ae8716946c8a].
  • An increase in RSV hospitalizations among the elderly population, with a significant shift in the age composition of patients, reported by the Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. [news-0ca3bf28f0b28c81].
  • A study published by CIDRAP found that children with underlying medical conditions (UMCs) hospitalized with RSV had significantly higher medical costs compared to those without UMCs.
  • Some postpartum care centers in Korea require RSV vaccination for admission, and a postpartum care center in Jeju had an outbreak of RSV infection in September 2026, leading to temporary closure. [news-ab4ade8405097539].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: JAMA pediatrics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-01  |  DOI: 10.1001/jamapediatrics.2026.3391  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 180, 11, 1269-1281
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗 对住院婴儿中 呼吸道合胞病毒 的有效性。

Maternal RSVpreF Vaccine Effectiveness Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Hospitalized Infants.
作者： Ushma Wadia, Madeline M. Ong, Sarah N. Cox, Huong Le, Damien Foo, Shauna Wolf Mankiewicz, Jeremy Carr, Helen S. Marshall, Julia E. Clark, Philip N. Britton
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估继 2025 年 2 月在澳大利亚国家免疫计划中引入 疫苗 后,澳大利亚妊娠期间母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 对婴儿 RSV 住院治疗的有效性。
设计与对象
一项采用测试阴性设计的回顾性病例对照研究在澳大利亚9家医院进行,研究对象为2025年3月1日至2026年2月28日期间妊娠28周出生、6个月以下因急性呼吸道疾病(ARI)住院的婴儿。
核心方法
使用混杂因素调整的逻辑回归将 疫苗 有效性 (VE) 计算为(1 - 调整后的比值比)× 100%,数据分析时间为 2026 年 3 月至 2026 年 5 月。
主要结果
在1012名因急性呼吸道感染(ARI)住院的婴儿中,有655名患有下呼吸道疾病(LRTD),其中386名病例婴儿中有114名(30%)和269名对照婴儿中有171名(64%)出生于呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)preF疫苗接种的母亲。疫苗对RSV-LRTD住院的保护效果为77.8%(95%置信区间,66.4%-85.3%),在出生至6个月龄的婴儿中观察到更高的保护效果。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为,RSVpreF 疫苗接种 对 6 个月或以下婴儿的 RSV 相关住院治疗具有全年较高的有效性,在最早的年龄间隔中观察到的有效性最高。
科研与公卫意义
结果为母亲 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 在预防小婴儿 RSV 住院方面的有效性提供了谨慎的证据,这可以为未来的 疫苗接种 策略和公共卫生建议提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性病例对照研究,采用测试阴性设计,可能会引入偏差,并且证据受到样本量的限制,未来需要进行分析来评估其他亚组之间的有效性。

Maternal RSVpreF Vaccine Effectiveness Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Hospitalized Infants.

Authors: Ushma Wadia, Madeline M. Ong, Sarah N. Cox, Huong Le, Damien Foo, Shauna Wolf Mankiewicz, Jeremy Carr, Helen S. Marshall, Julia E. Clark, Philip N. Britton
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: In February 2025, Australia introduced maternal vaccination with bivalent respiratory syncytial virus prefusion F protein-based vaccine (RSVpreF) in the National Immunization Program for pregnant individuals from 28 weeks' gestation as the primary, year-round strategy to prevent RSV disease in infants 6 months of age or younger. OBJECTIVE: To estimate effectiveness of RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy against infant RSV hospitalization in Australia. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This retrospective case-control study with a test-negative design was conducted in 9 hospitals across Australia with year-round respiratory testing for acute respiratory illness (ARI) among infants born from 28 weeks' gestation and hospitalized with ARI at 6 months of age or younger, March 1, 2025, through February 28, 2026. Data were analyzed March 2026 through May 2026. EXPOSURE: Maternal RSVpreF vaccination administered from 28 weeks' gestation and 14 days or more before delivery. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Maternal RSVpreF effectiveness was evaluated against hospitalization for RSV lower respiratory tract disease (RSV-LRTD, primary objective), severe RSV-LRTD, and RSV-ARI. Logistic regression with confounder adjustment was used to calculate vaccine effectiveness (VE) as (1 - adjusted odds ratio) × 100%. RESULTS: Of 1012 infants hospitalized with ARI, 655 had LRTD, with 114 of 386 case infants (30%) and 171 of 269 control infants (64%) born to RSVpreF-vaccinated mothers (median [IQR] gestational age at vaccination, 31 weeks [29-33]). Median (IQR) maternal age at delivery was 32 years (29-35). Median (IQR) age of infants hospitalized for LRTD was 52 days (30-89); 495 of 655 (76%) were aged 3 months or younger. VE against RSV-LRTD hospitalization was 77.8% (95% CI, 66.4%-85.3%) among infants from birth through 6 months of age, 80.2% (95% CI, 71.1%-86.5%) among infants from birth through 3 months of age, 86.3% (95% CI, 79.0%-91.1%) among infants from birth through 2 months of age, and 68.2% (95% CI, 42.2%-82.5%) among infants older than 2 months through 4 months of age. Comparable VE among infants aged 6 months or younger was observed for severe RSV-LRTD (80.4%; 95% CI, 54.4%-91.6%) and RSV-ARI (80.8%; 95% CI, 70.4%-87.6%) hospitalization, with similar age-specific trends. VE was consistent across stratifications by gestational age at vaccination, vaccination to delivery time interval, and proximal administration with other vaccines during pregnancy. Descriptively, among all infants hospitalized with ARI, 161 of 499 (32%) born to RSVpreF-unvaccinated mothers had severe LRTD, while 87 of 513 (17%) born to RSVpreF-vaccinated mothers had severe LRTD. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: Findings in this case-control study demonstrate high year-round effectiveness of RSVpreF vaccination against RSV-associated hospitalization in infants 6 months of age or younger in the first year following introduction in Australia, with the highest VE observed in the earliest age intervals. Future analyses with larger sample sizes will evaluate VE among additional subgroups.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate the effectiveness of maternal RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy against infant RSV hospitalization in Australia, following the introduction of the vaccine in the National Immunization Program in February 2025.
Design and population
A retrospective case-control study with a test-negative design was conducted in 9 hospitals across Australia, involving infants born from 28 weeks' gestation and hospitalized with acute respiratory illness (ARI) at 6 months of age or younger, from March 1, 2025, through February 28, 2026.
Core methods
Logistic regression with confounder adjustment was used to calculate vaccine effectiveness (VE) as (1 - adjusted odds ratio) × 100%, with data analyzed from March 2026 through May 2026.
Main results
Of 1012 infants hospitalized with ARI, 655 had lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD), with 114 of 386 case infants (30%) and 171 of 269 control infants (64%) born to RSVpreF-vaccinated mothers. Vaccine effectiveness against RSV-LRTD hospitalization was 77.8% (95% CI, 66.4%-85.3%) among infants from birth through 6 months of age, with higher effectiveness observed in younger age groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating high year-round effectiveness of RSVpreF vaccination against RSV-associated hospitalization in infants 6 months of age or younger, with the highest effectiveness observed in the earliest age intervals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide cautious evidence for the effectiveness of maternal RSVpreF vaccination in preventing RSV hospitalization in young infants, which could inform future vaccination strategies and public health recommendations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective case-control study with a test-negative design, which may introduce bias, and the evidence is limited by the sample size, with future analyses needed to evaluate effectiveness among additional subgroups.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

呼吸道合胞病毒 和人类偏肺病毒组合病毒样颗粒 疫苗 IVX-A12 在 60 岁以上成年人中的 2 期试验。

A Phase 2 Trial of a Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Metapneumovirus Combination Virus-Like Particle Vaccine IVX-A12 in Adults ≥60 Years of Age.
作者： Temitope Oyedele, Lawrence D. Sher, Kelly Morales, Craig Shapiro, Elizabeth M. Adams, Margaret MacDonald, Judy Wen, John A Pura, Kathryn Shoemaker, Jennifer Price
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
老年人(≥60 岁)容易因感染 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 或人类偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 而出现严重后果。本研究旨在评估针对两种病毒的组合 疫苗 的安全性和免疫原性。
设计与对象
This Phase 2 trial (NCT06481579) randomized adults ≥60 years of age to receive a single dose of IVX-A12 (150 μg RSV/150 μg hMPV) or the licensed RSV vaccine RSVPreF3 120 μg with AS01E adjuvant. A total of 143 participants were enrolled, with 71 receiving IVX-A12 and 72 receiving RSVPreF3.
核心方法
疫苗接种 后 180 天评估安全性和免疫原性。在 疫苗接种 后 7 天报告主动不良反应,并在第 29 天收集主动不良事件 (AE)。在第 181 天记录严重 AE 和就医的 AE。
主要结果
在第29天,IVX-A12和RSVPreF3施用增加了RSV A中和抗体(nAb)水平,几何平均倍数上升(GMFR)分别为3.4和6.5,以及RSV B nAb水平,GMFR分别为2.9和5.4。IVX-A12(而非 RSVPreF3)也增加了 hMPV A (GMFR 3.0) 和 hMPV B (GMFR 2.9) nAb 水平。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,IVX-A12 和 RSVPreF3 具有良好的耐受性,并能引发针对其目标病原体的中和抗体反应。研究表明,与 RSVPreF3 相比,IVX-A12 针对 RSV 和 hMPV 诱导更广泛的 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,IVX-A12 可能对老年人提供针对 RSV 和 hMPV 的增强保护,这可以为未来 疫苗 的开发和呼吸道病毒感染的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单剂量和短期随访,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据是中等的,因为研究结果是基于样本量相对较小的单一试验。

A Phase 2 Trial of a Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Metapneumovirus Combination Virus-Like Particle Vaccine IVX-A12 in Adults ≥60 Years of Age.

Authors: Temitope Oyedele, Lawrence D. Sher, Kelly Morales, Craig Shapiro, Elizabeth M. Adams, Margaret MacDonald, Judy Wen, John A Pura, Kathryn Shoemaker, Jennifer Price
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Older adults (≥60 years) are susceptible to severe outcomes from infection with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) or human metapneumovirus (hMPV). IVX-A12 is an investigational vaccine that uses virus-like particles (VLPs) to elicit an immune response against RSV and hMPV. Methods This Phase 2 trial (NCT06481579) randomized (1:1) adults ≥60 years of age to receive a single dose of IVX-A12 (150 μg RSV/150 μg hMPV) or the licensed RSV vaccine RSVPreF3 120 μg with AS01E adjuvant. Safety and immunogenicity were evaluated through 180 days post-vaccination. Results Overall, 143 participants received IVX-A12 (n = 71) or RSVPreF3 (n = 72). Solicited adverse reactions were reported through 7 days post-vaccination (IVX-A12, 25 [35.2%]; RSVPreF3, 36 [50.0%]). Unsolicited adverse events (AEs) were collected through Day 29 (IVX-A12, 5 [7.0%]; RSVPreF3, 11 [15.3%]). Serious AEs (IVX-A12, 2 [2.8%]; RSVPreF3, 1 [1.4%]) and medically attended AEs (IVX-A12, 1 [1.4%]; RSVPreF3, 9 [12.5%]) were documented through Day 181. There were no AEs of special interest and AEs leading to study discontinuation. At Day 29, IVX-A12 and RSVPreF3 administration increased RSV A neutralizing antibody (nAb) levels with geometric mean fold rise (GMFR) of 3.4 and 6.5, and RSV B nAb levels with GMFR of 2.9 and 5.4, respectively. IVX-A12, but not RSVPreF3, also increased hMPV A (GMFR 3.0) and hMPV B (GMFR 2.9) nAb levels. Conclusions IVX-A12 and RSVPreF3 were well tolerated and elicited nAb responses against their targeted pathogens. Given the neutralizing antibody response following IVX-A12, further investigation of an RSV-hMPV VLP combination vaccine is warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Older adults (≥60 years) are susceptible to severe outcomes from infection with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) or human metapneumovirus (hMPV). This study aimed to evaluate the safety and immunogenicity of a combination vaccine targeting both viruses.
Design and population
This Phase 2 trial (NCT06481579) randomized adults ≥60 years of age to receive a single dose of IVX-A12 (150 μg RSV/150 μg hMPV) or the licensed RSV vaccine RSVPreF3 120 μg with AS01E adjuvant. A total of 143 participants were enrolled, with 71 receiving IVX-A12 and 72 receiving RSVPreF3.
Core methods
Safety and immunogenicity were evaluated through 180 days post-vaccination. Solicited adverse reactions were reported through 7 days post-vaccination, and unsolicited adverse events (AEs) were collected through Day 29. Serious AEs and medically attended AEs were documented through Day 181.
Main results
At Day 29, IVX-A12 and RSVPreF3 administration increased RSV A neutralizing antibody (nAb) levels with geometric mean fold rise (GMFR) of 3.4 and 6.5, and RSV B nAb levels with GMFR of 2.9 and 5.4, respectively. IVX-A12, but not RSVPreF3, also increased hMPV A (GMFR 3.0) and hMPV B (GMFR 2.9) nAb levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that IVX-A12 and RSVPreF3 were well tolerated and elicited neutralizing antibody responses against their targeted pathogens. The study demonstrated that IVX-A12 induced broader immune responses against both RSV and hMPV compared to RSVPreF3.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that IVX-A12 may offer enhanced protection against both RSV and hMPV in older adults, which could inform future vaccine development and public health strategies for respiratory viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single dose and short-term follow-up, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on a single trial with a relatively small sample size.

Journal: Public health research & practice  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-12-01  |  DOI: 10.1071/pu25136  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 4, PU25136
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2019-2023 年悉尼儿童医院网络收治的幼儿的增强型 呼吸道合胞病毒 监测。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.
作者： Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在全面描述被送往悉尼儿童医院网络的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疾病患儿的临床特征、管理及预后,确定严重疾病的总体负担,并识别特定人群进入重症监护病房(ICU)的风险因素。
设计与对象
该研究是一项多中心、回顾性 队列研究 研究,在澳大利亚新南威尔士州大都会的两家三级儿科医院进行了 5 年多的时间,涉及 4414 名因实验室确诊 RSV 住院的 2 岁以下儿童,其中 11.3% 入住 ICU。
核心方法
这项研究是在澳大利亚新南威尔士州大都市的两家三级儿科医院进行的,当时该医院正在实施政府资助的儿童 RSV 免疫计划。主要结果指标是 2 岁以下儿童 RSV 住院和 ICU 入院的特征和 发病率 率。使用多元逻辑回归确定了与 ICU 入院几率较高相关的因素。
主要结果
5 年期间,4414 名儿童因实验室确诊的 RSV 住院,其中 11.3% (499/4414) 入住 ICU。与非 ICU 样本 (n = 1000;3.3 个月 vs 7.1 个月,P < 0.0001) 相比,ICU 队列研究 中的中位年龄 (n = 499) 更年轻。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为,RSV 免疫计划预计将大大减轻澳大利亚儿童第一年的住院负担。他们还发现,土著儿童、早产儿童或患有心脏病的儿童患严重疾病的风险最大。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为土著儿童、早产儿和患有心脏病的儿童制定有针对性的预防计划提供信息,并指导澳大利亚 RSV 免疫计划的实施。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在政府资助的免疫计划实施之前进行的,这可能会限制其普遍性。证据有力,基于全面的数据收集和统计分析进行了高置信度评估。

Enhanced respiratory syncytial virus surveillance of young children admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, 2019-2023.

Authors: Gemma L Saravanos, Kathryn Meredith, Shirley Wong, Rebecca Burrell, Adam Bartlett, Nusrat Homaira, Philip N Britton, Rama Kandasamy
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: In this study, we aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network (SCHN), determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission. STUDY TYPE: Multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years. METHODS: The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. Participants were children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV from 1 January 2019 to 31 December 2023, comprising all ICU admitted, and a random sample of non-ICU admitted patients. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. RESULTS: Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001). Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission in multivariate logistic regression were decreasing month of age (OR 1.10, 95% CI 1.06-1.11), prematurity (OR 2.17, 95% CI 1.61-2.91), Indigenous identity (OR 4.10, 95% CI 2.30-7.56) and cardiac disease (OR 3.39, 95% CI 2.06-5.70). The average hospitalisation incidence rate was 1615 per 100,000 (95% CI 1565-1666), with higher rates among children aged <1 year, 2141 per 100,000 (95% CI 2061-2224) compared with those aged 1-2 years, 1088 per 100,000 (95% CI 1030-1148). CONCLUSIONS: RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. Children who are Indigenous, were born prematurely or have heart disease are at greatest risk of severe disease and may benefit from extended preventative programs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to comprehensively describe the clinical characteristics, management and outcomes of children with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease admitted to the Sydney Children's Hospitals Network, determine the full burden of severe disease and identify population-specific risk factors for intensive care unit (ICU) admission.
Design and population
The study was a multi-centre, retrospective cohort study conducted over 5 years at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, involving 4414 children aged <2 years hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% were admitted to ICU.
Core methods
The study was conducted at two tertiary paediatric hospitals in metropolitan New South Wales, Australia, before implementation of government-funded immunisation programs against RSV in children. The main outcome measures were characteristics and incidence rate of RSV hospitalisations and ICU admissions among children aged <2 years. Factors associated with higher odds of ICU admission were identified using multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
Over a 5-year period, 4414 children were hospitalised with laboratory-confirmed RSV, of whom 11.3% (499/4414) were admitted to ICU. The median age was younger in the ICU cohort (n = 499) compared with the non-ICU sample (n = 1000; 3.3 vs 7.1 months, P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that RSV immunisation programs are expected to substantially reduce the burden of hospitalisation among Australian children in their first year of life. They also identified that Indigenous children, those born prematurely or with cardiac disease are at greatest risk of severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted preventative programs for Indigenous children, those born prematurely and children with cardiac disease, as well as guide the implementation of RSV immunisation programs in Australia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted before the implementation of government-funded immunisation programs, which may limit its generalisability. The evidence is strong, with a high confidence assessment based on the comprehensive data collection and statistical analysis.

呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 居住在疗养院的医疗保险受益人的有效性。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Effectiveness in Medicare Beneficiaries Residing in Nursing Homes.
作者： Ryan E. Wiegand, Heng-Ming Sung, Yue Zhang, Andrea J. Chavez, Kelly M. Hatfield, Amber Kautz, Josephine Mak, Morgan Najdowski, Yangping Chen, Yenlin Lai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 RSV 疫苗接种 对居住在疗养院的医疗保险受益人的有效性,解决高危人群在 RSV 疫苗 可用性第一季期间对 VE 估计的需求。
设计与对象
队列研究 对居住在美国疗养院的 65 岁或以上医疗保险受益人进行的回顾性研究,使用了 2023 年 9 月 10 日至 2024 年 3 月 30 日的电子医疗索赔数据。
核心方法
该研究使用电子医疗索赔数据来识别 RSV 相关结果和 RSV 疫苗接种 状态,应用多变量 Cox 比例风险模型来计算风险比和 VE。
主要结果
RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes, with VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations at 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%), and varying by age and residency duration.
解释与创新
研究发现,RSV 疫苗 与疗养院居民中 RSV 相关住院率和其他严重后果的减少相关,这凸显了 疫苗接种 在这一高危人群中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 疫苗 可以显着减少疗养院居民中与 RSV 相关的严重后果,为未来的 疫苗接种 战略和公共卫生政策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对索赔数据的依赖可能会引入偏差,限制研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Effectiveness in Medicare Beneficiaries Residing in Nursing Homes.

Authors: Ryan E. Wiegand, Heng-Ming Sung, Yue Zhang, Andrea J. Chavez, Kelly M. Hatfield, Amber Kautz, Josephine Mak, Morgan Najdowski, Yangping Chen, Yenlin Lai
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: Vaccine effectiveness (VE) estimates are needed to determine the effectiveness of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccinations during their first season of availability among populations at high risk for severe RSV. OBJECTIVE: To assess RSV VE against RSV-associated outcomes for nursing home residents. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This retrospective cohort study of Medicare fee-for-service beneficiaries used electronic medical claims data from September 10, 2023, to March 30, 2024. A cohort was created for each outcome: an RSV-associated hospitalization cohort; an RSV-associated death cohort; a severe RSV-associated outcome cohort; and an RSV-associated thromboembolic event cohort. To be eligible for inclusion in a cohort, a Medicare fee-for-service beneficiary must be aged 65 years or older, reside in a US nursing home during the study period for at least 1 day, have continuous enrollment in Medicare Parts A and B (365 days prior to the index date), and have continuous enrollment in Part D (beginning on June 21, 2023). EXPOSURE: RSV vaccination. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Outcomes were RSV-associated hospitalization, RSV-associated death, severe RSV-associated outcomes (intensive care unit or critical care unit admission, medical ventilator use, or RSV-associated death), and RSV-associated thromboembolic event. RSV diagnoses and outcome definitions were based on claims with relevant International Classification of Diseases and Related Health Problems, Tenth Revision codes. RSV vaccine doses were determined from Medicare Part D claims using the National Drug Code Directory. Hazard ratios (HRs) from multivariable Cox proportional hazards models compared the times to first outcomes among RSV vaccinated beneficiaries and unvaccinated beneficiaries. VE was calculated as (1-HR) × 100%. RESULTS: Of 597 430 included Medicare beneficiaries (66% female; median age, 82 [IQR, 75-88] years), RSV VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%). Among beneficiaries aged 65 to 74 years VE was 60% (95% CI, 35%-76%), 75% (95% CI, 67%-82%) among beneficiaries 75 years or older, 67% (95% CI, 55%-76%) among short-stay residents (<100 cumulative days), and 80% (95% CI, 68%-87%) among long-stay residents (≥100 cumulative days). VE against RSV-associated death was 56% (95% CI, 39%-69%), VE against severe RSV-associated outcomes was 59% (95% CI, 44%-70%), and VE against RSV-associated thromboembolic events was 66% (95% CI, 44%-79%). CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: In this study, in the first season of availability, RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes among US Medicare beneficiaries residing in nursing homes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the effectiveness of RSV vaccinations among Medicare beneficiaries residing in nursing homes, addressing the need for VE estimates during the first season of RSV vaccine availability for high-risk populations.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study of Medicare beneficiaries aged 65 years or older, residing in US nursing homes, using electronic medical claims data from September 10, 2023, to March 30, 2024.
Core methods
The study used electronic medical claims data to identify RSV-associated outcomes and RSV vaccination status, applying multivariable Cox proportional hazards models to calculate hazard ratios and VE.
Main results
RSV vaccines were associated with improved RSV-associated outcomes, with VE against RSV-associated hospitalizations at 73% (95% CI, 65%-79%), and varying by age and residency duration.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that RSV vaccines were associated with reduced RSV-associated hospitalizations and other severe outcomes among nursing home residents, highlighting the importance of vaccination in this high-risk population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV vaccines can significantly reduce severe RSV-associated outcomes among nursing home residents, informing future vaccination strategies and public health policies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's retrospective design and reliance on claims data may introduce biases, limiting the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

压力之下:从澳大利亚悉尼快速实施 呼吸道合胞病毒 孕产妇 疫苗 和婴儿 尼塞韦单抗 中吸取的经验教训。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.
作者： Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
摘要中文翻译目标:分享悉尼地方卫生区 (SLHD) 推出 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。服务类型:推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和用于新生儿的可注射长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗,以保护他们免受 RSV 感染和住院治疗。方法:以计数和比例的形式对两家 SLHD 妇产医院的 RSV 免疫状况电子病历中的去识别化汇总数据进行描述性分析。实施研究综合框架被用作反思推出过程的镜头。结果:两家 SLHD 医院分娩妇女的 RSV 疫苗接种 覆盖率从推出第一个月(2025 年 2 月)的 4% 提高到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,但没有记录拒绝的原因。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。经验教训:多学科领导者和临床医生的参与对于协调应对和计划成功推出至关重要。利用现有的教育结构、员工会议和使用在线预录音可以优化临床医生教育。新计划需要时间才能融入常规临床实践,而涉及为不同群体提供多种免疫产品的计划需要明确的信息传递和系统保障措施以防止错误。将免疫接种作为标准做法纳入医疗记录工作流程可以支持计划的采用和采用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分享快速实施 RSV 预防产品的经验教训,并提供两家妇产医院母亲和婴儿的免疫接种数据。这项研究需要解决将新的 RSV 预防策略纳入常规临床实践的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在悉尼地方卫生区的两家妇产医院推出 RSV 母体 疫苗 和可注射单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗。该研究时间跨度为 2025 年 2 月至 9 月,重点关注母亲和符合条件的新生儿的免疫接种情况。
核心方法
来自电子病历的去识别化摘要数据被描述性地分析为计数和比例。实施研究综合框架用于评估推广过程并确定影响免疫接种的因素。
主要结果
RSV 疫苗接种 在两家医院分娩的妇女的覆盖率从推出第一个月的 4% 增加到 2025 年 6 月至 9 月的 70-74%。随着产前覆盖率的提高,需要 尼塞韦单抗 的活产婴儿数量减少。在符合条件的新生儿中,36%的家庭拒绝了尼塞韦单抗,拒绝的原因没有记录。随着推广的进展,RSV 免疫状态的记录得到改善。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明多学科领导者和临床医生的协调参与对于项目的成功推出至关重要。该研究强调了在针对不同群体实施多种免疫产品时,明确的信息传递和系统保障措施的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过证明临床医生教育和将免疫接种纳入医疗记录工作流程等策略的有效性,为未来 RSV 预防计划的发展提供信息。他们还提供了有关提高 RSV 疫苗 和单克隆抗体的吸收率和降低拒绝率的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于它依赖于未识别身份的汇总数据,并且没有记录拒绝的原因。由于分析的描述性以及缺乏详细的统计或因果解释,证据的强度中等。

Under pressure: lessons learnt from rapid implementation of the respiratory syncytial virus maternal vaccine and infant nirsevimab in Sydney, Australia.

Authors: Isabella Townshend, Kate Griew, Rosemary Burke, Vendula Blaya-Novakova, Rebekah Dashwood, Sarah Everett, Leena Gupta
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To share lessons learnt from the Sydney Local Health District (SLHD) rollout of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevention products, and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. TYPE OF SERVICE: Rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable, long-acting monoclonal antibody nirsevimab for newborn infants to protect them from RSV infection and hospitalisation. METHODS: Deidentified summary data from the electronic medical record on RSV immunisation status in two SLHD maternity hospitals were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used as a lens to reflect on the rollout process. RESULTS: RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in two SLHD hospitals improved from 4% in the first month of rollout (February 2025) to a range of 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reason for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed. LESSONS LEARNT: Engaging multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for a coordinated response and program rollout success. Leveraging existing education structures, staff meetings and using online prerecording can optimise clinician education. New programs take time to embed into routine clinical practice, and programs that involve delivering multiple immunisation products for distinct groups require clear messaging and systemic safeguards to prevent errors. Integrating immunisation into medical record workflows as standard practice can support program adoption and uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to share lessons from the rapid implementation of RSV prevention products and present data on immunisation uptake for mothers and babies in two maternity hospitals. The research was needed to address the challenge of integrating new RSV prevention strategies into routine clinical practice.
Design and population
The study involved the rollout of the RSV maternal vaccine and the injectable monoclonal antibody nirsevimab in two maternity hospitals within the Sydney Local Health District. The study period spanned from February to September 2025, focusing on immunisation uptake among mothers and eligible newborns.
Core methods
Deidentified summary data from electronic medical records were analysed descriptively as counts and proportions. The Consolidated Framework for Implementation Research was used to evaluate the rollout process and identify factors influencing immunisation uptake.
Main results
RSV vaccination coverage in women giving birth in the two hospitals increased from 4% in the first month of rollout to 70-74% from June to September 2025. As antenatal coverage improved, the number of live born babies requiring nirsevimab decreased. Among eligible newborns, 36% of families declined nirsevimab, with reasons for refusal not recorded. Documentation of RSV immunisation status improved as the rollout progressed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that coordinated engagement with multidisciplinary leaders and clinicians is critical for successful program rollout. The study highlights the importance of clear messaging and systemic safeguards in implementing multiple immunisation products for distinct groups.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of future RSV prevention programs by demonstrating the effectiveness of strategies such as clinician education and integration of immunisation into medical record workflows. They also provide insights into improving uptake and reducing refusal rates for RSV vaccines and monoclonal antibodies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on deidentified summary data and the absence of recorded reasons for refusal. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the descriptive nature of the analysis and the lack of detailed statistical or causal interpretations.

呼吸道合胞病毒 发病率 和炎症性肠病患者的临床结果:国家退伍军人事务部 队列研究 研究。

Respiratory syncytial virus incidence and clinical outcomes in patients with inflammatory bowel disease: a National Veterans Affairs cohort study.
作者： Nabeel Khan, Ramaswamy Sundararajan, Freddy Caldera, Francis A Farraye
摘要中文翻译背景:描述炎症性肠病 (IBD) 患者中 发病率 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染临床结果的数据有限。方法:我们利用退伍军人健康管理局 (VHA) 数据库进行了一项全国范围的回顾性 队列研究 研究。使用先前验证的算法识别患有 IBD 的成人,并使用倾向评分匹配将其与非 IBD 对照进行 1:2 匹配。使用标准化实验室代码识别2021年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间发生的实验室确诊的RSV感染,并通过手动图表判定进行确认。主要结局包括 RSV 发病率 和住院治疗。次要结局包括重症监护病房 (ICU) 入院和按 IBD 药物类别分层的患者结局。结果:倾向评分匹配后,纳入了 52 740 名 IBD 患者和 104 997 名非 IBD 对照。219 名 IBD 患者和 426 名非 IBD 患者发生 RSV 感染。在我们研究期间的所有年份中,发病率 之间的 发病率 之间的 RSV 发生率相似。18.26% 的 IBD-RSV 患者因 RSV 相关住院,而非 IBD 患者则为 13.15% (P = .08)。两组均很少入住 ICU(<1%)。在 IBD 患者中,将生物疗法 (8.93%) 与 5-氨基水杨酸单药疗法 (17.58%) 进行比较时,RSV 相关住院率在不​​同药物类别之间没有显着差异 (P = .15)。结论:在这项全国性的 队列研究 中,与匹配的非 IBD 对照相比,接受先进治疗的 IBD 患者的 RSV 感染率较高,临床结果也较差。不同 IBD 药物类别的 RSV 结果相似。这些发现支持当前的 疫苗接种 建议,并为 IBD 患者的 RSV 风险提供了保证。

Respiratory syncytial virus incidence and clinical outcomes in patients with inflammatory bowel disease: a National Veterans Affairs cohort study.

Authors: Nabeel Khan, Ramaswamy Sundararajan, Freddy Caldera, Francis A Farraye
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Data describing the incidence and clinical outcomes of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection among patients with inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) are limited. METHODS: We conducted a nationwide retrospective cohort study using the Veterans Health Administration (VHA) database. Adults with IBD were identified using a previously validated algorithm and matched 1:2 to non-IBD controls using propensity score matching. Laboratory-confirmed RSV infections that occurred between January 1, 2021, and December 31, 2024, were identified using standardized laboratory codes and confirmed by manual chart adjudication. Primary outcomes included RSV incidence and hospitalizations. Secondary outcomes included intensive care unit (ICU) admission and patient outcomes stratified by IBD medication class. RESULTS: After propensity score matching, 52 740 IBD patients and 104 997 non-IBD controls were included. Infection with RSV occurred in 219 patients with IBD and 426 non-IBD patients. Incidence rates of RSV were similar between cohorts across all years in our study period. Hospitalization related to RSV occurred in 18.26% of IBD-RSV patients compared with 13.15% of non-IBD patients (P = .08). ICU admission was rare in both groups (<1%). Among patients with IBD, RSV-related hospitalization did not differ significantly by medication class, when comparing biologic therapy (8.93%) with 5-aminosalicylic acid monotherapy (17.58%) (P = .15). CONCLUSIONS: In this nationwide cohort, patients with IBD, with predominantly lower exposure to advanced therapies, did not have higher rates of RSV infection or worse clinical outcomes compared with matched non-IBD controls. RSV outcomes were similar across IBD medication classes. These findings support current vaccination recommendations and provide reassurance regarding RSV risk among patients with IBD.
View research seven-element analysis

These findings support current vaccination recommendations and provide reassurance regarding RSV risk among patients with IBD.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

磷酸盐修饰的 PROTEC 通过靶向融合蛋白,成为针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 的有效 抗病毒药物。

Phosphate-modified PROTECs as potent antivirals against respiratory syncytial virus by targeting the fusion protein.
作者： Xiao-Fan Zhang, Song-Ning Wang, Ye Li, Jinaqiang Xu, Wen-Kai Suo, Fang-Xu Pu, Jia-Ming Lan, Ai-Guo Hu
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,这凸显了对具有新颖机制的 抗病毒药物 策略的需求。在这里,我们将蛋白质自由基氧化靶向烯二炔嵌合体 (PROTEC) 策略扩展到 RSV 融合 (F) 蛋白。BMS-433771 被用作蛋白质靶向基团,并与带有磷酸盐亲水性调节基团的基于马来酰亚胺的无环烯二炔缀合,与我们之前的含硫酸盐的 PROTEC 相比,这简化了合成。合成并评估了具有不同接头长度的三种类似物(n = 3、5 和 7)。所有化合物均表现出低细胞毒性 (CC 50 > 100 μM) 和有效的抗 RSV 活性。PROTEC-R7 ( n = 7) 表现出最高的效力,IC 50 为 110.3 nM,选择性指数 > 906。相比之下,非靶向对照 EDY-R 显示出明显较弱的活性 (IC 50 = 16.3 μM)。这些结果证明了靶向递送和接头工程的重要性,并支持 PROTEC 作为靶向病毒表面蛋白的有前途的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是严重呼吸道感染的主要原因,这凸显了对具有新颖机制的 抗病毒药物 策略的需求。
设计与对象
在这里,我们将蛋白质自由基氧化靶向烯二炔嵌合体 (PROTEC) 策略扩展到 RSV 融合 (F) 蛋白。
核心方法

解释与创新
这些结果证明了靶向递送和接头工程的重要性,并支持 PROTEC 作为靶向病毒表面蛋白的有前途的策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,PROTEC-R7 可能是一种有效的 抗病毒药物 抗 RSV 药物,可能会带来新的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究没有提供有关 PROTEC-R7 在临床环境中的长期疗效或安全性的信息,限制了其普遍性。

Phosphate-modified PROTECs as potent antivirals against respiratory syncytial virus by targeting the fusion protein.

Authors: Xiao-Fan Zhang, Song-Ning Wang, Ye Li, Jinaqiang Xu, Wen-Kai Suo, Fang-Xu Pu, Jia-Ming Lan, Ai-Guo Hu
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a major cause of severe respiratory infections, highlighting the need for antiviral strategies with novel mechanisms. Here, we extended our protein-radical-oxidation targeting enediyne chimera (PROTEC) strategy to the RSV fusion (F) protein. BMS-433771 was employed as the protein-targeting group and conjugated to a maleimide-based acyclic enediyne bearing phosphate hydrophilicity-adjusting groups, which simplified synthesis compared with our previous sulfate-containing PROTECs. Three analogues with different linker lengths ( n = 3, 5, and 7) were synthesized and evaluated. All compounds showed low cytotoxicity (CC 50 > 100 μM) and potent anti-RSV activity. PROTEC-R7 ( n = 7) exhibited the highest potency, with an IC 50 of 110.3 nM and a selectivity index > 906. In contrast, the non-targeted control EDY-R showed substantially weaker activity (IC 50 = 16.3 μM). These results demonstrate the importance of targeted delivery and linker engineering and support PROTEC as a promising strategy for targeting viral surface proteins.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a major cause of severe respiratory infections, highlighting the need for antiviral strategies with novel mechanisms.
Design and population
Here, we extended our protein-radical-oxidation targeting enediyne chimera (PROTEC) strategy to the RSV fusion (F) protein.
Core methods
Three analogues with different linker lengths ( n = 3, 5, and 7) were synthesized and evaluated. BMS-433771 was employed as the protein-targeting group and conjugated to a maleimide-based acyclic enediyne bearing phosphate hydrophilicity-adjusting groups, which simplified synthesis compared with our previous sulfate-containing PROTECs.
Main results
All compounds showed low cytotoxicity (CC 50 > 100 μM) and potent anti-RSV activity. PROTEC-R7 ( n = 7) exhibited the highest potency, with an IC 50 of 110.3 nM and a selectivity index > 906. In contrast, the non-targeted control EDY-R showed substantially weaker activity (IC 50 = 16.3 μM).
Interpretation and novelty
These results demonstrate the importance of targeted delivery and linker engineering and support PROTEC as a promising strategy for targeting viral surface proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that PROTEC-R7 could be a potent antiviral agent against RSV, potentially leading to new therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study does not provide information on the long-term efficacy or safety of PROTEC-R7 in clinical settings, limiting its generalizability.

重新利用的 抗病毒药物 及其组合针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 和人类副流感病毒 2 型的体外评估:利巴韦林-奥司他韦协同作用的证据。

In Vitro Evaluation of Repurposed Antivirals and Their Combinations Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Parainfluenza Virus Type 2: Evidence of Ribavirin-Oseltamivir Synergy.
作者： Fatima Nur Olcay, Tuba Sevimoglu, Sibel Dosler
摘要中文翻译治疗呼吸道病毒感染仍然具有挑战性,需要探索新的治疗方法。替代策略,例如药物重新利用或联合用药,对于治疗它们至关重要。本研究评估了 抗病毒药物 利巴韦林、法匹拉韦和奥司他韦以及粘液溶解药物 N-乙酰半胱氨酸 (NAC) 和厄多司坦单独或组合对抗人副流感病毒 (HPIV) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的体外活性。HPIV-2和RSV分别在VERO和HEp-2细胞系中培养。通过使用MTT和噬菌斑减少测定计算IC 50 值来评估抗病毒药物功效。对于组合研究,使用 SynergyFinder 程序分析协同分数。我们使用计算机方法研究了协同活性背后的潜在相互作用,该方法结合了虚拟筛选和分子对接。利巴韦林、法匹拉韦和奥司他韦针对 HPIV-2 和 RSV 的 IC 50 值范围为 4.46 至 14.1 μg/mL,两种方法的结果高度相似。所有三种 抗病毒药物 的细胞毒性均远高于其 C-max,HPIV-2 的 SI 为 12.43-36.69,RSV 的 SI 为 15.88-38.12。利巴韦林-奥司他韦组合对 HPIV-2 的协同得分为 13.35,对 RSV 的协同得分为 17.98,表明有明显的协同作用,而其他组合则具有相加作用。对接分析确定了可能有助于利巴韦林-奥司他韦组合针对 RSV 和 HPIV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性的潜在分子相互作用。利巴韦林、法匹拉韦和奥司他韦对 HPIV-2 和 RSV 表现出良好的活性,并且利巴韦林 - 奥司他韦组合表现出很强的协同相互作用,表明有良好的治疗潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
治疗呼吸道病毒感染仍然具有挑战性,需要探索新的治疗方法。
设计与对象
本研究评估了 抗病毒药物 利巴韦林、法匹拉韦和奥司他韦以及粘液溶解药物 N-乙酰半胱氨酸 (NAC) 和厄多司坦单独或组合对抗人副流感病毒 (HPIV) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的体外活性。
核心方法
通过使用MTT和噬菌斑减少测定计算IC 50 值来评估抗病毒药物功效。对于组合研究,使用 SynergyFinder 程序分析协同分数。对接分析确定了可能有助于利巴韦林-奥司他韦组合针对 RSV 和 HPIV-2 的 抗病毒药物 活性的潜在分子相互作用。
主要结果
利巴韦林、法匹拉韦和奥司他韦对 HPIV-2 和 RSV 表现出良好的活性,并且利巴韦林 - 奥司他韦组合表现出很强的协同相互作用,表明有良好的治疗潜力。
解释与创新
利巴韦林-奥司他韦组合表现出明显的协同相互作用,表明对 HPIV-2 和 RSV 具有良好的治疗潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,利巴韦林-奥司他韦组合可能是治疗 HPIV-2 和 RSV 感染的有前途的治疗选择。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到实验体外性质的限制,可能无法完全反映体内条件。样本量很小,并且该研究不包括安慰剂或对照组。

In Vitro Evaluation of Repurposed Antivirals and Their Combinations Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Human Parainfluenza Virus Type 2: Evidence of Ribavirin-Oseltamivir Synergy.

Authors: Fatima Nur Olcay, Tuba Sevimoglu, Sibel Dosler
Original abstract (English)
Treating respiratory viral infections remains challenging, necessitating exploration of novel therapeutic approaches. Alternative strategies, such as drug repurposing or combinations, are essential for treating them. This study evaluated the in vitro activities of the antivirals ribavirin, favipiravir, and oseltamivir, and the mucolytic drugs N-acetylcysteine (NAC) and erdosteine, alone or in combination, against Human Parainfluenza Virus (HPIV) and Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV). HPIV-2 and RSV were cultured in VERO and HEp-2 cell lines, respectively. Antiviral efficacy was assessed by calculating IC 50 values using MTT and plaque reduction assays. For combination studies, synergy scores were analyzed using the SynergyFinder program. We investigated potential interactions underlying the synergistic activities using an in silico approach, that combined virtual screening and molecular docking. The IC 50 values of ribavirin, favipiravir, and oseltamivir against HPIV-2 and RSV ranged from 4.46 to 14.1 μg/mL, and the results from both methods were highly similar. All three antivirals' cytotoxicity was well above their C-max, and SIs were 12.43-36.69 for HPIV-2 and 15.88-38.12 for RSV. The ribavirin-oseltamivir combination synergy scores of 13.35 for HPIV-2 and 17.98 for RSV indicate clear synergism, while other combinations were additive. Docking analysis identified potential molecular interactions that may contribute to the antiviral activity of the ribavirin-oseltamivir combination against RSV and HPIV-2. Ribavirin, favipiravir, and oseltamivir showed good activity against HPIV-2 and RSV, and the ribavirin-oseltamivir combination exhibited strong synergistic interactions, suggesting promising therapeutic potential.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Treating respiratory viral infections remains challenging, necessitating exploration of novel therapeutic approaches.
Design and population
This study evaluated the in vitro activities of the antivirals ribavirin, favipiravir, and oseltamivir, and the mucolytic drugs N-acetylcysteine (NAC) and erdosteine, alone or in combination, against Human Parainfluenza Virus (HPIV) and Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV).
Core methods
Antiviral efficacy was assessed by calculating IC 50 values using MTT and plaque reduction assays. For combination studies, synergy scores were analyzed using the SynergyFinder program. Docking analysis identified potential molecular interactions that may contribute to the antiviral activity of the ribavirin-oseltamivir combination against RSV and HPIV-2.
Main results
Ribavirin, favipiravir, and oseltamivir showed good activity against HPIV-2 and RSV, and the ribavirin-oseltamivir combination exhibited strong synergistic interactions, indicating promising therapeutic potential.
Interpretation and novelty
The ribavirin-oseltamivir combination exhibited clear synergistic interactions, suggesting promising therapeutic potential against HPIV-2 and RSV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the ribavirin-oseltamivir combination may be a promising therapeutic option for treating HPIV-2 and RSV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the in vitro nature of the experiments, which may not fully reflect in vivo conditions. The sample size was small, and the study did not include a placebo or control group.

预防婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的建议:技术报告。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Technical Report.
作者： Committee on Infectious Diseases .
摘要中文翻译本技术报告附有美国儿科学会 (AAP) 政策声明 (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079047) 以及 2026-2027 RSV 季节期间 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫的建议。提出了建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行免疫接种的理由,除非婴儿有记录表明怀孕父母对 疫苗接种 具有保护作用;8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童,他们患有严重 RSV 疾病并进入第二个 RSV 季节。该报告综合了儿科人群中 RSV 流行病学、疾病负担、免疫有效性、安全性和成本效益的当前证据;包括支持在第二季管理中扩大高风险群体的证据。此外,该报告还概述了可用的 RSV 免疫产品、配方以及与其他免疫的联合给药。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本报告解决了针对婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病更新预防策略的需求,重点关注 2026-2027 RSV 季节期间免疫建议的基本原理。
设计与对象
该研究是一份技术报告,综合了儿科人群(包括患有严重 RSV 疾病的高风险人群)的 RSV 流行病学、疾病负担、免疫有效性、安全性和成本效益的当前证据。
核心方法
该报告回顾并综合了 RSV 免疫的现有证据,包括有效性、安全性和成本效益的数据,并概述了可用的 RSV 免疫产品和配方。
主要结果
该报告概述了建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行免疫接种的理由,除非婴儿已证明怀孕父母对 疫苗接种 具有保护作用,以及患有严重 RSV 疾病高风险并进入第二个 RSV 季节的 8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持扩大第二季 RSV 免疫接种的高危人群,这代表了解决弱势儿科人群疾病负担的一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 RSV 预防的公共卫生策略提供信息,特别是在识别高危人群和优化婴儿和儿童的免疫计划方面。
局限与证据强度
该报告基于现有证据的综合,这些证据在普遍性和最新数据方面可能存在局限性,但证据被认为是强有力的,并且对所提供的建议得到了充分支持。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Technical Report.

Authors: Committee on Infectious Diseases .
Original abstract (English)
This technical report accompanies the American Academy of Pediatrics (AAP) policy statement (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079047) and recommendations for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization during the 2026-2027 RSV season. The rationale is presented for recommending immunization of all infants younger than 8 months born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of the pregnant parent; and infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season. The report synthesizes current evidence on RSV epidemiology, disease burden, immunization effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness in pediatric populations; including evidence supporting expansion of high-risk groups for second season administration. Additionally, the report provides an overview of available RSV immunization products, formulations, and coadministration with other immunizations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This report addresses the need for updated prevention strategies against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease in infants and children, focusing on the rationale for immunization recommendations during the 2026-2027 RSV season.
Design and population
The study is a technical report that synthesizes current evidence on RSV epidemiology, disease burden, immunization effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness in pediatric populations, including those at high risk of severe RSV disease.
Core methods
The report reviews and synthesizes existing evidence on RSV immunization, including data on effectiveness, safety, and cost-effectiveness, and provides an overview of available RSV immunization products and formulations.
Main results
The report outlines the rationale for recommending immunization of all infants younger than 8 months born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of the pregnant parent, and infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as supporting the expansion of high-risk groups for second season RSV immunization, which represents a novel approach to addressing the disease burden in vulnerable pediatric populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for RSV prevention, particularly in identifying high-risk groups and optimizing immunization schedules for infants and children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The report is based on a synthesis of existing evidence, which may have limitations in terms of generalizability and up-to-date data, but the evidence is considered strong and well-supported for the recommendations provided.

呼吸道合胞病毒 融合糖蛋白 F 中保守相互作用热点的动态结构适应。

Dynamic structural adaptation of a conserved interaction hotspot in the respiratory syncytial virus fusion glycoprotein F.
作者： Rahul Kaushik, Suyong Re
摘要中文翻译蛋白质相互作用位点必须保持结构完整性,同时适应构象波动和序列扰动。将局部适应性残基与保守界面内结构受限位置区分开来的分子决定因素仍不完全清楚。在这里,我们使用预融合 呼吸道合胞病毒 融合糖蛋白 F 作为模型系统来研究这个问题,重点关注靠近 MEDI8897 结合界面的保守相互作用热点。使用结合了进化守恒、基于结构的能量分析、残基相互作用网络和分子动力学模拟的集成计算框架来表征依赖于 突变 的结构响应。对 1848 个 RSV F 序列的分析揭示了 CPINKC 热点(包括 C69、P205、I206、N208、K209 和 C212)的高度保守性。尽管保守,饱和诱变和结构分析揭示了计算的替代能量响应中明显的异质性。P205R 和 N208R 产生了非常不利的计算能量效应,而 I206R 表现出近似中性的能量响应。残基相互作用网络分析将 I206 确定为拓扑中心残基,在热点内具有最高的中介性和接近中心性。聚合 MD 采样包括七个系统中每个系统的三个独立的 500 ns 模拟,产生 10.5 μs 的总模拟时间。所有系统在模拟时间尺度上都保留了整体预融合架构,但表现出不同的局部和原体间响应。N208R 产生最强的局部扰动,包括增加热点灵活性和减少原体间接触占用。相比之下,P205R 尽管存在能量损失,但仍保持有限的局部动态,而 I206R 则保留了大致与野生型相似的动态特征。这些发现表明能量成本、残基相互作用拓扑和构象动力学为表征 突变 诱导的结构适应提供了补充信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解蛋白质相互作用位点如何保持结构完整性,同时允许构象变化和序列变化。该研究解决了关于区分保守病毒界面中适应性残基和结构受限残基的分子因素的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 融合糖蛋白 F,特别检查 MEDI8897 结合界面附近的保守相互作用热点。它分析了 1848 个 RSV F 序列,以确定保守模式和 突变 效应。
核心方法
研究人员采用了一个集成的计算框架,结合了进化守恒分析、基于结构的能量计算、残基相互作用网络评估和分子动力学模拟来评估依赖于 突变 的结构响应。
主要结果
RSV F 序列分析显示 CPINKC 热点具有很强的保守性,包括残基 C69、P205、I206、N208、K209 和 C212。饱和诱变和结构分析揭示了对取代的能量反应的显着变化,其中P205R和N208R显示出高度不利的影响,而I206R具有中性反应。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,能量成本、残基相互作用拓扑和构象动力学为 突变 如何影响病毒蛋白的结构适应提供了独特但互补的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解 RSV 蛋白的结构弹性,这可以为开发更有效的 抗病毒药物 策略提供信息,包括药物设计和 疫苗 靶向。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于计算模型和序列分析,可能无法完全捕捉体内动态。然而,大型数据集和多种模拟方法的使用为结论提供了适度的可信度。

Dynamic structural adaptation of a conserved interaction hotspot in the respiratory syncytial virus fusion glycoprotein F.

Authors: Rahul Kaushik, Suyong Re
Original abstract (English)
Protein interaction sites must maintain structural integrity while accommodating conformational fluctuations and sequence perturbations. Molecular determinants that distinguish locally adaptable residues from structurally constrained positions within conserved interfaces remain incompletely understood. Here, we investigate this problem using the prefusion respiratory syncytial virus fusion glycoprotein F as a model system, focusing on a conserved interaction hotspot proximal to the MEDI8897-binding interface. An integrated computational framework combining evolutionary conservation, structure-based energetic analysis, residue interaction networks, and molecular dynamics simulations was used to characterize mutation-dependent structural responses. Analysis of 1848 RSV F sequences revealed strong conservation of the CPINKC hotspot comprising C69, P205, I206, N208, K209, and C212. Despite conservation, saturation mutagenesis and structural analysis revealed pronounced heterogeneity in calculated energetic response to substitution. P205R and N208R produced highly unfavorable calculated energetic effects, whereas I206R exhibited an approximately neutral energetic response. Residue interaction network analysis identified I206 as topologically central residue with the highest betweenness and closeness centrality within the hotspot. Aggregate MD sampling comprised three independent 500-ns simulations for each of seven systems, yielding 10.5 μs of total simulation time. All systems retained the overall prefusion architecture over the simulated timescale but exhibited distinct local and interprotomer responses. N208R produced the strongest local perturbation, including increased hotspot flexibility and reduced interprotomer contact occupancy. In contrast, P205R maintained restrained local dynamics despite its energetic penalty, whereas I206R preserved a broadly wild-type-like dynamic profile. These findings indicate that energetic cost, residue interaction topology, and conformational dynamics provide complementary information for characterizing mutation-induced structural adaptation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how protein interaction sites maintain structural integrity while allowing conformational changes and sequence variations. The research addressed a gap in knowledge about the molecular factors that differentiate adaptable residues from structurally constrained ones in conserved viral interfaces.
Design and population
The study focused on the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) fusion glycoprotein F, specifically examining a conserved interaction hotspot near the MEDI8897-binding interface. It analyzed 1848 RSV F sequences to identify patterns of conservation and mutation effects.
Core methods
The researchers employed an integrated computational framework that combined evolutionary conservation analysis, structure-based energetic calculations, residue interaction network assessments, and molecular dynamics simulations to evaluate mutation-dependent structural responses.
Main results
Analysis of RSV F sequences showed strong conservation of the CPINKC hotspot, including residues C69, P205, I206, N208, K209, and C212. Saturation mutagenesis and structural analysis revealed significant variation in the energetic response to substitutions, with P205R and N208R showing highly unfavorable effects and I206R having a neutral response.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that energetic cost, residue interaction topology, and conformational dynamics provide distinct yet complementary insights into how mutations affect structural adaptation in viral proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to understanding the structural resilience of RSV proteins, which could inform the development of more effective antiviral strategies, including drug design and vaccine targeting.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on computational models and sequence analysis, which may not fully capture in vivo dynamics. However, the use of a large dataset and multiple simulation approaches provides moderate confidence in the conclusions.

在因肺炎住院的儿科中检测到呼吸道病原体:日本埼玉县的一项多中心研究。

Respiratory Pathogens Detected among Pediatric Hospitalizations for Pneumonia: A Multicenter Study from Saitama, Japan.
作者： Takuma Ohnishi, Naoki Kuremoto, Maika Tezuka, Shuhei Sako, Shinji Oana, Shigenao Mimura, Tetsuyoshi Taneichi, Takeshi Sato, Shigeru Takishima, Keiko Ichikawa
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估除了对肺炎住院儿童进行常规院内呼吸道病原体检测之外,通过多重聚合酶链反应(PCR)获得的其他结果。
设计与对象
2023年7月至2024年3月,在日本埼玉县的四家医院进行了一项全县范围的多中心回顾性队列研究研究,涉及1,170名因急性呼吸道感染(ARI)住院的儿童,其中457名并发肺炎。
核心方法
在 199 例住院治疗中进行了多重 PCR,经常在常规检测阴性后发现病原体,从而将可检测的病原体谱拓宽到院内针对性检测之外。
主要结果
在 123 例院内呼吸道病原体检测呈阴性的住院患者中,多重 PCR 在 85 例患者中检测到至少一种病原体(69.1%)。此外,在 210 例接受抗生素治疗且院内检测未呈阳性的住院患者中,74/110 例 (67.3%) 通过多重 PCR 检测到病毒病原体。
解释与创新
作者解释说,多重 PCR 显着增强了常规检测之外的病原体检测,突显了其提高小儿肺炎病例诊断准确性的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,多重 PCR 可以提供更全面的病原体检测,这可能为改进小儿肺炎的诊断策略和有针对性的 治疗 方法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,但由于样本量大且使用了经过验证的多重 PCR 方法,因此证据充足。

Respiratory Pathogens Detected among Pediatric Hospitalizations for Pneumonia: A Multicenter Study from Saitama, Japan.

Authors: Takuma Ohnishi, Naoki Kuremoto, Maika Tezuka, Shuhei Sako, Shinji Oana, Shigenao Mimura, Tetsuyoshi Taneichi, Takeshi Sato, Shigeru Takishima, Keiko Ichikawa
Original abstract (English)
Routine in-hospital testing may not detect the full range of respiratory pathogens in children hospitalized with pneumonia. We conducted a prefecture-wide multicenter retrospective cohort study at four hospitals in Saitama, Japan, from July 2023 to March 2024 to describe detected respiratory pathogens and assess the additional findings obtained by multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) beyond routine in-hospital testing. Among 1,170 pediatric hospitalizations for acute respiratory infections (ARIs), 457 (39.1%) were complicated by pneumonia. Human metapneumovirus (hMPV; n=124, 27.1%) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV; n=85, 18.6%) were the most frequently detected pathogens. Multiplex PCR was performed in 199 hospitalizations. Among 123 hospitalizations in which at least one in-hospital respiratory pathogen test was performed and all results were negative, multiplex PCR detected at least one pathogen in 85 (69.1%). In an exploratory unadjusted comparison excluding 16 group A Streptococcus-positive hospitalizations, antibiotic initiation was associated with older age, higher C-reactive protein levels, and absence of a positive in-hospital test. Among 210 antibiotic-treated hospitalizations without a positive in-hospital test, 110 underwent multiplex PCR and a viral pathogen was detected in 74/110 (67.3%). Multiplex PCR frequently identified pathogens after negative routine testing and broadened the detectable pathogen spectrum beyond targeted in-hospital tests.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the additional findings obtained by multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) beyond routine in-hospital testing for respiratory pathogens in children hospitalized with pneumonia.
Design and population
A prefecture-wide multicenter retrospective cohort study was conducted at four hospitals in Saitama, Japan, from July 2023 to March 2024, involving 1,170 pediatric hospitalizations for acute respiratory infections (ARIs), of which 457 were complicated by pneumonia.
Core methods
Multiplex PCR was performed in 199 hospitalizations, and it frequently identified pathogens after negative routine testing, broadening the detectable pathogen spectrum beyond targeted in-hospital tests.
Main results
Among 123 hospitalizations with negative in-hospital respiratory pathogen tests, multiplex PCR detected at least one pathogen in 85 (69.1%). Additionally, in 210 antibiotic-treated hospitalizations without a positive in-hospital test, 74/110 (67.3%) had a viral pathogen detected by multiplex PCR.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that multiplex PCR significantly enhances pathogen detection beyond routine testing, highlighting its potential to improve diagnostic accuracy in pediatric pneumonia cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that multiplex PCR can provide more comprehensive pathogen detection, which may inform improved diagnostic strategies and targeted treatment approaches for pediatric pneumonia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and use of a validated multiplex PCR method.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)——呼吸系统疾病预防的当务之急

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention
作者： Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。然而,监测 中的不平等、季节性驱动的时间安排以及负担能力和交付方面的挑战(特别是在低收入和中等收入国家)阻碍了公平的采用。这篇社论强调了重点人群,综合了当前的证据和政策方向,并概述了融资、采购和综合母婴保健服务方面的可行杠杆,以加速获得服务,使 RSV 预防成为紧迫且可实现的全球卫生优先事项。

问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿、老年人以及患有心肺合并症或免疫功能低下的个体下呼吸道疾病的主要原因。

该研究是一篇社论,综合了 RSV 预防的当前证据和政策方向,重点关注优先人群和融资、采购和综合母婴健康服务方面的可行杠杆。

该社论回顾了预防策略的最新进展,包括孕产妇 疫苗接种、长效单克隆抗体和针对老年人的 疫苗,并讨论了 监测、季节性驱动的时机以及低收入和中等收入国家的负担能力和交付方面的挑战。
主要结果
最近的进展使得分层预防成为可能,通过用于婴儿保护的母体 疫苗接种、用于新生儿和特定幼儿的长效单克隆抗体以及用于老年人的 疫苗。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)–An Urgent Priority in Respiratory Disease Prevention

Authors: Poonam Talwan, Darsh Gautam
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states. Recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults. However, inequities in surveillance, seasonality-driven timing, and challenges in affordability and delivery—particularly in low- and middle-income countries— impede equitable uptake. This editorial underscores priority populations, synthesizes current evidence and policy directions, and outlines actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery to accelerate access, making RSV prevention an urgent and achievable global health priority.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of lower respiratory tract disease in infants, young children, older adults, and individuals with cardiopulmonary comorbidities or immunocompromised states.
Design and population
The study is an editorial that synthesizes current evidence and policy directions for RSV prevention, focusing on priority populations and actionable levers in financing, procurement, and integrated maternal–child health delivery.
Core methods
The editorial reviews recent advances in prevention strategies, including maternal vaccination, long-acting monoclonal antibodies, and vaccines for older adults, and discusses challenges in surveillance, seasonality-driven timing, and affordability and delivery in low- and middle-income countries.
Main results
Recent advances enable layered prevention through maternal vaccination for infant protection, long-acting monoclonal antibodies for newborns and select toddlers, and vaccines for older adults.
Interpretation and novelty
The editorial emphasizes the urgency of addressing RSV as a global health priority and outlines strategies to accelerate access to prevention measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of improving surveillance, addressing seasonality, and enhancing affordability and delivery to ensure equitable access to RSV prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an editorial and does not include original data, thus the strength of the evidence is moderate.

基于抗原的快速检测试剂盒对儿童六种呼吸道病毒的诊断性能:与多重 PCR 检测组的比较以及与病毒载量的相关性。

Diagnostic performance of antigen-based rapid test kits for six respiratory viruses in children: comparison with a multiplex PCR panel and correlation with viral load.
作者： Daisuke Kitagawa, Antonio Di Lorenzo, Takumi Okamura, Soshi Hachisuka, Naohiro Yamamoto, Hiroki Nishikawa, Masayuki Onaka, Rika Suzuki, Soma Suzuki, Ayu Mukai
摘要中文翻译摘要 快速抗原检测广泛用于儿童呼吸道感染 (RTI) 的护理点 诊断,但病毒载量对敏感性的影响尚未全面表征多种儿科呼吸道病毒的特征。在儿科中心进行的这项前瞻性配对样本研究中,从患有 RTI 的儿童身上同时采集了两份鼻咽拭子。使用基于抗原的快速检测试剂盒(ImunoAce 系列,Tauns Laboratories,Inc.)对一根拭子进行了 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感、腺病毒、人类偏肺病毒 (hMPV) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 的检测;另一个使用 FilmArray 呼吸面板 (bioMérieux) 进行测试。任一方法呈阳性的样本均需进行验证性定量实时 PCR 以确定病毒载量。敏感性按病毒载量分层。总共分析了来自 1,232 名儿童(中位年龄:3.0 岁 [IQR:1.0–6.0];53.7% 男性)的 1,584 个配对样本。抗原测试的敏感性范围为 20.6%(腺病毒)至 79.7%(hMPV),而所有六个目标的特异性均超过 99%,总体一致性范围为 90.7% 至 99.0%。对于每种病毒,抗原检测阳性率随病毒载量急剧增加,病毒载量≥10 6 –10 7 拷贝/100 μL 时接近 90-100%,病毒载量≤10 4 –10 5 拷贝/100 μL 时下降到 50% 以下。针对常见儿科呼吸道病毒的基于抗原的快速检测显示出高特异性和病毒载量依赖性敏感性,可检测几乎所有具有高病毒载量的样本,无论目标病毒如何。这些发现支持抗原检测在识别病毒负荷最高的儿童(最有可能患有急性感染)方面的作用。重要性 呼吸道病毒快速抗原检测相对于基于 PCR 的检测的敏感性尚未完全确定。这项研究根据 1,584 份临床样本的多重 PCR 参考标准评估了六种常见儿科呼吸道病毒(SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感、腺病毒、人类偏肺病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒)的抗原测试,并将测试性能直接与定量病毒载量联系起来。我们表明,抗原测试敏感性强烈且始终依赖于病毒载量,在高病毒载量时接近 90-100%,并且对所有六个目标具有非常高的特异性。这些发现阐明了快速抗原检测的临床作用:它可靠地识别出病毒负荷最高的儿童,这些儿童最有可能患有急性症状感染,需要立即采取临床行动。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估基于抗原的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 快速检测的敏感性如何受到病毒载量的影响,因为儿童呼吸道病毒的这种关系尚未得到充分表征。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性配对样本研究是在儿科中心进行的,从 1,232 名患有呼吸道感染的儿童(中位年龄:3.0 岁 [IQR:1.0–6.0];53.7% 男性)同时采集了两份鼻咽拭子。
核心方法
使用基于抗原的快速检测试剂盒(ImunoAce 系列,Tauns Laboratories,Inc.)对一根拭子进行 RSV 和其他呼吸道病毒的检测,而另一根拭子则使用 FilmArray 呼吸面板(bioMérieux)进行检测。任一方法呈阳性的样本均需进行验证性定量实时 PCR 以确定病毒载量。
主要结果
RSV 抗原检测的敏感性范围为 20.6%(腺病毒)至 79.7%(hMPV),而所有六个目标的特异性均超过 99%。对于 RSV,抗原检测阳性率随病毒载量急剧增加,病毒载量≥10^6–10^7 拷贝/100 μL 时接近 90–100%,病毒载量≤10^4–10^5 拷贝/100 μL 时下降到 50% 以下。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 抗原测试的敏感性强烈且一致地依赖于病毒载量,并且对所有目标具有非常高的特异性。这凸显了抗原检测在识别病毒负荷最高的儿童方面的效用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果阐明了 RSV 快速抗原检测的临床作用,显示了其在检测高病毒载量病例方面的可靠性,这些病例最有可能代表需要立即采取临床行动的急性症状感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于来自单个儿科中心的配对标本,这可能会影响普遍性。然而,证据是强有力的,RSV 具有高特异性和明显的病毒载量依赖性敏感性。

Diagnostic performance of antigen-based rapid test kits for six respiratory viruses in children: comparison with a multiplex PCR panel and correlation with viral load.

Authors: Daisuke Kitagawa, Antonio Di Lorenzo, Takumi Okamura, Soshi Hachisuka, Naohiro Yamamoto, Hiroki Nishikawa, Masayuki Onaka, Rika Suzuki, Soma Suzuki, Ayu Mukai
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Rapid antigen tests are widely used for point-of-care diagnosis of respiratory tract infection (RTI) in children, but how the sensitivity is influenced by viral load has not been comprehensively characterized across multiple pediatric respiratory viruses. In this prospective, paired-specimen study conducted at the pediatric center, two simultaneous nasopharyngeal swabs were obtained from children with RTI. One swab was tested using antigen-based rapid test kits (ImunoAce series, Tauns Laboratories, Inc.) for SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, adenovirus, human metapneumovirus (hMPV), and respiratory syncytial virus; the other was tested using the FilmArray Respiratory Panel (bioMérieux). Specimens positive by either method underwent confirmatory quantitative real-time PCR to determine viral load. Sensitivity was stratified by viral load. A total of 1,584 paired specimens from 1,232 children (median age: 3.0 years [IQR: 1.0–6.0]; 53.7% male) were analyzed. Sensitivity of the antigen tests ranged from 20.6% (adenovirus) to 79.7% (hMPV), while specificity exceeded 99% for all six targets and overall agreement ranged from 90.7% to 99.0%. For every virus, antigen test positivity increased steeply with viral load, approaching 90–100% at viral loads ≥10 6 –10 7 copies/100 μL and falling below 50% at loads ≤10 4 –10 5 copies/100 μL. Antigen-based rapid tests for common pediatric respiratory viruses showed high specificity and viral-load-dependent sensitivity, detecting nearly all specimens with high viral load regardless of the target virus. These findings support a role for antigen testing in identifying children with the highest viral burden—who are most likely to have acute infection. IMPORTANCE The sensitivity of rapid antigen tests for respiratory viruses relative to PCR-based testing has been incompletely characterized. This study evaluated antigen tests for six common pediatric respiratory viruses—SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, adenovirus, human metapneumovirus, and respiratory syncytial virus—against a multiplex PCR reference standard across 1,584 clinical specimens and linked test performance directly to quantitative viral load. We show that antigen test sensitivity is strongly and consistently dependent on viral load, approaching 90–100% at high viral loads with very high specificity across all six targets. These findings clarify the clinical role of rapid antigen testing: it reliably identifies children with the highest viral burden, who are most likely to have acute, symptomatic infection warranting immediate clinical action.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate how the sensitivity of antigen-based rapid tests for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is influenced by viral load, as this relationship has not been fully characterized for pediatric respiratory viruses.
Design and population
This prospective, paired-specimen study was conducted at a pediatric center, where two simultaneous nasopharyngeal swabs were collected from 1,232 children (median age: 3.0 years [IQR: 1.0–6.0]; 53.7% male) with respiratory tract infections.
Core methods
One swab was tested using antigen-based rapid test kits (ImunoAce series, Tauns Laboratories, Inc.) for RSV and other respiratory viruses, while the other was tested using the FilmArray Respiratory Panel (bioMérieux). Specimens positive by either method underwent confirmatory quantitative real-time PCR to determine viral load.
Main results
Sensitivity of the antigen tests for RSV ranged from 20.6% (adenovirus) to 79.7% (hMPV), while specificity exceeded 99% for all six targets. For RSV, antigen test positivity increased steeply with viral load, approaching 90–100% at viral loads ≥10^6–10^7 copies/100 μL and falling below 50% at loads ≤10^4–10^5 copies/100 μL.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that antigen test sensitivity for RSV is strongly and consistently dependent on viral load, with very high specificity across all targets. This highlights the utility of antigen testing in identifying children with the highest viral burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results clarify the clinical role of rapid antigen testing for RSV, showing its reliability in detecting high viral load cases, which are most likely to represent acute, symptomatic infection requiring immediate clinical action.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to paired specimens from a single pediatric center, which may affect generalizability. However, the evidence is strong, with high specificity and clear viral-load-dependent sensitivity observed for RSV.

呼吸道合胞病毒 COVID-19 大流行期间儿童感染:来自三级护理儿科的调查结果 队列研究

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Children During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Findings from a Tertiary-Care Pediatric Cohort
作者： Halil Uğur Hatipoğlu, Gülşen Akkoç, Canan Yolcu, Fidel Ceren Yavuzyilmaz, Cevher Kızılırmak, Ahsen Güler, Derya Büyükkayhan, Fidel Ceren Yavuzyılmaz
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是儿童下呼吸道感染的主要原因。本研究旨在描述三级护理 队列研究 中 28 天以上 RSV 阳性儿童的临床和实验室特征,探索与住院治疗相关的组间比较特征,并确定与住院儿童入院儿科重症监护病房 (PICU) 独立相关的因素。
设计与对象
这项回顾性单中心研究筛选了 2020 年 8 月至 2022 年 8 月期间的 RSV 检测记录,纳入了 2021 年 2 月至 2022 年 8 月期间经实验室确诊的 RSV 感染的 28 天以上儿童。在研究期间有可用 RSV 检测结果的 3523 名儿童中,包括 561 名年龄 >28 天的 RSV 阳性儿童。
核心方法
记录人口统计、临床、实验室、治疗、住院、PICU 入院、呼吸支持和结果数据。根据住院状况、临床 诊断 和 PICU 入院情况进行比较。预先指定的简约多变量逻辑回归模型评估了住院儿童入住 PICU 的相关因素。
主要结果
478 名患者(85.2%)需要住院治疗,其中 35 名患者(6.2%)需要入住 PICU。肺炎与缺氧、无创通气支持、PICU 入住、住院时间较长和临床改善延迟的发生率较高有关。在 30 名患者 (5.3%) 中检测到有记录的病毒双重感染;在此数据集中,未发现记录的病毒合并感染与住院或 PICU 入住之间存在关联。
解释与创新
在该三级护理 队列研究 中,肺炎和缺氧与 PICU 入住独立相关。该研究重点介绍了 RSV 感染儿童的临床特征和入住 PICU 的危险因素,为了解 RSV 疾病的严重程度提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 和儿科人群 RSV 风险评估策略提供信息,特别是在识别可能需要重症监护的高风险患者方面。他们还支持制定有针对性的干预措施来管理严重 RSV 感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和单中心设置而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。该证据是中等的,因为它基于三级护理 队列研究,并且不包括前瞻性数据或外部验证。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Children During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Findings from a Tertiary-Care Pediatric Cohort

Authors: Halil Uğur Hatipoğlu, Gülşen Akkoç, Canan Yolcu, Fidel Ceren Yavuzyilmaz, Cevher Kızılırmak, Ahsen Güler, Derya Büyükkayhan, Fidel Ceren Yavuzyılmaz
Original abstract (English)
Objective: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infection in children. This study aimed to describe the clinical and laboratory characteristics of RSV-positive children older than 28 days in a tertiary-care cohort, explore characteristics associated with hospitalization in group comparisons, and identify factors independently associated with pediatric intensive care unit (PICU) admission among hospitalized children.Materials and Methods: This retrospective single-center study screened RSV testing records between August 2020 and August 2022 and included children older than 28 days with laboratory-confirmed RSV infection identified between February 2021 and August 2022. Demographic, clinical, laboratory, treatment, hospitalization, PICU admission, respiratory support, and outcome data were recorded. Comparisons were performed according to hospitalization status, clinical diagnosis, and PICU admission. A prespecified parsimonious multivariable logistic regression model evaluated factors associated with PICU admission among hospitalized children.Results: Of the 3523 children with available RSV test results during the study period, 561 RSV-positive children aged >28 days were included. Median age was 4 months (IQR, 2-14), and 317 patients (56.5%) were male. Bronchiolitis was the most common diagnosis (63.5%), followed by pneumonia (28.5%) and upper respiratory tract infection (8.0%). Hospitalization was required in 478 patients (85.2%), and 35 (6.2%) required PICU admission. Pneumonia was associated with higher rates of hypoxia, noninvasive ventilatory support, PICU admission, longer hospitalization, and delayed clinical improvement. Documented viral coinfection was detected in 30 patients (5.3%); no association was detected between documented viral coinfections and hospitalizations or PICU admission in this dataset. In hospitalized patients, pneumonia (OR, 2.59; 95% CI, 1.07-6.24; p=0.034) and hypoxia (OR, 32.08; 95% CI, 13.09-78.66; p<0.001) were independently associated with PICU admission.Conclusion: RSV infection predominantly affected infants and most commonly presented as bronchiolitis. Pneumonia and hypoxia were independently associated with PICU admission in this tertiary-care cohort.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of lower respiratory tract infection in children. This study aimed to describe the clinical and laboratory characteristics of RSV-positive children older than 28 days in a tertiary-care cohort, explore characteristics associated with hospitalization in group comparisons, and identify factors independently associated with pediatric intensive care unit (PICU) admission among hospitalized children.
Design and population
This retrospective single-center study screened RSV testing records between August 2020 and August 2022 and included children older than 28 days with laboratory-confirmed RSV infection identified between February 2021 and August 2022. Of the 3523 children with available RSV test results during the study period, 561 RSV-positive children aged >28 days were included.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, laboratory, treatment, hospitalization, PICU admission, respiratory support, and outcome data were recorded. Comparisons were performed according to hospitalization status, clinical diagnosis, and PICU admission. A prespecified parsimonious multivariable logistic regression model evaluated factors associated with PICU admission among hospitalized children.
Main results
Hospitalization was required in 478 patients (85.2%), and 35 (6.2%) required PICU admission. Pneumonia was associated with higher rates of hypoxia, noninvasive ventilatory support, PICU admission, longer hospitalization, and delayed clinical improvement. Documented viral coinfection was detected in 30 patients (5.3%); no association was detected between documented viral coinfections and hospitalizations or PICU admission in this dataset.
Interpretation and novelty
Pneumonia and hypoxia were independently associated with PICU admission in this tertiary-care cohort. The study highlights the clinical features and risk factors for PICU admission in RSV-infected children, providing insights into the severity of RSV disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for RSV in pediatric populations, particularly in identifying high-risk patients who may require intensive care. They also support the development of targeted interventions for managing severe RSV infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a tertiary-care cohort and does not include prospective data or external validation.
