Bardoxolonemethyl 通过 IKKβ/NF-κB 信号通路下调 IL-6 对 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染产生抑制作用

Bardoxolone methyl exerts an inhibitory effect on respiratory syncytial virus infection by downregulating IL-6 via the IKKβ/NF-κB signaling pathway
作者： Xunping Wu, Hongping Wang, Ling Zhang, Bingyao Wang, Xinran Tan, Zhu Li, Jin Nie, Daishun Liu
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)会导致婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下人群的严重下呼吸道感染,但针对活动性感染的治疗选择仍然有限。白细胞介素-6(IL-6)促进RSV复制和RSV引发的炎症,而IKKβ/NF-κB通路是调控IL-6表达的主要途径。甲基巴多昔芬(BXM)通过网络医学被鉴定为一种潜在的抗RSV化合物,分子对接研究表明其与IKKβ/NF-κB通路存在相互作用。我们研究了BXM是否通过调节RSV感染的BEAS-2B细胞和BALB/c小鼠中的IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6轴来抑制RSV感染。RSV感染会增加IL-6表达并激活IKKβ/NF-κB信号通路,而IL-6敲低则会减少病毒复制和炎症介质的表达。BXM在体外显著抑制了RSV复制,如RT-qPCR、TCID50、斑块减少实验和Western blotting所示,并降低了IL-6的表达。从机制上看,BXM减弱了RSV诱导的IKKβ和NF-κB的磷酸化。IKKβ抑制剂TPCA-1产生了类似的抑制效果,而重组人IL-6添加则部分恢复了BXM处理的RSV感染细胞中炎症介质的表达。在小鼠中,BXM降低了肺部病毒滴度、RSV-F表达、IL-6表达、IKKβ/NF-κB激活以及RSV诱导的肺损伤。这些发现表明,BXM通过调节IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6轴抑制RSV复制和炎症,支持其作为RSV感染治疗候选药物的潜力。重要性 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是导致呼吸道疾病的主要原因之一,尤其是在婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下人群中。尽管某些高危群体已有预防策略,但针对活动性RSV感染的治疗选择仍然有限。本研究确定了甲基巴多昔芬(BXM)作为潜在的抗RSV化合物,并表明其在细胞和小鼠模型中可以减少病毒复制和病毒诱导的炎症。重要的是,我们的发现表明,BXM至少部分通过减少与IL-6相关的炎症信号发挥作用,而这些信号与RSV疾病的严重程度密切相关。这些结果为BXM作为对抗RSV感染的潜在候选药物提供了实验依据,并为针对宿主的治疗策略提供了见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨甲基巴多索龙 (BXM) 是否通过调节 IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6 轴抑制 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染,从而解决活动性 RSV 感染的有限治疗选择。
设计与对象
该研究使用 BEAS-2B 细胞在体外进行,并使用 BALB/c 小鼠进行体内研究,重点关注 RSV 感染的细胞和动物。
核心方法
在 BEAS-2B 细胞和 BALB/c 小鼠中测试了 BXM 对 RSV 复制和炎症的影响,分子对接表明与 IKKβ/NF-κB 通路存在相互作用。
主要结果
BXM在体外显著抑制了RSV复制并降低了IL-6的表达,在小鼠中则减少了肺部病毒滴度、RSV-F表达、IL-6表达、IKKβ/NF-κB激活以及RSV诱导的肺损伤。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,BXM通过调节IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6轴抑制RSV复制和炎症,支持其作为RSV感染治疗候选药物的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为BXM作为对抗RSV感染的潜在候选药物提供了实验依据,并为针对宿主的治疗策略提供了见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用体外和体内模型,可能无法完全代表人类 RSV 感染的复杂性。由于使用了动物模型并且需要进一步的临床验证,证据强度中等。

Bardoxolone methyl exerts an inhibitory effect on respiratory syncytial virus infection by downregulating IL-6 via the IKKβ/NF-κB signaling pathway

Authors: Xunping Wu, Hongping Wang, Ling Zhang, Bingyao Wang, Xinran Tan, Zhu Li, Jin Nie, Daishun Liu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes severe lower respiratory tract infections in infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals, but therapeutic options for active infection remain limited. Interleukin-6 (IL-6) contributes to RSV replication and RSV-induced inflammation, and the IKKβ/NF-κB pathway is a major regulator of IL-6 expression. Bardoxolone methyl (BXM) was identified by network medicine as a potential anti-RSV compound, with molecular docking suggesting interactions with the IKKβ/NF-κB pathway. We investigated whether BXM inhibits RSV infection through modulation of the IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6 axis in RSV-infected BEAS-2B cells and BALB/c mice. RSV infection increased IL-6 expression and activated IKKβ/NF-κB signaling, whereas IL-6 knockdown reduced viral replication and inflammatory mediator expression. BXM significantly suppressed RSV replication, as shown by RT-qPCR, TCID50, plaque reduction assays, and Western blotting, and decreased IL-6 expression in vitro . Mechanistically, BXM attenuated RSV-induced phosphorylation of IKKβ and NF-κB. The IKKβ inhibitor TPCA-1 produced similar inhibitory effects, whereas recombinant human IL-6 add-back partially restored inflammatory mediator expression in BXM-treated RSV-infected cells. In mice, BXM reduced lung viral titers, RSV-F expression, IL-6 expression, IKKβ/NF-κB activation, and RSV-induced lung injury. These findings suggest that BXM suppresses RSV replication and inflammation, at least in part, by modulating the IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6 axis, supporting its potential as a therapeutic candidate for RSV infection. IMPORTANCE Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of respiratory illness, especially in infants, older adults, and people with weakened immune systems. Although preventive strategies are available for some high-risk groups, treatment options for active RSV infection remain limited. This study identifies bardoxolone methyl (BXM) as a potential anti-RSV compound and shows that it can reduce both viral replication and virus-induced inflammation in cell and mouse models. Importantly, our findings suggest that BXM acts, at least in part, by reducing IL-6-related inflammatory signaling, which is closely associated with RSV disease severity. These results provide experimental evidence supporting BXM as a promising candidate for further development against RSV infection and offer insight into a potential host-targeted therapeutic strategy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether bardoxolone methyl (BXM) inhibits respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection through modulation of the IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6 axis, addressing the limited therapeutic options for active RSV infection.
Design and population
The study was conducted in vitro using BEAS-2B cells and in vivo using BALB/c mice, focusing on RSV-infected cells and animals.
Core methods
BXM was tested for its effect on RSV replication and inflammation in BEAS-2B cells and BALB/c mice, with molecular docking suggesting interactions with the IKKβ/NF-κB pathway.
Main results
BXM significantly suppressed RSV replication and decreased IL-6 expression in vitro and reduced lung viral titers, RSV-F expression, IL-6 expression, IKKβ/NF-κB activation, and RSV-induced lung injury in mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that BXM suppresses RSV replication and inflammation by modulating the IKKβ/NF-κB/IL-6 axis, supporting its potential as a therapeutic candidate for RSV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide experimental evidence supporting BXM as a promising candidate for further development against RSV infection and offer insight into a potential host-targeted therapeutic strategy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent the complexity of human RSV infection. The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of animal models and the need for further clinical validation.

2015-2020 年高危成人 队列研究 中 呼吸道合胞病毒 的合并感染:一项回顾性观察研究

Coinfections With Respiratory Syncytial Virus in a Cohort of Adults With High‐Risk Conditions, 2015–2020: A Retrospective Observational Study
作者： Oluwakemi D. Alonge, Maria E. Sundaram, Huong Q. Nguyen, Jennifer P. King, Elisha Stefanski, Andrew G. Allmon, Jean‐Yves Pirçon, Pouya Saeedi
摘要中文翻译摘要 合并感染可能会影响呼吸道疾病的临床结果。有关高危成人 RSV 合并感染的数据有限。使用多重检测组对患有 ≥ 1 种高危病症的社区居住成年人(≥ 18 岁)的残留呼吸道标本进行了多种病毒病原体的重新检测。我们比较了 RSV 合并感染者与 RSV 单一感染者之间的人口统计和临床特征。在患有 ≥ 1 种高危病症的成人中,RSV 合并感染占所有 RSV 感染的 5.9%。根据特定高危状况或临床疾病表现,RSV 合并感染者和单一感染者之间没有观察到统计学上的显着差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 RSV 合并感染对高危成人临床结果的影响。背景强调,合并感染可能会影响呼吸道疾病的临床结果。
设计与对象
使用患有 ≥ 1 种高危病症的社区居住成年人(≥ 18 岁)的残留呼吸道标本进行了一项回顾性观察研究。该研究的时间跨度为2015年至2020年。
核心方法
使用多重检测板重新测试残留的呼吸道样本,以检测多种病毒病原体。该研究比较了 RSV 合并感染者和 RSV 单一感染者之间的人口统计和临床特征。
主要结果
在患有 ≥ 1 种高危病症的成人中,RSV 合并感染占所有 RSV 感染的 5.9%。在特定高风险状况或临床疾病表现方面,RSV 合并感染者和单一感染者之间没有观察到统计学上的显着差异。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 合并感染在患有高危疾病的成年人中相对常见,但与单一感染相比,似乎不会显着影响临床结果。本研究对该人群中 RSV 合并感染进行了描述性分析。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解高危成人 RSV 合并感染的 流行病学,这可能为未来的 监测 和呼吸道疾病的诊断策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对残留样本的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入选择偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Coinfections With Respiratory Syncytial Virus in a Cohort of Adults With High‐Risk Conditions, 2015–2020: A Retrospective Observational Study

Authors: Oluwakemi D. Alonge, Maria E. Sundaram, Huong Q. Nguyen, Jennifer P. King, Elisha Stefanski, Andrew G. Allmon, Jean‐Yves Pirçon, Pouya Saeedi
Original abstract (English)
Summary Coinfections may influence the clinical outcome of respiratory illness. Data are limited regarding RSV coinfections in adults with high‐risk conditions. Residual respiratory specimens from community‐dwelling adults (≥ 18 years) with ≥ 1 high‐risk condition were retested using a multiplex panel for multiple viral pathogens. We compared demographic and clinical characteristics between those with RSV coinfection versus RSV single infection. RSV coinfections comprised 5.9% of all RSV infections among adults with ≥ 1 high‐risk condition. No statistically significant differences were observed between those with RSV coinfections and single infections according to presence of specific high‐risk conditions or clinical disease presentation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the impact of RSV coinfections on clinical outcomes in adults with high-risk conditions. The background highlights that coinfections may influence the clinical outcome of respiratory illness.
Design and population
A retrospective observational study was conducted using residual respiratory specimens from community-dwelling adults (≥ 18 years) with ≥ 1 high-risk condition. The study spanned from 2015 to 2020.
Core methods
Residual respiratory specimens were retested using a multiplex panel to detect multiple viral pathogens. The study compared demographic and clinical characteristics between individuals with RSV coinfection and those with RSV single infection.
Main results
RSV coinfections accounted for 5.9% of all RSV infections among adults with ≥ 1 high-risk condition. No statistically significant differences were observed between those with RSV coinfections and single infections regarding specific high-risk conditions or clinical disease presentation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that RSV coinfections are relatively common in adults with high-risk conditions but do not appear to significantly affect clinical outcomes compared to single infections. This study provides a descriptive analysis of RSV coinfections in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to understanding the epidemiology of RSV coinfections in adults with high-risk conditions, which may inform future surveillance and diagnostic strategies for respiratory illnesses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on residual specimens, which may introduce selection bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

Đặc điểm tiến hóa phân tử của vi rút hợp bào hô hấp trên bệnh nhân nhi viêm đường hô hấp cấp tính nặng (SARI) tại miền Bắc 越南,2017-2020 越南北部严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 儿科患者中人 呼吸道合胞病毒 的分子 进化,2017-2020

Đặc điểm tiến hóa phân tử của vi rút hợp bào hô hấp trên bệnh nhân nhi viêm đường hô hấp cấp tính nặng (SARI) tại miền Bắc Việt Nam, 2017-2020 Molecular Evolution of Human Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Pediatric Patients with Severe Acute Respiratory Infection (SARI) in Northern Viet Nam, 2017-2020
作者： Ứng Thị Hồng Trang, Vũ Mai Phương Hoàng, Vũ Sơn Nguyễn, Lê Khánh Hằng Nguyễn, Thị Quỳnh Mai Lê, Đức Cường Vương, Thị Thu Hương Trần, Phương Anh Nguyễn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定2017年至2020年越南北部严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)儿科患者中人类呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的分子进化特征,解决低收入和中等收入国家对RSV分子进化的有限研究。
设计与对象
该研究对 2017 年至 2020 年越南北部两家医院的 SARI 儿科患者的 RSV 阳性样本进行,重点关注 56 个 RSV-A 和 50 个 RSV-B 样本。
核心方法
该研究包括根据Ct值、分组、年龄、性别、采样时间和地点选择RSV阳性样本,然后使用下一代测序技术对整个G基因进行测序。
主要结果
结果显示,所有RSV-A株均属于A.D进化枝,有53个ON1和3个非ON1基因型;所有RSV-B株均属于B.D.4进化枝,有BA9基因型。RSV-A 和 RSV-B 的 G 基因的分子 进化 率估计分别为每年每个位点 1.32 × 10-3 和 8.29 × 10-4 替换。
解释与创新
该研究提供了2017年至2020年越南北部RSV-A和RSV-B的分子进化特征,有助于该地区RSV循环的监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 监测 和越南北部 RSV 循环监测提供信息,有助于预防和控制儿科患者的 RSV 感染。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括根据特定标准选择样本以及潜在的抽样偏差,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Đặc điểm tiến hóa phân tử của vi rút hợp bào hô hấp trên bệnh nhân nhi viêm đường hô hấp cấp tính nặng (SARI) tại miền Bắc Việt Nam, 2017-2020 Molecular Evolution of Human Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Pediatric Patients with Severe Acute Respiratory Infection (SARI) in Northern Viet Nam, 2017-2020

Authors: Ứng Thị Hồng Trang, Vũ Mai Phương Hoàng, Vũ Sơn Nguyễn, Lê Khánh Hằng Nguyễn, Thị Quỳnh Mai Lê, Đức Cường Vương, Thị Thu Hương Trần, Phương Anh Nguyễn
Original abstract (English)
Vi rút hợp bào hô hấp ở người (Respiratory Syncytial Virus – RSV) là một trong những nguyên nhân hàng đầu gây nhiễm trùng đường hô hấp cấp tính ở trẻ nhỏ. Các nghiên cứu về đặc điểm sinh học phân tử và tiến hoá của RSV vẫn còn hạn chế tại những quốc gia có thu nhập thấp và trung bình. Nghiên cứu này được thực hiện nhằm xác định đặc điểm tiến hóa phân tử của RSV trên bệnh nhân nhi nhiễm trùng hô hấp cấp tính nặng tại miền Bắc Việt Nam giai đoạn 2017–2020. Từ các mẫu viêm đường hô hấp cấp tính nặng được xác định dương tính với RSV tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương và Bệnh viện đa khoa tỉnh Quảng Ninh trong 4 năm (2017-2020) tiến hành lựa chọn 56 mẫu dương tính RSV-A và 50 mẫu dương tính RSV-B để giải trình tự toàn bộ gen G bằng kỹ thuật giải trình tự gen thế hệ mới. Các mẫu này được lựa chọn theo giá trị Ct, phân nhóm, tuổi, giới tính, thời gian lấy mẫu và địa điểm. Từ kết quả giải trình tự, các phân tích phát sinh chủng loại và tiến hóa được thực hiện để phân tích đặc điểm tiến hoá phân tử. Kết quả cho thấy các chủng RSV-A đều thuộc clade A.D, kiểu gen ON1 (53 chủng) và non-ON1 (3 chủng), 50 chủng RSV-B thuộc clade B.D.4, kiểu gen BA9. Tất cả các chủng RSV-A của Việt Nam trong nghiên cứ này đều không có đoạn lặp 72 nucleotide ở vùng siêu biến đổi thứ hai trên gen G. Tốc độ tiến hóa phân tử ước tính của gen G RSV-A và RSV-B lần lượt là 1,32 × 10-3 và 8,29 × 10-4 thay thế/ vị trí/năm. Các vị trí N-glycosyl hóa và các vị trí axit amin chịu áp lực chọn lọc ở cả hai phân nhóm vi rút cũng được xác định. Nghiên cứu đã đưa ra được tốc độ tiến hóa và các đặc điểm tiến hoá phân tử của cả RSV-A và RSV-B tại miền Bắc Việt Nam giai đoạn 2017-2020, góp phần cung cấp các dữ liệu cho việc thực hiện giám sát sự lưu hành của vi rút này trong thời gian tới tại Việt Nam.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the molecular evolutionary characteristics of human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in pediatric patients with severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) in northern Vietnam from 2017 to 2020, addressing the limited research on RSV molecular evolution in low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population
The study was conducted on RSV-positive samples from pediatric patients with SARI at two hospitals in northern Vietnam from 2017 to 2020, focusing on 56 RSV-A and 50 RSV-B samples.
Core methods
The study involved selecting RSV-positive samples based on Ct values, grouping, age, gender, sampling time, and location, then sequencing the entire G gene using next-generation sequencing technology.
Main results
The results showed that all RSV-A strains belonged to clade A.D, with 53 ON1 and 3 non-ON1 genotypes, while all RSV-B strains belonged to clade B.D.4, with the BA9 genotype. The molecular evolution rates of the G gene for RSV-A and RSV-B were estimated to be 1.32 × 10-3 and 8.29 × 10-4 substitutions per site per year, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provided the molecular evolutionary characteristics of both RSV-A and RSV-B in northern Vietnam from 2017 to 2020, contributing to the surveillance of RSV circulation in the region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the surveillance and monitoring of RSV circulation in northern Vietnam, aiding in the prevention and control of RSV infections in pediatric patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the selection of samples based on specific criteria and the potential for sampling bias, which may affect the generalizability of the results.

2016 年至 2024 年间 OpenSAFELY 中 呼吸道合胞病毒、流感和 COVID-19 的临床编码的分类和区分

Classifying and Differentiating Clinical Coding of Respiratory Syncytial Virus, Influenza, and COVID-19 in OpenSAFELY Between 2016 and 2024
作者： Prestige, E., Warren-Gash, C., Quint, J. K., Evans, D., Costello, R. E., Mehrkar, A., Bacon, S., Goldacre, B., Barley-McMullen, S., Yameen, F.
摘要中文翻译电子健康记录 (EHR) 是丰富的数据源,可用于使用可计算表型来分析健康结果。经英国 NHS 批准,我们使用 OpenSAFELY 安全分析平台来设计和评估表型,以对 2016 年 9 月至 2024 年 8 月期间英文编码的健康数据中的三种关键呼吸道病毒 - 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 COVID-19 进行分类。我们将狭义和广义表型相互比较,并与公开的 监测 数据进行比较。两种表型的病例均显示出与 监测 数据相似的季节性模式。对于轻度病例,广泛的表型比狭窄的表型导致错误分类的风险增加。对于严重病例,无论使用何种表型,婴儿错误分类的风险均高于老年人。这里提出的表型提供了一种在缺乏检测信息的情况下从编码健康记录中对呼吸道病毒进行分类的潜在途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在对健康记录中的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 COVID-19 的临床编码进行分类和区分,以改进 监测 和 诊断。
设计与对象
该研究使用英国 NHS 批准的 OpenSAFELY 安全分析平台来评估 2016 年 9 月至 2024 年 8 月期间英文编码健康数据中 RSV、流感和 COVID-19 的表型。
核心方法
研究人员将 RSV、流感和 COVID-19 的狭义和广义表型与公开的 监测 数据进行比较,以评估分类准确性。
主要结果
狭义和广义表型的病例均显示出与 监测 数据相似的季节性模式。对于轻度病例,广泛的表型比狭窄的表型导致错误分类的风险增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,广泛的表型可能会给轻度病例带来错误分类的风险,而无论表型如何,严重病例在婴儿中都表现出较高的错误分类风险。
科研与公卫意义
所提出的表型提供了一种在检测信息不可用时从编码健康记录中对呼吸道病毒进行分类的潜在方法,这可以增强 监测 和公共卫生反应。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于编码的健康记录,不包括测试数据,这可能会影响分类的准确性。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于现有数据,而没有直接的生物学验证。

Classifying and Differentiating Clinical Coding of Respiratory Syncytial Virus, Influenza, and COVID-19 in OpenSAFELY Between 2016 and 2024

Authors: Prestige, E., Warren-Gash, C., Quint, J. K., Evans, D., Costello, R. E., Mehrkar, A., Bacon, S., Goldacre, B., Barley-McMullen, S., Yameen, F.
Original abstract (English)
Electronic health records (EHRs) are a rich source of data which can be used to analyse health outcomes using computable phenotypes. With the approval of NHS England we used the OpenSAFELY secure analytics platform to design and assess phenotypes to classify three key respiratory viruses - respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza, and COVID-19 - in English coded health data between September 2016 and August 2024. We compared narrow and broad phenotypes to one another and to publicly available surveillance data. Cases from both phenotypes showed similar seasonal patterns to surveillance data. Broad phenotypes led to increased risk of misclassification than narrow phenotypes for mild cases. For severe cases the risk of misclassification was higher in infants than for older adults, irrespective of the phenotype used. The phenotypes presented here offer a potential route to classify respiratory viruses from coded health records in the absence of testing information.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to classify and differentiate clinical coding of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza, and COVID-19 in health records to improve surveillance and diagnosis.
Design and population
The study used the OpenSAFELY secure analytics platform with NHS England approval to assess phenotypes for RSV, influenza, and COVID-19 in English coded health data between September 2016 and August 2024.
Core methods
The researchers compared narrow and broad phenotypes for RSV, influenza, and COVID-19 against publicly available surveillance data to evaluate classification accuracy.
Main results
Cases from both narrow and broad phenotypes showed similar seasonal patterns to surveillance data. Broad phenotypes led to increased risk of misclassification than narrow phenotypes for mild cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that broad phenotypes may introduce misclassification risks for mild cases, while severe cases showed higher misclassification risks in infants regardless of phenotype.
Scientific and public-health significance
The phenotypes presented offer a potential method to classify respiratory viruses from coded health records when testing information is unavailable, which could enhance surveillance and public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to coded health records and does not include testing data, which may affect the accuracy of classification. The evidence is moderate as it relies on existing data without direct biological verification.

住院儿童 RSV 疾病:第二年与第一年多中心研究。

RSV Disease Among Hospitalized Children: Second vs. First Year of Life Multicenter Study.
作者： Keren Armoni Domany, Inbal Golan-Tripto, Nitzan Burrack, Kamal Masarweh, Michal Gur, Mika Rochman, Moria Be'er, Dario Prais, Meirav Mor, Moran Weinberger Opek
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是儿科住院的主要原因,但有关二岁时因 RSV 住院的儿童的数据有限。
设计与对象
这项回顾性多中心研究纳入了 2017 年至 2021 年间 11 个医疗中心因 RSV 感染住院的 0-23 个月儿童。
核心方法
我们比较了出生后第二年和第一年的人口统计数据、结局(住院时间 [LOS] > 3 天和儿科重症监护病房 [PICU] 入院)以及全身皮质类固醇和抗生素的使用情况,并根据早产、合并症和 COVID-19 大流行进行了调整。
主要结果
在 6260 名因实验室确诊 RSV 感染而住院的儿童中,有 1321 名 (21%) 是在出生后第二年住院的。与第一年入院相比,第二年患者的早产率(18.0% vs. 13.4%)和合并症发生率(6.3% vs. 2.6%)更高(均 p < 0.001)。他们更频繁地接受皮质类固醇(53.5% vs. 23.2%)和抗生素(63.4% vs. 45.9%)(均 p < 0.001),并且 PICU 入住率较低(4.6% vs. 7.0%;p < 0.001),且长期 LOS 率相似(35.0% vs.
解释与创新
在调整分析中,第二年住院与糖皮质激素使用增加(调整后比值比[aOR]为4.28;95%置信区间[CI]为3.67-4.99)和抗生素使用增加(aOR为2.09;95% CI为1.80-2.43)相关,且与较低的PICU入院可能性(aOR为0.56;95% CI为0.40-0.79)相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要更好地表征该年龄组,以优化未来的免疫预防策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

RSV Disease Among Hospitalized Children: Second vs. First Year of Life Multicenter Study.

Authors: Keren Armoni Domany, Inbal Golan-Tripto, Nitzan Burrack, Kamal Masarweh, Michal Gur, Mika Rochman, Moria Be'er, Dario Prais, Meirav Mor, Moran Weinberger Opek
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of pediatric hospitalization, yet data on children hospitalized with RSV in their second year of life are limited. OBJECTIVE: To compare clinical characteristics and management practices of children hospitalized with RSV during their second versus first year of life. METHODS: This retrospective multicenter study included children aged 0-23 months hospitalized with RSV infection across 11 medical centers between 2017 and 2021. We compared demographics, outcomes (length of stay [LOS] > 3 days, and pediatric intensive care unit [PICU] admission), and systemic corticosteroid and antibiotic use between the second and first years of life, adjusting for prematurity, comorbidities, and the COVID-19 pandemic. RESULTS: Among 6260 children hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed RSV infection, 1321 (21%) were hospitalized during their second year of life. Compared with first-year admissions, second-year patients had higher rates of prematurity (18.0% vs. 13.4%) and comorbidities (6.3% vs. 2.6%) (both p < 0.001). They more frequently received corticosteroids (53.5% vs. 23.2%) and antibiotics (63.4% vs. 45.9%) (both p < 0.001) and had lower PICU admission rates (4.6% vs. 7.0%; p < 0 0.001) and similar rates of prolonged LOS (35.0% vs. In adjusted analyses, second-year hospitalization was associated with increased corticosteroid use (adjusted odds ratio [aOR], 4.28; 95% CI, 3.67-4.99) and antibiotic use (aOR, 2.09; 95% CI, 1.80-2.43), and lower odds of PICU admission (aOR, 0.56; 95% CI, 0.40-0.79). CONCLUSIONS: RSV hospitalization in the second year of life carries a significant burden, with higher antibiotic and corticosteroid use despite lower PICU rates and similar LOS. These findings highlight the need for better characterization of this age group to optimize future immunoprophylaxis strategies.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

2025-2026 年呼吸季节期间美国老年人横断面样本中未接种疫苗的 COVID-19 和流感的危险因素。

Risk Factors for COVID-19 and Influenza Nonvaccination during the 2025-2026 Respiratory Season among a Cross-Sectional Sample of Older US Adults.
作者： Kate Penrose, McKaylee M. Robertson, Jenna Sanborn, Josefina Nunez Sahr, Rachael N. Piltch-Loeb, Angela M. Parcesepe, Denis Nash, Josefina Andrea Núñez-Sahr
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
年龄 ≥ 65 岁的成年人是严重 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19) 和流感结果的高风险,但不接种疫苗的原因仍未得到充分研究。
设计与对象
我们估计了居住在美国的社区、家庭和 SARS-CoV-2 流行病学 (CHASING) COVID 队列研究 中 65 岁以上成年人的年龄和性别调整后的 患病率 比率 (aPR) 和 95% 置信区间 (CI),进行两项比较:(1) 既没有感染 COVID-19 也没有感染流感 疫苗 与两者都感染疫苗,以及 (2) 在 2025 年 9 月至 2026 年 3 月期间仅接受流感与同时接受流感和 COVID-19 疫苗。
核心方法
总结了未收到的原因。
主要结果
在 2025-2026 年呼吸道季节,649 名参与者中,42.1% 的人未感染 COVID-19 疫苗,21.4% 的人未感染流感 疫苗。
解释与创新
那些对医生信任度最低、最难向提供者询问 疫苗 问题、对流感和 COVID-19 疫苗接种 的社区效益认知度最低、以及在 2025-2026 年呼吸道季节期间没有收到其他药物(呼吸道合胞病毒 [RSV]、带状疱疹或肺炎球菌)疫苗 的人,两者都不接受的可能性更高疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
特别需要集中精力增加这一高风险群体中的 COVID-19 疫苗接种。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Risk Factors for COVID-19 and Influenza Nonvaccination during the 2025-2026 Respiratory Season among a Cross-Sectional Sample of Older US Adults.

Authors: Kate Penrose, McKaylee M. Robertson, Jenna Sanborn, Josefina Nunez Sahr, Rachael N. Piltch-Loeb, Angela M. Parcesepe, Denis Nash, Josefina Andrea Núñez-Sahr
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Adults aged ≥ 65 years are high-risk for severe coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) and influenza outcomes, but reasons for nonvaccination remain understudied. METHODS: We estimated age- and gender-adjusted prevalence ratios (aPRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CI) among adults aged ≥ 65 years in the Communities, Households, and SARS-CoV-2 Epidemiology (CHASING) COVID Cohort living in the USA for two comparisons: (1) receiving neither the COVID-19 nor the influenza vaccine versus both vaccines, and (2) receiving only influenza versus both vaccines between September 2025 and March 2026. Reasons for nonreceipt are summarized. RESULTS: Among 649 participants, 42.1% did not receive the COVID-19 vaccine and 21.4% did not receive the influenza vaccine in the 2025-2026 respiratory season. Those with the lowest trust in their doctor (aPR: 5.56, 95% CI: 4.25-7.27), most difficulty asking their provider vaccine questions (aPR: 2.59, 95% CI: 1.70-3.94), lowest perceived community benefit of influenza (aPR: 12.25, 95% CI: 8.58-17.49) and COVID-19 (aPR: 12.12, 95% CI: 8.52-17.26) vaccination, and receipt of no other (respiratory syncytial virus [RSV], shingles, or pneumococcal) vaccine during the 2025-2026 respiratory season (aPR: 3.48, 95% CI: 2.13-5.66) had a higher likelihood of receiving neither versus both vaccines. These associations were attenuated when comparing receiving only influenza versus both the influenza and COVID-19 vaccines. Side effects and safety concerns were the most common reasons for not receiving the COVID-19 vaccine, though lack of a health care provider recommendation was a commonly reported reason among those who received ≥ 1 other vaccine (influenza, RSV, shingles, or pneumococcal) during the time period. For nonreceipt of the influenza vaccine, concerns about side effects and low perceived need were common reasons cited. CONCLUSIONS: Focused efforts are particularly needed to increase COVID-19 vaccination among this high-risk group. Health care providers should leverage clinical encounters to build trust, address safety and efficacy concerns, and ensure that older adults receive the full range of recommended vaccines, but reducing access barriers and building and maintaining trust in vaccines among older adults will likely also require a multipronged approach.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Adults aged ≥ 65 years are high-risk for severe coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) and influenza outcomes, but reasons for nonvaccination remain understudied.

Core methods
Reasons for nonreceipt are summarized.
Main results
Among 649 participants, 42.1% did not receive the COVID-19 vaccine and 21.4% did not receive the influenza vaccine in the 2025-2026 respiratory season.

Focused efforts are particularly needed to increase COVID-19 vaccination among this high-risk group.

2022-2025 年巴西严重急性呼吸道感染的病毒病原学、联合检测和年龄特异性 死亡率 的变化:一项全国性 监测 研究。

Shifting viral aetiology, codetection and age-specific mortality in severe acute respiratory infection in Brazil, 2022-2025: a nationwide surveillance study.
作者： Natasha Cristina Oliveira Andrade, Suziane do Socorro Dos Santos, Tamires de Nazaré Soares, Marcos Jessé Abrahão Silva, Rebecca Lobato Marinho, Karina Faine Freitas Takeda, Daniele Melo Sardinha, Luana Nepomuceno Gondim Costa Lima
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 通知中的呼吸道病毒病原学和联合检测在 COVID-19 后发生了变化。
设计与对象
提供的证据不报告研究设计或人群详细信息。
核心方法
我们通过 RT-PCR 病毒检测分析了全国 SIVEP-Gripe SARI 记录(2022-2025 年;2026 年 7 月提取)。
主要结果
Among 447,107 virus-positive records, 7.2% involved codetections and 25.4% included RSV. Isolated SARS-CoV-2 declined from 72.4% (2022) to 6.8% (2025), while codetections rose from 3.0% to 11.2%. Compared to isolated RSV, mortality was higher for codetection without RSV (aRR 1.80, 95% CI: 1.67-1.94), isolated SARS-CoV-2 (1.62, 1.54-1.70), and codetection with RSV (1.40, 1.27-1.55). Associations varied by age ($p < .001$). Among RSV-positive cases, codetection conferred increased mortality risk (aRR 1.46, 1.32-1.61). Excluding isolated SARS-CoV-2 attenuated the 2025-versus-2022 codetection prevalence ratio from 1.85 to 1.10.
解释与创新
在 监测 组成变化的强烈推动下,病毒检测转向更大的异质性。
科研与公卫意义
集成的、对年龄敏感的 监测 和标准化诊断报告至关重要;发病率 比例不反映人口 发病率。
局限与证据强度
我们研究了巴西的时间模式、年龄异质性和严重的结果关联。

Shifting viral aetiology, codetection and age-specific mortality in severe acute respiratory infection in Brazil, 2022-2025: a nationwide surveillance study.

Authors: Natasha Cristina Oliveira Andrade, Suziane do Socorro Dos Santos, Tamires de Nazaré Soares, Marcos Jessé Abrahão Silva, Rebecca Lobato Marinho, Karina Faine Freitas Takeda, Daniele Melo Sardinha, Luana Nepomuceno Gondim Costa Lima
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory viral aetiology and codetection among severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) notifications have evolved post-COVID-19. We examined temporal patterns, age heterogeneity, and severe outcome associations in Brazil. METHODS: We analysed nationwide SIVEP-Gripe SARI records (2022-2025; extracted July 2026) with RT-PCR viral detection. Seven mutually exclusive categories were evaluated using robust Poisson regression to estimate adjusted risk ratios (aRRs) for SARI-attributed mortality and a severe composite outcome (ICU admission, invasive ventilation, or death). RESULTS: Among 447,107 virus-positive records, 7.2% involved codetections and 25.4% included RSV. Isolated SARS-CoV-2 declined from 72.4% (2022) to 6.8% (2025), while codetections rose from 3.0% to 11.2%. Compared to isolated RSV, mortality was higher for codetection without RSV (aRR 1.80, 95% CI: 1.67-1.94), isolated SARS-CoV-2 (1.62, 1.54-1.70), and codetection with RSV (1.40, 1.27-1.55). Associations varied by age ($p < .001$). Among RSV-positive cases, codetection conferred increased mortality risk (aRR 1.46, 1.32-1.61). Excluding isolated SARS-CoV-2 attenuated the 2025-versus-2022 codetection prevalence ratio from 1.85 to 1.10. CONCLUSIONS: Viral detections shifted towards greater heterogeneity, strongly driven by changing surveillance composition. Varying testing indications and panels limit causal inference. Integrated, age-sensitive surveillance with standardized diagnostic reporting is essential; surveillance proportions do not reflect population incidence.

使用呼出气颗粒对下呼吸道抗原特异性抗体进行无创定量 - 一项观察性概念验证研究。

Non-invasive quantification of lower airway antigen-specific antibodies using exhaled breath particles - an observational proof-of-concept study.
作者： Katherina Aguilera, Andreas Hober, Nina Greilert Norin, Mina H. Mahdi, Vera C Stäheli, Iryna Kolosenko, Pia Lindberg, Emma Larsson, Ali Rihani, Jessica J. Alm
摘要中文翻译背景:粘膜 疫苗 发育的进展凸显了迫切需要实用的方法来评估上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的 免疫应答。尽管鼻腔采样日益标准化,但仍缺乏直接评估远端气道中抗原特异性抗体的非侵入性技术。方法:我们使用呼出空气中的颗粒物 (PExA) 来定量抗原特异性 IgA 和 IgG,这是一种从远端气道捕获雾化呼吸道内衬液 (RTLF) 的非侵入性方法。2025 年春季,对 28 名医护人员的配对血清、鼻腔分泌物和 PExA 样本进行了抗体检测,其中 18 名医护人员经 RT-qPCR 确诊为 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感 (H1N1) 或 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染。结果:PExA 样品中抗原特异性 IgA 和 IgG 测量值显示出较低的变异性(中位 CV:spike-IgA 3.7%、H1-IgA 7.2%、RSV pre-F-IgA 5.3%)。在 PExA 和血清 IgG 之间观察到强相关性(Spearman's rho >0.85,p < 0.0001),这可能与对下气道 IgG 水平的全身贡献一致。相比之下,IgA 相关性更为温和,这可能反映了肺部局部 IgA 的产生。PExA 和鼻分泌物之间的 IgG 和 IgA 相关,而鼻分泌物中的 IgA/IgG 比率较高(中位数 5.0 vs 0.6,p < 0.0001),与上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的预期同种型分布一致。解释:这项研究证明了无创测量下呼吸道抗原特异性抗体的可行性。我们的方法提出了一个评估下呼吸道免疫力和支持下一代粘膜 疫苗 开发的框架。资助:这项研究由欧洲研究理事会、瑞典医学研究学会以及克努特和爱丽丝·瓦伦堡基金会资助。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
粘膜 疫苗 发育的进展凸显了迫切需要实用的方法来评估上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的 免疫应答。
设计与对象
我们使用呼出空气中的颗粒 (PExA) 来定量抗原特异性 IgA 和 IgG,这是一种从远端气道捕获雾化呼吸道内衬液 (RTLF) 的非侵入性方法。2025 年春季,对 28 名医护人员的配对血清、鼻腔分泌物和 PExA 样本进行了抗体检测,其中 18 名医护人员经 RT-qPCR 确认感染 RSV。
核心方法
2025 年春季,对 28 名医护人员的配对血清、鼻腔分泌物和 PExA 样本进行了抗体检测,其中 18 名医护人员经 RT-qPCR 确诊为 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感 (H1N1) 或 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染。
主要结果
PExA 样品中抗原特异性 IgA 和 IgG 测量结果显示出较低的变异性(中值 CV:spike-IgA 3.7%、H1-IgA 7.2%、RSV pre-F-IgA 5.3%)。在 PExA 和血清 IgG 之间观察到强相关性(Spearman's rho >0.85,p < 0.0001),这可能与对下气道 IgG 水平的全身贡献一致。相比之下,IgA 相关性更为温和,这可能反映了肺部局部 IgA 的产生。
解释与创新
这项研究证明了无创测量下呼吸道抗原特异性抗体的可行性。我们的方法提出了一个评估下呼吸道免疫力和支持下一代粘膜 疫苗 开发的框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了一种评估下呼吸道免疫力的非侵入性方法,可以为 监测、诊断 和 疫苗 感染的发展提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小样本量和观察设计,但结果与上呼吸道和下呼吸道的预期同型分布一致,表明证据强度中等。

Non-invasive quantification of lower airway antigen-specific antibodies using exhaled breath particles - an observational proof-of-concept study.

Authors: Katherina Aguilera, Andreas Hober, Nina Greilert Norin, Mina H. Mahdi, Vera C Stäheli, Iryna Kolosenko, Pia Lindberg, Emma Larsson, Ali Rihani, Jessica J. Alm
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Advancements in mucosal vaccine development highlight the urgent need for practical methods to assess immune responses in both upper and lower airways. Although nasal sampling is increasingly standardised, a non-invasive technique for directly assessing antigen-specific antibodies in the distal airways has been lacking. METHODS: We used Particles in Exhaled Air (PExA), a non-invasive method capturing aerosolised respiratory tract lining fluid (RTLF) from the distal airways, to quantify antigen-specific IgA and IgG. Antibodies were measured in paired serum, nasal secretions, and PExA samples from 28 healthcare workers during spring 2025, including 18 with RT-qPCR-confirmed SARS-CoV-2, influenza A (H1N1), or respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection. FINDINGS: Antigen-specific IgA and IgG measurements in PExA samples showed low variability (median CVs: spike-IgA 3.7%, H1-IgA 7.2%, RSV pre-F-IgA 5.3%). Strong correlations (Spearman's rho >0.85, p < 0.0001) were observed between PExA and serum IgG, which may be consistent with a systemic contribution to lower-airway IgG levels. In contrast, IgA correlations were more modest, which may reflect local IgA production in the lungs. IgG and IgA correlated between PExA and nasal secretions, while the IgA/IgG ratio was higher in nasal secretions (median 5.0 vs 0.6, p < 0.0001), consistent with expected isotype distribution in upper and lower airways. INTERPRETATION: This study demonstrates the feasibility of non-invasive measurement of antigen-specific antibodies in the lower airways. Our approach proposes a framework for assessing lower-airway immunity and supporting next-generation mucosal vaccine development. FUNDING: This study was funded by the European Research Council, the Swedish Society for Medical Research, and the Knut and Alice Wallenberg Foundation.

Question and background
Advancements in mucosal vaccine development highlight the urgent need for practical methods to assess immune responses in both upper and lower airways.
Design and population
We used Particles in Exhaled Air (PExA), a non-invasive method capturing aerosolised respiratory tract lining fluid (RTLF) from the distal airways, to quantify antigen-specific IgA and IgG. Antibodies were measured in paired serum, nasal secretions, and PExA samples from 28 healthcare workers during spring 2025, including 18 with RT-qPCR-confirmed RSV infection.
Core methods
Antibodies were measured in paired serum, nasal secretions, and PExA samples from 28 healthcare workers during spring 2025, including 18 with RT-qPCR-confirmed SARS-CoV-2, influenza A (H1N1), or respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection.
Main results
Antigen-specific IgA and IgG measurements in PExA samples showed low variability (median CVs: spike-IgA 3.7%, H1-IgA 7.2%, RSV pre-F-IgA 5.3%). Strong correlations (Spearman's rho >0.85, p < 0.0001) were observed between PExA and serum IgG, which may be consistent with a systemic contribution to lower-airway IgG levels. In contrast, IgA correlations were more modest, which may reflect local IgA production in the lungs.
Interpretation and novelty
This study demonstrates the feasibility of non-invasive measurement of antigen-specific antibodies in the lower airways. Our approach proposes a framework for assessing lower-airway immunity and supporting next-generation mucosal vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a non-invasive method for assessing lower-airway immunity, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and vaccine development for respiratory syncytial virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a small sample size and observational design, but the results are consistent with expected isotype distribution in upper and lower airways, suggesting moderate evidence strength.

儿科 Omicron 感染的上呼吸道和神经系统表现:从新型病原体到地方性呼吸道病毒 - 免疫初始儿童与已免疫儿童的自然实验

Upper Airway and Neurological Manifestations of Pediatric Omicron Infection: From Novel Pathogen to Endemic Respiratory Virus — A Natural Experiment in Immuno-naive versus Primed Children
作者： Hao Chen1, Deze Li2, Shen Chen1
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征 Omicron 感染住院儿童的上气道阻塞 (UAO) 和神经系统事件,使用倾向评分匹配分析比较 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人类鼻病毒 (HRV) 的结果,并利用独特的自然实验来评估第一次和第二次免疫暴露对疾病严重程度的影响。
设计与对象
This retrospective cohort study identified 2,085 hospitalized children (0–18 years) with laboratory-confirmed infection: 211 with Omicron (temporal proxy), 424 with RSV, 824 with HRV, and 957 with other viruses. A natural-experiment analysis compared first exposure (Wave 1, December 2022–February 2023, n = 82) versus second exposure (Wave 2+, March–December 2023, n = 51) among children ≥ 6 months of age.
核心方法
倾向评分匹配 (1:2) 将 Omicron 与 RSV 和 HRV 进行比较,调整年龄、性别、合并症和入院严重程度。年龄分层和波周期分析是预先指定的。
主要结果
211 名 Omicron 儿童中,有 20 名发生 UAO(9.5%,95% CI 5.9-14.2%),而 424 名 RSV 儿童中,有 1 名发生 UAO(0.2%),824 名 HRV 儿童中,有 1 名发生 UAO(0.2%)。使用 Omicron 的 211 例患者中有 44 例观察到任何神经系统事件(20.9%,95% CI 15.7-26.9%),而使用 RSV 的 424 例患者中有 20 例(4.7%),824 例 HRV 患者中有 26 例(3.2%)观察到任何神经系统事件。
解释与创新
After matching, Omicron was associated with increased risks of MODS score ≥ 2 (vs. RSV: RR 3.80, 95% CI 1.84–7.86; vs. HRV: RR 4.50, 95% CI 2.32–8.72), non-febrile neurological events (vs. RSV: RR 5.2, 95% CI 2.8–9.6; vs. HRV: RR 6.8, 95% CI 3.9–11.8), and severe adverse outcomes (vs. RSV: RR 3.08, 95% CI 1.52–6.24; vs. HRV: RR 3.85, 95% CI 1.98–7.49).
科研与公卫意义
这些发现记录了 Omicron 从一种新型严重儿科病原体向一种既定呼吸道病毒的转变,并强调了针对特定年龄的临床警惕和持续 监测 的必要性。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Upper Airway and Neurological Manifestations of Pediatric Omicron Infection: From Novel Pathogen to Endemic Respiratory Virus — A Natural Experiment in Immuno-naive versus Primed Children

Authors: Hao Chen1, Deze Li2, Shen Chen1
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The Omicron variant of SARS-CoV-2 has been associated with altered clinical phenotypes in children, including upper airway involvement and neurological complications. However, the magnitude of these manifestations relative to established pediatric respiratory viruses remains incompletely quantified, and the extent to which immune priming attenuates severity is unknown. We aimed to characterize upper airway obstruction (UAO) and neurological events in hospitalized children with Omicron infection, to compare outcomes with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and human rhinovirus (HRV) using propensity score-matched analyzes, and to leverage a unique natural experiment to assess the impact of first versus second immune exposure on disease severity. Methods In this retrospective cohort study, we identified 2,085 hospitalized children (0–18 years) with laboratory-confirmed infection: 211 with Omicron (temporal proxy), 424 with RSV, 824 with HRV, and 957 with other viruses. Primary outcomes were UAO and neurological events. Propensity score matching (1:2) compared Omicron with RSV and HRV, adjusting for age, sex, comorbidities, and admission severity. A natural-experiment analysis compared first exposure (Wave 1, December 2022–February 2023, n = 82) versus second exposure (Wave 2+, March–December 2023, n = 51) among children ≥ 6 months of age. Age-stratified and wave-period analyzes were prespecified. Results UAO occurred in 20 of 211 children with Omicron (9.5%, 95% CI 5.9–14.2%) versus 1 of 424 with RSV (0.2%) and 1 of 824 with HRV (0.2%). Any neurological event was observed in 44 of 211 with Omicron (20.9%, 95% CI 15.7–26.9%) versus 20 of 424 with RSV (4.7%) and 26 of 824 with HRV (3.2%). After matching, Omicron was associated with increased risks of MODS score ≥ 2 (vs. RSV: RR 3.80, 95% CI 1.84–7.86; vs. HRV: RR 4.50, 95% CI 2.32–8.72), non-febrile neurological events (vs. RSV: RR 5.2, 95% CI 2.8–9.6; vs. HRV: RR 6.8, 95% CI 3.9–11.8), and severe adverse outcomes (vs. RSV: RR 3.08, 95% CI 1.52–6.24; vs. HRV: RR 3.85, 95% CI 1.98–7.49). In the immune exposure analysis, severe outcomes were markedly lower after second exposure (13.7%) than after first exposure (45.1%; p < 0.001), representing a 71% relative reduction. UAO decreased from 18.3% to 2.0% (p = 0.005), while LRTI rates were unchanged (45.1% vs. 56.9%; p = 0.214), indicating immune “taming” of severity without prevention of infection. Conclusions Pediatric Omicron infection was associated with substantially higher rates of UAO and neurological events than RSV or HRV, with risks extending beyond infancy. Immune priming dramatically attenuated severe outcomes and upper airway toxicity, though lower respiratory involvement persisted. These findings document the transition of Omicron from a novel, severe pediatric pathogen toward an established respiratory virus, and highlight the need for age-specific clinical vigilance and ongoing surveillance.

国家 RSV 免疫计划对希腊 毛细支气管炎 的影响。

Impact of national RSV immunization programme on the epidemiology of bronchiolitis in Greece.
作者： Argyro Konstantopoulou, Kyriaki Velali, Panagiota Markopoulou, Aliki S. Anastasiadou, Theodoros Nikolopoulos, Panagiota Panagiotou, Maria-Eleni Bompou, Anna Messaritaki, Athanasios Michos, Christina Kanaka-Gantenbein
摘要中文翻译摘要 2025-2026 年季节期间,希腊首次实施了婴儿 RSV 通用免疫策略,包括孕期单克隆抗体注射和母亲 疫苗接种。我们对2025年11月至2026年4月期间在希腊一家四级儿科医院住院使用毛细支气管炎的年龄<12个月的婴儿进行了一项前瞻性观察研究。将毛细支气管炎 毛细支气管炎与2023-2024年和2024-2025年季节进行了比较。使用修改后的 Tal 评分评估 毛细支气管炎 的严重程度。在这三个赛季中,15246 次入场中有 854 次是由于 毛细支气管炎 造成的。2025-2026 年,发病率 显着下降至每 1000 名入学者中有 42 名,而 2024-2025 年和 2023-2024 年分别为 64 名和 59 名。毛细支气管炎 2025-2026年流行曲线更平坦、持续时间更长,3月份出现延迟高峰。在 186 名 毛细支气管炎 婴儿中,19.9% 接受过 RSV 免疫接种。尽管未免疫婴儿年龄较大且并发症发生率较高,但免疫婴儿和未免疫婴儿之间的疾病严重程度和需氧量没有显着差异。总之,全国 RSV 免疫接种与 毛细支气管炎 住院率和并发症的减少相关。然而,疾病发病率下降幅度小于预期,凸显了提高认识、覆盖率和及时免疫接种的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估国家 RSV 免疫计划对希腊 毛细支气管炎 的影响,解决了解此类干预措施如何影响疾病模式的需要。
设计与对象
希腊一家四级儿科医院进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,检查了 2025 年 11 月至 2026 年 4 月期间住院接受 毛细支气管炎 治疗的年龄 <12 个月的婴儿。
核心方法
对三个赛季的 毛细支气管炎 发病率 进行比较,并使用修改后的 Tal 评分评估严重程度。记录因 毛细支气管炎 入院的婴儿的免疫状况。
主要结果
在这三个赛季中,15246 次入场中有 854 次是由于 毛细支气管炎 造成的。2025-2026 年,发病率 显着下降至每 1000 名入学者中有 42 名,而 2024-2025 年和 2023-2024 年分别为 64 名和 59 名。
解释与创新
研究发现,全国 RSV 免疫接种与 毛细支气管炎 住院率和并发症的减少相关,突显了其对疾病负担的潜在影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 免疫可能是减少 发病率 和 毛细支气管炎 严重程度的宝贵工具,为未来的公共卫生策略和 疫苗 实施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
疾病发病率下降幅度小于预期表明需要提高认识、覆盖范围和及时免疫接种,这可能会影响观察到的关联强度。

Impact of national RSV immunization programme on the epidemiology of bronchiolitis in Greece.

Authors: Argyro Konstantopoulou, Kyriaki Velali, Panagiota Markopoulou, Aliki S. Anastasiadou, Theodoros Nikolopoulos, Panagiota Panagiotou, Maria-Eleni Bompou, Anna Messaritaki, Athanasios Michos, Christina Kanaka-Gantenbein
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Universal RSV immunization strategies for infants, including monoclonal antibody administration and maternal vaccination during pregnancy, were implemented in Greece for the first time during the 2025–2026 season. We conducted a prospective observational study of infants aged <12 months hospitalized with bronchiolitis at a quaternary paediatric hospital in Greece between November 2025 and April 2026. Bronchiolitis incidence was compared with that of the 2023–2024 and 2024–2025 seasons. Severity of bronchiolitis was assessed using the modified Tal score. Across the three seasons, 854 of 15246 admissions were due to bronchiolitis. The incidence decreased significantly in 2025–2026 to 42 per 1000 admissions, compared to 64 and 59 in 2024–2025 and 2023–2024, respectively. The 2025–2026 epidemic curve of bronchiolitis was flatter and more prolonged, with a delayed peak in March. Of 186 infants with bronchiolitis, 19.9% had received RSV immunization. Disease severity and oxygen requirements did not differ significantly between immunized and unimmunized infants, although unimmunized infants were older and had a trend towards higher complication rates. In conclusion, national RSV immunization was associated with reduced bronchiolitis hospitalizations and complications. However, the smaller-than-expected reduction in disease rates underscores the need to improve awareness, coverage, and timely immunization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of the national RSV immunization programme on the epidemiology of bronchiolitis in Greece, addressing the need to understand how such interventions affect disease patterns.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted at a quaternary paediatric hospital in Greece, examining infants aged <12 months hospitalized with bronchiolitis between November 2025 and April 2026.
Core methods
Bronchiolitis incidence was compared across three seasons, and severity was assessed using the modified Tal score. Immunization status was recorded for infants admitted with bronchiolitis.
Main results
Across the three seasons, 854 of 15246 admissions were due to bronchiolitis. The incidence decreased significantly in 2025–2026 to 42 per 1000 admissions, compared to 64 and 59 in 2024–2025 and 2023–2024, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that national RSV immunization was associated with reduced bronchiolitis hospitalizations and complications, highlighting its potential impact on disease burden.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV immunization could be a valuable tool in reducing the incidence and severity of bronchiolitis, informing future public health strategies and vaccine implementation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The smaller-than-expected reduction in disease rates indicates the need for improved awareness, coverage, and timely immunization, which may affect the strength of the observed association.

估计废水中的病毒干扰 监测:贝叶斯多病原体 SEIR 框架。

Estimating viral interference from wastewater surveillance: a Bayesian multi-pathogen SEIR framework.
作者： Affan Shoukat, Mahsa Moosavi, Nicole Hansmeier, Jian-Hong Wu
摘要中文翻译流感、SARS-CoV-2 和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的共循环造成了复杂的 传播 动态,其中一种病毒的感染可能会减少或增强 传播 的另一种病毒。我们开发了一个贝叶斯多病原体 SEIR 模型,其中每种病毒的 传播 都会因共循环病原体的 患病率 而减少,这种减少通过一个汇总的人群水平干扰系数 θ 来量化。我们利用了 2024-2025 年呼吸道季节中六个加拿大省份的每周污水数据来拟合该模型,避免使用临床数据,因为临床数据受限于报告不足和检测不一致。我们的研究结果表明,估计的干扰效应对应于每种病毒在共循环期间的峰值 传播 减少了 4 到 30%,对有效繁殖数的减少则较小。尽管这些估计基于一个呼吸道季节,并且在不同的共循环模式下可能会有所不同,但它们与近期使用临床 监测 数据进行的多病原体病毒干扰研究结果一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估呼吸道病原体(特别是流感、SARS-CoV-2 和 RSV)之间的病毒干扰,因为它们的并发循环产生了复杂的 传播 动态。
设计与对象
使用 2024-2025 年呼吸季节期间加拿大六个省份的废水数据,开发了贝叶斯多病原体 SEIR 模型,以量化在存在共循环病原体的情况下每种病毒的 传播 减少量。
核心方法
该模型根据每周的废水数据进行拟合,而不使用临床数据来估计人群水平的干扰系数θ,而临床数据受到报告不足和测试不一致的限制。
主要结果
估计的干扰显示,在共循环期间,每种病毒的峰值传播减少了4到30%,有效繁殖数的减少幅度较小。
解释与创新
尽管基于单一呼吸季节和不同共循环模式下的潜在变异性,但这些发现与最近使用临床 监测 数据进行的多病原体病毒干扰研究一致。
科研与公卫意义
结果提供了有关病毒干扰对 传播 动态的潜在影响的见解,这可以为 监测 针对呼吸道病毒的策略和公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一呼吸季节废水数据的依赖而受到限制,并且由于共循环模式的潜在变异性,证据并不充分。

Estimating viral interference from wastewater surveillance: a Bayesian multi-pathogen SEIR framework.

Authors: Affan Shoukat, Mahsa Moosavi, Nicole Hansmeier, Jian-Hong Wu
Original abstract (English)
The concurrent circulation of influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV creates complex transmission dynamics in which infection with one virus may reduce or enhance transmission of another. We developed a Bayesian multi-pathogen SEIR model in which the transmission of each virus is reduced by the prevalence of co-circulating pathogens, quantified by an aggregate population-level interference coefficient θ. We fit the model to weekly wastewater data from six Canadian provinces over the 2024-2025 respiratory season, avoiding the use of clinical data, which are limited by under-reporting and inconsistent testing. Our findings show the estimated interference corresponded to a 4 to 30% reduction in peak transmission of each virus during co-circulation, with smaller reductions in the effective reproduction number. Although these estimates are based on a single respiratory season and can vary under different patterns of co-circulation, they are consistent with recent studies on multi-pathogen viral interference using clinical surveillance data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to estimate viral interference among respiratory pathogens, particularly influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV, due to the complex transmission dynamics created by their concurrent circulation.
Design and population
A Bayesian multi-pathogen SEIR model was developed to quantify the transmission reduction of each virus in the presence of co-circulating pathogens, using wastewater data from six Canadian provinces during the 2024-2025 respiratory season.
Core methods
The model was fitted to weekly wastewater data without using clinical data, which are limited by under-reporting and inconsistent testing, to estimate the population-level interference coefficient θ.
Main results
The estimated interference showed a 4 to 30% reduction in peak transmission of each virus during co-circulation, with smaller reductions in the effective reproduction number.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings are consistent with recent studies on multi-pathogen viral interference using clinical surveillance data, despite being based on a single respiratory season and potential variability under different co-circulation patterns.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the potential impact of viral interference on transmission dynamics, which can inform surveillance strategies and public health interventions for respiratory viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on wastewater data from a single respiratory season, and the evidence is moderate due to the potential variability in co-circulation patterns.

关于腺病毒 媒介 疫苗 对抗 RSV 的新型口咽喷雾免疫功效的验证性双中心非人灵长类动物研究 - 重要经验教训。

A Confirmatory, Dual Center Nonhuman Primate Study on the Efficacy of Novel Oropharyngeal Spray Immunization With an Adenoviral Vector Vaccine Against RSV-Important Lessons Learned.
作者： Matthias Tenbusch, Gerrit Koopman, Petra Mooij, Berit Roshani, Pascal Irrgang, Dennis Lapuente, Ivanela Kondova, Willy M Bogers, Edmond J Remarque, Ramona Vestweber
摘要中文翻译背景:在欧洲两个灵长类研究机构的一项随机、盲法、双中心研究中,我们评估了 DNA 初免口咽腺病毒增强 疫苗接种 策略针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的免疫原性和保护功效。方法:恒河猴通过电穿孔接收 RSV 融合蛋白 (F) DNA 疫苗,然后两次粘膜注射编码相同抗原的重组腺病毒 媒介。模拟接种动物和模拟原发性 RSV 感染诱导的自然免疫的比较组作为对照。纵向监测全身和粘膜免疫应答。结果:我们检测到了强大的细胞和体液 免疫应答,但两个中心之间存在一些差异。然而,接种 RSV 疫苗的动物在 RSV 攻击感染后,上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的病毒复制明显减少。一个研究中心的对照组中出现了意外的 RSV 特异性免疫,导致无法确认预定义的主要终点。结论:我们的结果支持 DNA 引发后粘膜腺病毒增强诱导针对 RSV 的保护性免疫的潜力,同时强调了与多中心临床前 疫苗 研究相关的挑战。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
为了评估恒河猴中 DNA 初免口咽腺病毒增强 疫苗接种 策略针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的免疫原性和保护功效,满足改进 RSV 疫苗 的需求。
设计与对象
两个欧洲灵长类研究机构进行的一项随机、盲法、双中心研究评估了恒河猴中的 疫苗。
核心方法
恒河猴通过电穿孔接收 RSV F DNA 疫苗,然后两次粘膜施用编码相同抗原的重组腺病毒 媒介,其中模拟疫苗接种的动物和比较组作为对照。
主要结果
检测到强大的细胞和体液 免疫应答,并且 RSV 疫苗接种的动物在 RSV 攻击感染后证明上呼吸道和下呼吸道中的病毒复制减少。
解释与创新
该研究支持 DNA 引发后粘膜腺病毒增强诱导针对 RSV 的保护性免疫的潜力,凸显了多中心临床前 疫苗 研究中的挑战。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为 RSV 疫苗 的开发提供了一种有前途的方法,并强调了跨多个中心一致的研究设计的重要性。
局限与证据强度
研究设计的局限性,包括中心之间的差异和对​​照组的意外免疫力,可能会影响证据的强度。

A Confirmatory, Dual Center Nonhuman Primate Study on the Efficacy of Novel Oropharyngeal Spray Immunization With an Adenoviral Vector Vaccine Against RSV-Important Lessons Learned.

Authors: Matthias Tenbusch, Gerrit Koopman, Petra Mooij, Berit Roshani, Pascal Irrgang, Dennis Lapuente, Ivanela Kondova, Willy M Bogers, Edmond J Remarque, Ramona Vestweber
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In a randomized, blinded, dual center study across two European primate research facilities, we evaluated the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of a DNA prime-oropharyngeal adenoviral boost vaccination strategy against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). METHODS: Rhesus macaques received an RSV fusion protein (F) DNA vaccine via electroporation, followed by two mucosal administrations of a recombinant adenovirus vector encoding the same antigen. Mock-vaccinated animals and a comparator group mimicking natural immunity induced by primary RSV infection were included as controls. Systemic and mucosal immune responses were monitored longitudinally. RESULTS: We detected robust cellular and humoral immune responses, but with some variability between the two centers. Nevertheless, RSV-vaccinated animals demonstrated markedly reduced viral replication in the upper and lower respiratory tract after RSV challenge infection. Unexpected RSV-specific immunity in the control group at one single study site prevented confirmation of the predefined primary endpoint. CONCLUSION: Our results support the potential of mucosal adenoviral boosting following DNA priming to induce protective immunity against RSV, while highlighting challenges associated with multi-center preclinical vaccine studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To evaluate the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of a DNA prime-oropharyngeal adenoviral boost vaccination strategy against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in rhesus macaques, addressing the need for improved RSV vaccines.
Design and population
A randomized, blinded, dual center study across two European primate research facilities evaluated the vaccine in rhesus macaques.
Core methods
Rhesus macaques received an RSV F DNA vaccine via electroporation, followed by two mucosal administrations of a recombinant adenovirus vector encoding the same antigen, with mock-vaccinated animals and a comparator group included as controls.
Main results
Robust cellular and humoral immune responses were detected, and RSV-vaccinated animals demonstrated reduced viral replication in the upper and lower respiratory tract after RSV challenge infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study supports the potential of mucosal adenoviral boosting following DNA priming to induce protective immunity against RSV, highlighting challenges in multi-center preclinical vaccine studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a promising approach for RSV vaccine development and highlight the importance of consistent study design across multiple centers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study design limitations, including variability between centers and unexpected immunity in the control group, may affect the strength of the evidence.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

2023 年至 2025 年新 疫苗 监测 网络中 20 个月以下儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 相关的社会人口统计学和临床​​因素。

Sociodemographic and Clinical Factors Associated With Nirsevimab Receipt Among Children Aged Younger Than 20 Months in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network in 2023 to 2025.
作者： Zheyi Teoh, Ayzsa Tannis, Erin Sullivan, Julie A Boom, Natasha Halasa, Marian G Michaels, Daniel C Payne, Leila C Sahni, Jennifer Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan
摘要中文翻译目的:我们的目的是评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与在多个季节和出生后住院治疗的急性呼吸道疾病 (ARI) 儿童中接受 尼塞韦单抗 的关系。患者和方法:我们纳入了在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 季节(10 月 1 日至 3 月 31 日)期间加入新 疫苗 监测 网络的符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的儿童,年龄小于 8 个月和 8 至 19 个月。使用混合效应逻辑回归模型来评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 8 个月以下入组儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 的可能性之间的关联。结果:在 2023 年至 2024 年 RSV 季节登记的 2151 名符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的 ARI 儿童和 2024 年至 2025 年 RSV 季节登记的 1819 名儿童中,96% 的年龄小于 8 个月且处于第一个 RSV 季节。在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年季节,分别有 14% 和 36% 的 8 个月以下符合条件的儿童获得了 尼塞韦单抗。在 8 个月以下的婴儿中,在 RSV 季节出生的婴儿和有基础疾病的婴儿更有可能在两个 RSV 季节接受 尼塞韦单抗;婴儿女性性别和母亲教育也与 2023 年至 2024 年期间的 尼塞韦单抗 接收相关,但与 2024 年至 2025 年无关。结论:本次分析中,大多数符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格且就医的 ARI 儿童在 尼塞韦单抗 可用性的前 2 个季节期间没有接受 尼塞韦单抗。RSV 季节之前出生等因素与 尼塞韦单抗 接收的可能性降低相关,强调需要在出生住院后采取量身定制的干预措施,以增加该组儿童对 尼塞韦单抗 的吸收。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估多个季节和出生后住院治疗的急性呼吸道疾病 (ARI) 儿童中社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 尼塞韦单抗 收据的关联。
设计与对象
该研究包括在 2023 年至 2024 年和 2024 年至 2025 年 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 季节(10 月 1 日至 3 月 31 日)期间加入新 疫苗 监测 网络的符合 尼塞韦单抗 资格的儿童,年龄小于 8 个月和 8 至 19 个月,患有 ARI。
核心方法
使用混合效应逻辑回归模型来评估社会人口统计学和临床​​因素与 8 个月以下入组儿童接受 尼塞韦单抗 的可能性之间的关联。
主要结果
在2023至2024呼吸道合胞病毒流行季入组的2151名符合尼塞韦单抗条件的儿童中,以及在2024至2025呼吸道合胞病毒流行季入组的1819名儿童中,96%年龄小于8个月且处于首个呼吸道合胞病毒流行季。在年龄小于8个月的符合条件儿童中,于2023至2024流行季和2024至2025流行季分别有14%和36%接种了尼塞韦单抗。
解释与创新
在 8 个月以下的婴儿中,在 RSV 季节出生的婴儿和有基础疾病的婴儿更有可能在两个 RSV 季节接受 尼塞韦单抗;婴儿女性性别和母亲教育也与 2023 年至 2024 年期间的 尼塞韦单抗 收据相关,但与 2024 年至 2025 年无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以提高符合条件的儿童,特别是在 RSV 季节之前出生的儿童对 尼塞韦单抗 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性,但该研究基于新 疫苗 监测 网络的数据,这可能会限制对其他人群的普遍适用性。

Sociodemographic and Clinical Factors Associated With Nirsevimab Receipt Among Children Aged Younger Than 20 Months in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network in 2023 to 2025.

Authors: Zheyi Teoh, Ayzsa Tannis, Erin Sullivan, Julie A Boom, Natasha Halasa, Marian G Michaels, Daniel C Payne, Leila C Sahni, Jennifer Schuster, Rangaraj Selvarangan
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: We aim to evaluate the association of sociodemographic and clinical factors with nirsevimab receipt among children with medically attended acute respiratory illness (ARI) across multiple seasons and beyond birth hospitalization. PATIENTS AND METHODS: We included nirsevimab-eligible children aged younger than 8 months and 8 to 19 months with ARI enrolled in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network during the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) seasons (October 1 to March 31). A mixed-effects logistic regression model was used to evaluate the association between sociodemographic and clinical factors with the likelihood of nirsevimab receipt among enrolled children aged younger than 8 months. RESULTS: Of the 2151 nirsevimab-eligible children with ARI enrolled in the 2023 to 2024 RSV season and 1819 in the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, 96% were aged younger than 8 months and in their first RSV season. Fourteen percent and 36% of eligible children aged younger than 8 months received nirsevimab in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 seasons, respectively. Among infants aged younger than 8 months, those born during the RSV season and those with an underlying medical condition were more likely to receive nirsevimab in both RSV seasons; infant female sex and maternal education were also associated with nirsevimab receipt during 2023 to 2024 but not 2024 to 2025. CONCLUSION: Most nirsevimab-eligible children with medically attended ARI in this analysis did not receive nirsevimab during the first 2 seasons of nirsevimab availability. Factors such as birth before the RSV season were associated with a decreased likelihood of nirsevimab receipt, emphasizing the need for tailored interventions following birth hospitalization to increase nirsevimab uptake for this group of children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the association of sociodemographic and clinical factors with nirsevimab receipt among children with medically attended acute respiratory illness (ARI) across multiple seasons and beyond birth hospitalization.
Design and population
The study included nirsevimab-eligible children aged younger than 8 months and 8 to 19 months with ARI enrolled in the New Vaccine Surveillance Network during the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) seasons (October 1 to March 31).
Core methods
A mixed-effects logistic regression model was used to evaluate the association between sociodemographic and clinical factors with the likelihood of nirsevimab receipt among enrolled children aged younger than 8 months.
Main results
Of the 2151 nirsevimab-eligible children with ARI enrolled in the 2023 to 2024 RSV season and 1819 in the 2024 to 2025 RSV season, 96% were aged younger than 8 months and in their first RSV season. Fourteen percent and 36% of eligible children aged younger than 8 months received nirsevimab in the 2023 to 2024 and 2024 to 2025 seasons, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
Among infants aged younger than 8 months, those born during the RSV season and those with an underlying medical condition were more likely to receive nirsevimab in both RSV seasons; infant female sex and maternal education were also associated with nirsevimab receipt during 2023 to 2024 but not 2024 to 2025.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve nirsevimab uptake among eligible children, particularly those born before the RSV season.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations, but the study is based on data from the New Vaccine Surveillance Network, which may limit generalizability to other populations.

异质城市污水系统中的多病毒废水 治疗:来自污水 治疗 工厂和明渠的比较见解。

Multi-virus Wastewater Surveillance in Heterogeneous Urban-Sewage Systems: Comparative Insights From Sewage Treatment Plants and Open Drains.
作者： Ambreen Shaikh, Shivam Nikam, Nerges F. Mistry
摘要中文翻译基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 已应用于公共卫生 监测 数十年,但在 COVID-19 大流行期间在全球范围内获得了前所未有的关注,导致其在多病原体 监测 中得到更广泛的采用。然而,异构城市废水基础设施对病原体信号检测和解释的影响仍然没有得到充分表征,特别是在孟买等下水道网络分散且部分连接的环境中。本研究系统地比较了来自集中式污水处理厂(STP)和分散式明渠(OD)的多病原体废水 监测,它们分别代表聚集和局部人口集水区。在两个监测期(2023 年和 2024 年)收集废水,并通过 RT-qPCR 分析 SARS-CoV-2、流感病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)。比较分析包括检测频率、时间动态以及节点类型之间病毒共现的模式。STP 流入物中的病毒检出率最高,这与较大人群的信号整合和短期变异性降低一致。相反,漏极开路表现出更大的时间波动,这可能与局部的短期变化一致,但信号持久性降低。在缺乏并发临床数据的情况下,这些节点级差异被解释为废水信号的模式,而不是流行病学敏感性或早期检测的表现。SARS-CoV-2 在两种节点类型中均被检测到,而流感病毒检测则更为零星,并表现出明显的变异性,表明季节性 传播 动态。2023 年观察到甲型和乙型流感的共同检测,甲型流感亚型显示类型内多样性有限;无法确定检测到的亚型的来源归属。总的来说,这些发现表明废水信号曲线反映了病原体特定特征和采样网络结构配置之间的相互作用。总体而言,研究结果支持利用集中式和分散式节点的综合 监测 框架,同时强调需要扩展时间监测和独立临床验证以加强流行病学推断。

本研究系统地比较了来自集中式污水处理厂(STP)和分散式明渠(OD)的多病原体废水 监测,它们分别代表聚集和局部人口集水区。
核心方法

总体而言,研究结果支持利用集中式和分散式节点的综合 监测 框架,同时强调需要扩展时间监测和独立临床验证以加强流行病学推断。

Multi-virus Wastewater Surveillance in Heterogeneous Urban-Sewage Systems: Comparative Insights From Sewage Treatment Plants and Open Drains.

Authors: Ambreen Shaikh, Shivam Nikam, Nerges F. Mistry
Original abstract (English)
Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) has been applied for public health surveillance for several decades but gained unprecedented global prominence during the COVID-19 pandemic, leading to its broader adoption for multi-pathogen surveillance. However, the impact of heterogeneous urban wastewater infrastructure on the detection and interpretation of pathogen signals remains inadequately characterised, particularly in settings such as Mumbai, with fragmented and partially connected sewer networks. This study systematically compared multi-pathogen wastewater surveillance from centralised sewage treatment plants (STPs) and decentralised open drains (ODs), which represent aggregated and localised population catchments, respectively. Wastewater was collected during two monitoring periods (2023 and 2024) and analysed by RT-qPCR for SARS-CoV-2, influenza viruses, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Comparative analyses included detection frequency, temporal dynamics, and patterns of viral co-occurrence between node types. Viral detection rates were highest in STP influent, consistent with signal integration over larger populations and reduced short-term variability. Conversely, open drains exhibited greater temporal fluctuation, which may be consistent with localised, short-term changes but with reduced signal persistence. In the absence of concurrent clinical data, these node-level differences are interpreted as patterns in the wastewater signal rather than demonstrated gains in epidemiological sensitivity or earlier detection. SARS-CoV-2 was consistently detected across both node types, while influenza virus detection was more sporadic and exhibited marked variability, suggesting seasonal transmission dynamics. Co-detection of influenza A and B was observed in 2023, and influenza A subtyping revealed limited within-type diversity; source attribution for the detected subtypes could not be determined. Collectively, these findings indicate that wastewater signal profiles reflect an interplay between pathogen-specific characteristics and the structural configuration of the sampling network. Overall, the findings support integrated surveillance frameworks that leverage both centralised and decentralised nodes, while highlighting the need for extended temporal monitoring and independent clinical validation to strengthen epidemiological inference.

流行病学 监测 的快速工程:通过自然语言处理更广泛地获取公共卫生数据分析的一步。

Prompt engineering for epidemiological surveillance: A step toward wider access to public health data analysis through natural language processing.
作者： Gabriel dos Santos Mouta, Sarah P.R. Freire, Giorgio Montesi, Luis G.E. Souza, José D.A. Araújo, Vanderson de Souza Sampaio, Donata Medaglini, Helder Imoto Nakaya
摘要中文翻译目标:本研究的目的是描述和评估一个框架,该框架根据 监测 数据字典约束的自然语言查询生成流行病学分析脚本。研究设计:方法框架开发和内部一致性评估。方法:PEFES(流行病学提示工程 监测)将大型语言模型输出限制为字典声明的变量和编码方案,在生成脚本之前验证范围,并且不执行代码。两位具有编程经验的作者(“评估者”)使用 Claude Opus 4.8 和 Gemini 3.7 Flash 针对 OpenDataSUS 数据集在三个案例研究(呼吸道合胞病毒、甲型流感和登革热)中提交了相同的 22 个范围内查询和 22 个范围控制查询。结果:两位评估者就所有 22 个范围控制查询的行为达成一致:预计被拒绝的 17 个查询没有生成脚本,而所有 5 个范围内对应查询都生成了脚本。读取的每个源字段都是字典声明的。22 个范围内查询中,有 14 个查询的报告值相同,有 8 个查询的报告值不同(所有查询均在提示中留下了未说明的选项)。一个会话将声明的字段与错误类型的值进行比较,在格式化标题下返回空表。结论:字典约束限制了生成的脚本可以分析哪些数据,但不限制分析数据的方式:再现性取决于提示和字典,而不是单独的约束机制。声明的集合中的错误是由评估者审查而不是由框架识别的,从而激发执行前类型检查或返回未声明的选择作为澄清请求。PEFES 加速了流行病学工作,而不是取代它,这是朝着更广泛地获取 监测 数据分析迈出的一步。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述和评估一个框架,该框架根据 监测 数据字典约束的自然语言查询生成流行病学分析脚本。该框架的开发是为了满足对更易于使用的公共卫生数据分析工具的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及开发具有内部一致性评估的方法框架。两位具有编程经验的作者使用 Claude Opus 4.8 和 Gemini 3.7 Flash 针对 OpenDataSUS 数据集在三个案例研究(呼吸道合胞病毒、甲型流感和登革热)中提交了相同的 22 个范围内查询和 22 个范围控制查询集。
核心方法
PEFES(流行病学提示工程 监测)将大型语言模型输出限制为字典声明的变量和编码方案,在生成脚本之前验证范围,并且不执行代码。通过将生成的脚本与预定义的查询和数据集进行比较来测试该框架。
主要结果
两位评估者就所有 22 个范围控制查询的行为达成一致:预计被拒绝的 17 个查询没有生成脚本,而所有 5 个范围内对应查询都生成了脚本。读取的每个源字段都是字典声明的。22 个范围内查询中,有 14 个查询的报告值相同,有 8 个查询的报告值不同(所有查询均在提示中留下了未说明的选项)。
解释与创新
字典约束限制了生成的脚本可以分析哪些数据,但不限制如何分析数据:可重复性取决于提示和字典,而不仅仅是约束机制。该研究强调了自然语言处理在流行病学领域的潜力 监测。
科研与公卫意义
PEFES 加速了流行病学工作,而不是取代它,这是朝着更广泛地获取 监测 数据分析迈出的一步。这可以提高公共卫生数据处理和解释的效率。
局限与证据强度
声明的集合中的错误是由评估者审查而不是由框架识别的,从而激发执行前类型检查或返回未声明的选择作为澄清请求。由于该研究是基于内部一致性评估和有限的案例研究,因此证据是中等的。

Prompt engineering for epidemiological surveillance: A step toward wider access to public health data analysis through natural language processing.

Authors: Gabriel dos Santos Mouta, Sarah P.R. Freire, Giorgio Montesi, Luis G.E. Souza, José D.A. Araújo, Vanderson de Souza Sampaio, Donata Medaglini, Helder Imoto Nakaya
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: The aim of this study was to describe and assess a framework that generates epidemiological analysis scripts from natural-language queries constrained by surveillance data dictionaries. STUDY DESIGN: Methodological framework development with internal consistency assessment. METHODS: PEFES (Prompt Engineering for Epidemiological Surveillance) restricts large language model output to dictionary-declared variables and coding schemes, validates scope before generating a script, and does not execute code. Two authors with prior programming experience ('raters') submitted identical sets of 22 in-scope and 22 scope-control queries across three case studies (respiratory syncytial virus, influenza A and dengue) using Claude Opus 4.8 and Gemini 3.7 Flash against OpenDataSUS datasets. RESULTS: The two raters agreed on the behavior for all 22 scope-control queries: the 17 queries that were expected to be declined did not produce a script, and all 5 in-scope counterparts produced a script. Every source field read was dictionary-declared. Reported values were identical for 14 of the 22 in-scope queries and divergent for 8 (all where a prompt left a choice unstated). One session compared a declared field against wrong-type values, returning empty tables under formatted headings. CONCLUSIONS: Dictionary constraints restricted which data a generated script could analyze, but not how it was analyzed: reproducibility was dependent on both the prompt and dictionary, not the constraint mechanism alone. Errors within the declared set were identified by rater review, not by the framework, motivating pre-execution type checks or returning unstated choices as clarification requests. PEFES accelerates epidemiological work rather than replacing it, and it is a step toward wider access to surveillance data analysis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe and assess a framework that generates epidemiological analysis scripts from natural-language queries constrained by surveillance data dictionaries. The framework was developed to address the need for more accessible public health data analysis tools.
Design and population
The study involved the development of a methodological framework with internal consistency assessment. Two authors with prior programming experience submitted identical sets of 22 in-scope and 22 scope-control queries across three case studies (respiratory syncytial virus, influenza A, and dengue) using Claude Opus 4.8 and Gemini 3.7 Flash against OpenDataSUS datasets.
Core methods
PEFES (Prompt Engineering for Epidemiological Surveillance) restricts large language model output to dictionary-declared variables and coding schemes, validates scope before generating a script, and does not execute code. The framework was tested by comparing generated scripts against predefined queries and datasets.
Main results
The two raters agreed on the behavior for all 22 scope-control queries: the 17 queries that were expected to be declined did not produce a script, and all 5 in-scope counterparts produced a script. Every source field read was dictionary-declared. Reported values were identical for 14 of the 22 in-scope queries and divergent for 8 (all where a prompt left a choice unstated).
Interpretation and novelty
Dictionary constraints restricted which data a generated script could analyze, but not how it was analyzed: reproducibility was dependent on both the prompt and dictionary, not the constraint mechanism alone. The study highlights the potential of natural language processing in epidemiological surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
PEFES accelerates epidemiological work rather than replacing it, and it is a step toward wider access to surveillance data analysis. This could enhance the efficiency of public health data processing and interpretation.
Limitations and evidence strength
Errors within the declared set were identified by rater review, not by the framework, motivating pre-execution type checks or returning unstated choices as clarification requests. The evidence is moderate as the study is based on internal consistency assessment and limited case studies.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

尼塞韦单抗 免疫后第一年和第二年的结果。

First- and Second-Year Outcomes After Nirsevimab Immunization.
作者： Amélie Gabet, Epiphane Kolla, Ludovic Tréluyer, Marion Bertrand, Marie-Joelle Jabagi, Olivia Weill, Antoine Rachas, Valérie Olié, Mahmoud Zureik
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 尼塞韦单抗 在连续季节中对 RSV-LRTI 相关住院治疗的有效性,及其与随访第一年和第二年因非 RSV 感染和哮喘住院的关系。
设计与对象
这项 队列研究 研究使用法国国家健康数据系统分析了 2 个全国匹配的 队列研究,其中包括 2023 年和 2024 年免疫接种活动之前在法国本土出生的婴儿。接受 尼塞韦单抗 的婴儿与未免疫婴儿以 1:1 进行匹配。两种 队列研究 均在免疫后随访 1 年,并对 2023 队列研究 进行第二年随访。
核心方法
使用加权 Cox 比例风险模型评估 RSV-LRTI 相关、非 RSV 感染和哮喘住院情况。数据分析于2023年2月至2025年8月进行。
主要结果
Among 37 366 infant pairs in 2023 and 87 763 pairs in 2024, RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations during the first year of follow-up occurred in 299 (0.8%) immunized vs 899 (2.4%) unimmunized infants in 2023 and 456 (0.5%) vs 1711 (1.9%), respectively, in 2024. Nirsevimab was associated with a lower rate of RSV-LRTI-related hospitalization in both the 2023 and 2024 cohort during the first year of follow-up, with effectiveness estimates of 66% (95% CI, 61%-70%) and 72% (95% CI, 69%-75%), respectively. An association with higher rates of hospitalization for ENT bacterial infections was observed (2023 cohort: weighted hazard ratio [wHR], 1.36; 95% CI, 1.00-1.84; 2024 cohort: wHR, 1.43; 95% CI, 1.15-1.78).
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,尼塞韦单抗 与 2023-2024 年和 2024-2025 年季节第一年 RSV-LRTI 相关住院治疗的大幅减少有关。然而,他们指出,这与非 RSV 感染或哮喘导致的住院治疗减少无关,也与随访第二年的减少无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,尼塞韦单抗 可能在免疫后第一年针对 RSV-LRTI 相关住院提供显着保护,这可以为未来针对婴儿 RSV 的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息。然而,第二年缺乏保护性关联以及与非 RSV 感染或哮喘的关联表明需要进一步研究以了解长期影响和更广泛的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖于国家卫生数据系统的观察数据而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差或混杂因素。由于缺乏随机对照试验以及数据收集和随访方面的潜在限制,证据强度中等。

First- and Second-Year Outcomes After Nirsevimab Immunization.

Authors: Amélie Gabet, Epiphane Kolla, Ludovic Tréluyer, Marion Bertrand, Marie-Joelle Jabagi, Olivia Weill, Antoine Rachas, Valérie Olié, Mahmoud Zureik
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: The consistency of nirsevimab effectiveness across consecutive seasons and its broader clinical protective effects remain to be fully characterized. OBJECTIVE: To assess whether first-season effectiveness of nirsevimab against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI)-related hospitalizations is maintained during a new RSV season, whether the effectiveness persists beyond the first season, and whether nirsevimab exposure is associated with hospitalizations for non-RSV infections and asthma during the first and second years of follow-up. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: This cohort study analyzed 2 nationwide matched cohorts using the French National Health Data System, including infants born in metropolitan France before the 2023 and 2024 immunization campaigns. Infants receiving nirsevimab were matched 1:1 to unimmunized infants. Both cohorts were followed up for 1 year after immunization, with an additional second year of follow-up for the 2023 cohort. Data analysis was performed from February 2023 to August 2025. EXPOSURES: Single dose of nirsevimab. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: RSV-LRTI-related, non-RSV infection, and asthma hospitalizations were assessed using weighted Cox proportional hazards models. RESULTS: This cohort study included 74 672 infants in 2023 and 175 526 in 2024. At baseline, the mean (SD) age of immunized infants was 4.5 (2.0) months in the 2023 cohort; 19 633 (52.6%) were male and 17 703 (47.4%) were female. The mean (SD) age of the 2024 cohort was 4.8 (2.2) months; 44 863 immunized infants (51.1%) were male and 42 900 (48.9%) were female. Among 37 366 infant pairs in 2023 and 87 763 pairs in 2024, RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations during the first year of follow-up occurred in 299 (0.8%) immunized vs 899 (2.4%) unimmunized infants in 2023 and 456 (0.5%) vs 1711 (1.9%), respectively, in 2024. Nirsevimab was associated with a lower rate of RSV-LRTI-related hospitalization in both the 2023 and 2024 cohort during the first year of follow-up, with effectiveness estimates of 66% (95% CI, 61%-70%) and 72% (95% CI, 69%-75%), respectively. An association with higher rates of hospitalization for ear, nose, and throat (ENT) bacterial infections was observed (2023 cohort: weighted hazard ratio [wHR], 1.36; 95% CI, 1.00-1.84; 2024 cohort: wHR, 1.43; 95% CI, 1.15-1.78). No significant association was observed for other outcomes. During the second year of follow-up, no protective association was observed for RSV-LRTI-related hospitalization (wHR, 1.03; 95% CI, 0.73-1.43). An association with higher rates of hospitalization for ENT (wHR, 1.56; 95% CI, 1.10-2.22) and asthma (wHR, 1.24; 95% CI, 1.05-1.46) was observed. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: This study found that nirsevimab was associated with a substantial reduction in RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations during the first year across both the 2023-2024 and 2024-2025 seasons. It was not associated with reduced hospitalizations due to non-RSV infections or asthma, nor during the second year of follow-up.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of nirsevimab against RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations across consecutive seasons and its association with hospitalizations for non-RSV infections and asthma during the first and second years of follow-up.
Design and population
This cohort study analyzed 2 nationwide matched cohorts using the French National Health Data System, including infants born in metropolitan France before the 2023 and 2024 immunization campaigns. Infants receiving nirsevimab were matched 1:1 to unimmunized infants. Both cohorts were followed up for 1 year after immunization, with an additional second year of follow-up for the 2023 cohort.
Core methods
RSV-LRTI-related, non-RSV infection, and asthma hospitalizations were assessed using weighted Cox proportional hazards models. Data analysis was performed from February 2023 to August 2025.
Main results
Among 37 366 infant pairs in 2023 and 87 763 pairs in 2024, RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations during the first year of follow-up occurred in 299 (0.8%) immunized vs 899 (2.4%) unimmunized infants in 2023 and 456 (0.5%) vs 1711 (1.9%), respectively, in 2024. Nirsevimab was associated with a lower rate of RSV-LRTI-related hospitalization in both the 2023 and 2024 cohort during the first year of follow-up, with effectiveness estimates of 66% (95% CI, 61%-70%) and 72% (95% CI, 69%-75%), respectively. An association with higher rates of hospitalization for ENT bacterial infections was observed (2023 cohort: weighted hazard ratio [wHR], 1.36; 95% CI, 1.00-1.84; 2024 cohort: wHR, 1.43; 95% CI, 1.15-1.78).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that nirsevimab was associated with a substantial reduction in RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations during the first year across both the 2023-2024 and 2024-2025 seasons. However, they noted that it was not associated with reduced hospitalizations due to non-RSV infections or asthma, nor during the second year of follow-up.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nirsevimab may provide significant protection against RSV-LRTI-related hospitalizations in the first year after immunization, which could inform future vaccination strategies for RSV in infants. However, the lack of protective association during the second year and with non-RSV infections or asthma indicates the need for further research to understand long-term effects and broader implications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on observational data from a national health data system, which may introduce bias or confounding factors. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the absence of randomized controlled trials and potential limitations in data collection and follow-up.
