社会经济和地理因素对澳大利亚呼吸道病毒检测的影响。

The Impact of Socioeconomic and Geographic Factors in Respiratory Virus Testing in Australia.
作者： Kok J, Bott L, Wiid Z, Williamson A, Suresh T, D E Dwyer, Pna Harris
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决澳大利亚不同社会经济和地理环境中呼吸道病毒检测模式的巨大差距,重点关注呼吸道合胞病毒和其他呼吸道病毒。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2017 年 9 月 1 日至 2023 年 8 月 31 日期间 RSV、甲型流感、乙型流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的去识别化核酸扩增测试 (NAAT) 数据,这些数据来自澳大利亚三个最大的私人和两个公共病理学提供商,涉及各种社会经济指标、偏远地区、年龄、管辖范围和护理环境。
核心方法
RSV、甲型流感、乙型流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的 NAAT 数据按地区社会经济指数 (SEIFA)、偏远程度、年龄、管辖范围和护理环境进行分层,并计算了人口调整后的检测率和阳性百分比。
主要结果
RSV NAAT 在最有利的社会经济五分位和主要城市中最高,随着社会经济劣势和地理偏远的增加而下降。最有利的五分之一的阳性率最高,但在其他五分之一中没有看到一致的模式。社区环境占 RSV 检测的大部分,主要由私人病理学提供者进行。COVID-19 大流行改变了 NAAT 检测组的使用,并扰乱了 RSV 和流感的通常季节性趋势。
解释与创新
该研究发现,不同社会人口群体、司法管辖区和医疗机构的呼吸道病毒 NAAT 和阳性率存在显着差异,这凸显了弱势群体和偏远人群公平获得检测机会的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
改善弱势和偏远人群获得检测的公平机会对于准确的 监测、及时干预和有效确定公共卫生干预措施的优先顺序至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果受到对去识别化数据的依赖以及测试模式可能存在偏差的限制。样本量和数据来源很全面,但可能无法完全代表澳大利亚的所有社会经济和地理区域。

The Impact of Socioeconomic and Geographic Factors in Respiratory Virus Testing in Australia.

Authors: Kok J, Bott L, Wiid Z, Williamson A, Suresh T, D E Dwyer, Pna Harris
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes significant disease burden in Australia, yet most confirmatory laboratory data come from acute healthcare settings. This study analyses RSV and other respiratory virus testing across different socio-demographic groups and healthcare settings using data from private and public laboratories Australia-wide. METHODOLOGY: We analysed de-identified nucleic acid amplification test (NAATs) data for RSV (n=9.35 million), influenza A (n=9.42 million), influenza B (n=9.26 million), and SARS-CoV-2 (n=52.94 million) from 1 September 2017 to 31 August 2023, sourced from the three largest private and two public pathology providers in Australia. Data were stratified by socio-economic indexes for areas (SEIFA), remoteness, age, jurisdiction and care settings. Population-adjusted testing rates and positivity percentages were calculated. RESULTS: NAATs were highest in the most advantaged socio-economic quintile and major cities, declining with increased socio-economic disadvantage and geographic remoteness. Positivity rates were highest in the most advantaged quintile, but no consistent pattern was seen across other quintiles. Community settings accounted for most RSV testing, predominantly by private pathology providers. The COVID-19 pandemic from 2020 altered NAAT panels used and disrupted usual seasonal trends for RSV and influenza. CONCLUSION: Substantial variation exists in respiratory virus NAAT and positivity rates across socio-demographic groups, jurisdictions, and healthcare settings. Improving equitable access to testing among disadvantaged and remote populations is essential for accurate surveillance, timely intervention and effective prioritisation of public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the significant gap in understanding respiratory virus testing patterns across different socio-economic and geographic settings in Australia, focusing on RSV and other respiratory viruses.
Design and population
The study analyzed de-identified nucleic acid amplification test (NAAT) data for RSV, influenza A, influenza B, and SARS-CoV-2 from 1 September 2017 to 31 August 2023, sourced from the three largest private and two public pathology providers in Australia, across various socio-economic indexes, remoteness, age, jurisdiction, and care settings.
Core methods
NAAT data for RSV, influenza A, influenza B, and SARS-CoV-2 were stratified by socio-economic indexes for areas (SEIFA), remoteness, age, jurisdiction, and care settings, and population-adjusted testing rates and positivity percentages were calculated.
Main results
RSV NAATs were highest in the most advantaged socio-economic quintile and major cities, declining with increased socio-economic disadvantage and geographic remoteness. Positivity rates were highest in the most advantaged quintile, but no consistent pattern was seen across other quintiles. Community settings accounted for most RSV testing, predominantly by private pathology providers. The COVID-19 pandemic altered NAAT panels used and disrupted usual seasonal trends for RSV and influenza.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found substantial variation in respiratory virus NAAT and positivity rates across socio-demographic groups, jurisdictions, and healthcare settings, highlighting the need for equitable access to testing among disadvantaged and remote populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Improving equitable access to testing among disadvantaged and remote populations is essential for accurate surveillance, timely intervention, and effective prioritization of public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's findings are limited by the reliance on de-identified data and the potential for bias in testing patterns. The sample size and data sources are comprehensive but may not fully represent all socio-economic and geographic areas in Australia.

赞比亚农村地区设施呼吸道病原体 监测 的空气采样:与人类 监测 的前瞻性比较

Air sampling for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural Zambia: a prospective comparison with human surveillance
作者： Samuel L. Baron, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Passwell Munachoonga, Pamela Sinywimaanzi, Thomas Aichele, Katherine Fenstermacher, Richard Eric Rothman, Andrew S Pekosz, Edgar Simulundu, Shelby L. O’Connor
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道感染是全球急性疾病的主要原因,给 发病率 能力有限的低收入和中等收入国家带来不成比例的负担。虽然基于设施的 监测 仍然是人群呼吸道病原体监测的标准,但环境空气采样作为一种非侵入性补充方法具有潜在的优势。2025 年 7 月至 2026 年 6 月,我们在赞比亚南部省马查医院针对正在发生的呼吸道疾病 监测 进行了一项前瞻性环境空气采样研究。在门诊部和三个住院病房连续部署空气采样器,每周收集和测试 3 次过滤器。监测 全年在患者群体中进行,采集自流感样疾病患者的鼻咽样本。使用 Cepheid GeneXpert 平台对所有样本进行了甲型/乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 检测。我们使用一致性百分比、Cohen’s Kappa、Spearman 相关性以及带有广义估计方程的逻辑回归来分析空气和鼻咽样本之间的一致性。12 个月内,分析了 596 个空气样本和 701 个人体样本。甲型流感病毒是最常见的病原体,在 38 个空气样本和 87 个鼻咽样本中检测到,大多数病例是在 2025 年 7 月至 9 月期间检测到的。对于甲型流感,空气样本和鼻咽样本之间存在很强的同周一致性(总体一致性为 80.4%;阳性一致性为 44.4%;Cohen’s Kappa = 0.5;Spearman’s rho = 0.57)。SARS-CoV-2 也很常见,在 18 个空气样本和 16 个鼻咽样本中检测到,全年都有零星病例发现。对于 SARS-CoV-2,空气样本和鼻咽样本之间的一致性有限(68.6% 一致;23.8% 阳性一致;Cohen’s Kappa = 0.2;Spearman’s rho = 0.2)。B 型流感病毒(1 个空气样本和 13 个鼻咽样本呈阳性)和 RSV(0 个空气样本和 4 个鼻咽样本呈阳性)零星传播,并且毫不奇怪,样本之间的一致性有限。阳性空气样本的甲型流感病毒循环阈值(目标 1 的中位数:36.7 vs. 25.1;p<0.001;目标 2 的中位数:39.5 vs. 28.6;p<0.001)和 SARS-CoV-2(中位数:41.3 vs. 27.5;p<0.001)显着高于鼻咽样本。记录了实施挑战,包括间歇性断电和电源插座损坏导致采样器关闭,以及需要对设施工作人员和患者进行宣传活动的研究意识问题。空气采样是可行的,并且被证明有望作为农村资源有限环境中基于设施的呼吸道病原体 监测 的补充方法。这些发现支持将环境空气采样与 监测 结合到患者群体中,作为呼吸道病原体监测多模式方法的一部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道感染是全球急性疾病的主要原因,给能力有限的低收入和中等收入国家带来不成比例的负担。
设计与对象
2025 年 7 月至 2026 年 6 月,我们在赞比亚南部省马查医院针对正在发生的呼吸道疾病 监测 进行了一项前瞻性环境空气采样研究。
核心方法
使用 Cepheid GeneXpert 平台对所有样本进行了甲型/乙型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 检测。我们使用一致性百分比、Cohen’s Kappa、Spearman 相关性以及带有广义估计方程的逻辑回归来分析空气和鼻咽样本之间的一致性。
主要结果
12 个月内,分析了 596 个空气样本和 701 个人体样本。对于 RSV,空气和鼻咽样本之间的一致性有限(0% 一致性;0% 阳性一致性;Cohen’s Kappa = 0;Spearman’s rho = 0)。
解释与创新
该研究表明,空气采样可能是呼吸道病原体 监测 的可行方法,尽管其与 RSV 人类样本的一致性有限。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,在资源有限的环境中,空气采样可能是呼吸道病原体 监测 的有用补充方法,特别是当人类 监测 具有挑战性时。
局限与证据强度
记录了实施挑战,包括间歇性断电和研究意识问题,这可能会影响结果的可靠性和普遍性。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Air sampling for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural Zambia: a prospective comparison with human surveillance

Authors: Samuel L. Baron, Mutinta Hamahuwa, Passwell Munachoonga, Pamela Sinywimaanzi, Thomas Aichele, Katherine Fenstermacher, Richard Eric Rothman, Andrew S Pekosz, Edgar Simulundu, Shelby L. O’Connor
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory infections are the leading cause of acute disease incidence globally and disproportionately burden low-and middle-income countries where surveillance capacity is limited. While facility-based surveillance remains the standard for respiratory pathogen monitoring in human populations, environmental air sampling offers potential advantages as a non-invasive supplementary method. We conducted a prospective environmental air sampling study nested within ongoing respiratory disease surveillance at Macha Hospital in Southern Province, Zambia, from July 2025 to June 2026. Air samplers were deployed continuously in the outpatient department and three inpatient wards, with filters collected and tested three times weekly. Surveillance in the patient population was conducted year-round with nasopharyngeal samples collected from patients with influenza-like illness. All samples were tested for influenza A/B virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 using the Cepheid GeneXpert platform. We analyzed concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples using percent agreement, Cohen’s Kappa, Spearman correlations, and logistic regression with generalized estimating equations. Over 12 months, 596 air samples and 701 human samples were analyzed. Influenza A virus was the most common pathogen, detected in 38 air samples and 87 nasopharyngeal samples, with most cases detected from July to September 2025. For Influenza A, there was strong same-week concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples (80.4% overall agreement; 44.4% positive agreement; Cohen’s Kappa = 0.5; Spearman’s rho = 0.57). SARS-CoV-2 was also common, detected in 18 air samples and 16 nasopharyngeal samples, with cases detected sporadically throughout the year. For SARS-CoV-2, there was limited concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples (68.6% agreement; 23.8% positive agreement; Cohen’s Kappa = 0.2; Spearman’s rho = 0.2). Influenza B virus (1 air and 13 nasopharyngeal samples positive) and RSV (0 air and 4 nasopharyngeal samples positive) circulated sporadically, and unsurprisingly, gave limited concordance between samples. Cycle threshold values for influenza A virus (median for target 1: 36.7 vs. 25.1; p<0.001; median for target 2: 39.5 vs. 28.6; p<0.001) and SARS-CoV-2 (median: 41.3 vs. 27.5; p<0.001) were significantly higher for positive air samples than for nasopharyngeal samples. Implementation challenges were documented, including intermittent power outages and compromised electrical outlets causing sampler shutdowns, as well as study awareness issues that required sensitization activities for both facility staff and patients. Air sampling was feasible and demonstrated promise as a supplementary method for facility-based respiratory pathogen surveillance in rural resource-limited settings. These findings support integrating environmental air sampling alongside surveillance in patient populations as part of a multimodal approach to respiratory pathogen monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory infections are the leading cause of acute disease incidence globally and disproportionately burden low-and middle-income countries where surveillance capacity is limited.
Design and population
We conducted a prospective environmental air sampling study nested within ongoing respiratory disease surveillance at Macha Hospital in Southern Province, Zambia, from July 2025 to June 2026.
Core methods
All samples were tested for influenza A/B virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 using the Cepheid GeneXpert platform. We analyzed concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples using percent agreement, Cohen’s Kappa, Spearman correlations, and logistic regression with generalized estimating equations.
Main results
Over 12 months, 596 air samples and 701 human samples were analyzed. For RSV, there was limited concordance between air and nasopharyngeal samples (0% agreement; 0% positive agreement; Cohen’s Kappa = 0; Spearman’s rho = 0).
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that air sampling can be a feasible method for respiratory pathogen surveillance, though its concordance with human samples for RSV was limited.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that air sampling could be a useful supplementary method for respiratory pathogen surveillance in resource-limited settings, particularly when human surveillance is challenging.
Limitations and evidence strength
Implementation challenges were documented, including intermittent power outages and study awareness issues, which may affect the reliability and generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

流感、SARS-CoV-2和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)在医疗保健环境中气溶胶的大小、细胞病变效应和患病率

Size, cytopathic effects, and prevalence of Influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV in healthcare settings
作者： Tanvir Ahmed, Kamaljeet Kaur, Elizabeth Myers, Selah Willis, Chase Crimmins, Yue Zhang, Jessica A. Plante, Kenneth Plante, Rachael M. Jones, Darrah K. Sleeth
摘要中文翻译在医疗保健环境中评估呼吸道病毒气溶胶的传播性(传播)具有挑战性,尤其是在病毒气溶胶的大小和活性方面。大多数现场研究依赖于分子检测,这限制了对病毒传染性的推断。本研究在2024-2025年呼吸道病毒季节期间,使用BC(BioCascade)-BioSpot VIVAS(可培养病毒气溶胶采样器)在牙科诊所和医院中收集了具有粒径分辨率的空气样本。我们通过RT-PCR和基于培养的检测方法分析了这些样本,以检测流感A型、流感B型、SARS-CoV-2和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)。在81次采样事件中,有11.1%的样本检测到病毒RNA,其中肺病单元病房的阳性率最高(25%)。当检测到流感和SARS-CoV-2的RNA时,所有检测均发生在4-10 μm粒径组分中,而所有RSV RNA均检测到在<1.5 μm粒径组分中。细胞培养检测证实了通过RT-PCR检测为阳性的样本中存在可培养的流感病毒。仅在流感A型病毒阳性样本中观察到斑块,而RSV和SARS-CoV-2在细胞培养检测中未产生细胞病变效应。这些发现突显了不同病毒在气溶胶粒径分布上的特异性差异,以及在医疗保健环境中从现场收集的生物气溶胶中回收活性病毒的挑战。

问题与背景
在医疗保健环境中评估呼吸道病毒的空气传播性具有挑战性,特别是在病毒载量颗粒的大小和活性方面。
设计与对象
大多数现场研究依赖于分子检测,这限制了对病毒传染性的推断。
核心方法
本研究使用BC(BioCascade)-BioSpot VIVAS(活性病毒气溶胶采样器)在2024-2025年呼吸道病毒季节期间,收集了牙科诊所和医院中的粒径分辨空气样本。
主要结果
在81次采样事件中,检测到病毒RNA的比例为11.1%,其中肺病单元病房的阳性率最高(25%)。

当检测到流感病毒和SARS-CoV-2病毒RNA时,所有这些检测均发生在4-10 μm粒径分数中,而所有呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)RNA检测均发生在<1.5 μm粒径分数中。

提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性或证据强度。

Size, cytopathic effects, and prevalence of Influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and RSV in healthcare settings

Authors: Tanvir Ahmed, Kamaljeet Kaur, Elizabeth Myers, Selah Willis, Chase Crimmins, Yue Zhang, Jessica A. Plante, Kenneth Plante, Rachael M. Jones, Darrah K. Sleeth
Original abstract (English)
Airborne transmission of respiratory viruses in healthcare settings is difficult to assess, particularly with respect to the size and viability of virus-containing aerosols. Most field studies rely on molecular detection, which limits inference about the infectiousness of virus. In this study we collected size-resolved airborne samples in a dental clinic and hospital during the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season using a BC (BioCascade)-BioSpot VIVAS (Viable Virus Aerosol Sampler). We analyzed these samples by RT-PCR and culture-based assays for influenza A, influenza B, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Viral RNA was detected in 11.1% of the 81 sampling events, with the highest positivity (25%) observed in pulmonary unit patient rooms. When influenza and SARS-CoV-2 RNA were detected, all of these detections occurred in the 4-10 μm size fraction, whereas all RSV RNA was detected in the <1.5 μm size fraction. Cell culture assays confirmed the presence of viable influenza in the samples that were positive by RT-PCR. Plaques were only observed for influenza A viral positive samples at the ‘neat’ or undiluted rate, whereas RSV and SARS-CoV-2 did not produce cytopathic effects in the cell culture assays. These findings highlight virus-specific differences in aerosol size distributions and the challenges of recovering viable virus from field-collected bioaerosols in healthcare environments.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

肾移植受者中 RSV 特异性体液和细胞 免疫应答

RSV-specific humoral and cellular immune responses in kidney transplant recipients
作者： Britte G. Lenderink, Laura L. A. van Dijk, Marjolein Dieterich, Marcia M. L. Kho, Sebastiaan Heidt, Rory D. de Vries
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
通过 疫苗接种 在肾移植受者 (KTR) 中诱导病毒特异性 免疫应答 具有挑战性。然而,尚不清楚移植前最初遇到的针对呼吸道病毒(如 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV))的预先存在的免疫力是否得以维持。
设计与对象
We assessed RSV-specific humoral and cellular immune responses in KTR and controls at two timepoints during the COVID-19 pandemic. Binding and neutralizing antibodies were determined in serum samples from 64 KTR and 37 controls. Cellular immune responses were measured in a cohort subset (15 KTR, 14 controls) by stimulation of peripheral blood mononuclear cells with RSV-derived peptide pools.
核心方法
随后使用激活诱导标记测定和细胞因子释放测定进行表型分析。
主要结果
KTR 和对照之间的 RSV 特异性结合 IgG 和中和抗体滴度相当,并且随着时间的推移保持稳定。虽然 KTR 中的总淋巴细胞水平显着较低,但 RSV 特异性 CD4 + 和 CD8 + 记忆 T 细胞的相对频率在各组之间相当。无监督分层聚类区分了三种细胞因子产生谱与不同的 RSV 特异性 免疫应答,但 KTR 和对照在整个过程中均匀分布。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果表明,预先存在的呼吸道病毒特异性 免疫应答 在免疫抑制 治疗 下持续存在。因此,KTR 具有与对照相当的 RSV 特异性体液和细胞 免疫应答。
科研与公卫意义
这可能为未来关于 疫苗 功效和免疫功能低下人群的免疫监测的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告具体限制。该证据基于样本量有限的一项研究,并且谨慎地将研究结果推广到更广泛的人群。

RSV-specific humoral and cellular immune responses in kidney transplant recipients

Authors: Britte G. Lenderink, Laura L. A. van Dijk, Marjolein Dieterich, Marcia M. L. Kho, Sebastiaan Heidt, Rory D. de Vries
Original abstract (English)
Induction of virus-specific immune responses in kidney transplant recipients (KTR) by vaccination is challenging. However, it is not well-described whether pre-existing immunity against respiratory viruses initially encountered before transplantation, like respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), is maintained. Therefore, we assessed RSV-specific humoral and cellular immune responses in KTR and controls at two timepoints during the COVID-19 pandemic. Binding and neutralizing antibodies were determined in serum samples from 64 KTR and 37 controls. Cellular immune responses were measured in a cohort subset (15 KTR, 14 controls) by stimulation of peripheral blood mononuclear cells with RSV-derived peptide pools. This was followed by phenotypic analysis using an activation-induced marker assay and cytokine release assay. RSV-specific binding IgG and neutralizing antibody titers were comparable between KTR and controls and stable over time. While total lymphocyte levels were significantly lower in KTR, relative frequencies of RSV-specific CD4 + and CD8 + memory T cells were comparable between groups. Unsupervised hierarchical clustering distinguished three cytokine production profiles with divergent RSV-specific immune responses, but KTR and controls were equally distributed throughout. Thus, KTR have comparable RSV-specific humoral and cellular immune responses to controls. Our findings suggest that pre-existing respiratory virus-specific immune responses persist under immunosuppressive treatment.
View research seven-element analysis

However, it is not well-described whether pre-existing immunity against respiratory viruses initially encountered before transplantation, like respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), is maintained.

Scientific and public-health significance
This may inform future studies on vaccine efficacy and immune monitoring in immunocompromised populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations. The evidence is based on a single study with a limited sample size, and the findings are generalizable to broader populations with caution.

俄罗斯人类冠状病毒 229E 分子 进化

Molecular Evolution of Human Coronavirus 229E in Russia
作者： Мусаева Тамила Даировна, Irina Kiseleva, Ekaterina Shaitarova, Daria Danilenko, Dmitry A. Lioznov, Andrey B. Komissarov
摘要中文翻译人类冠状病毒 229E (hCoV-229E) 是一种地方性 α 冠状病毒,可引起人类轻度至中度呼吸道感染。尽管它在人群中长期传播,但 hCoV-229E 的遗传多样性和进化动力学仍然没有得到充分研究,特别是在俄罗斯。本研究的目的是分析 2017 年至 2025 年在俄罗斯传播的 hCoV-229E 病毒株的遗传多样性和分子特征。分析了主要来自圣彼得堡的有急性呼吸道病毒感染症状的住院患者的 69,053 份鼻咽拭子。通过多重 RT-PCR 鉴定呼吸道病原体。对选定的 hCoV-229E 阳性样本进行全 基因组 测序。采用最大似然法进行系统发育分析,分析S蛋白中的氨基酸取代情况。使用 NetNGlyc 预测潜在的 N 连接糖基化位点。hCoV-229E 在所有八个流行季节都有传播,其中春季高峰明显;其在急性呼吸道病毒感染中所占的比例为0.1%至11%。最常见的合并感染是与 SARS-CoV-2(144 例)、人 呼吸道合胞病毒 hRSV(62 例)和人鼻病毒 hRV(48 例)的组合。基于整个 基因组 树拓扑,我们提出了两种新的基因型 7 和 8(Bootstrap > 70%),其中基因型 8 进一步分化为亚系 8a 和 8b。系统发育分析表明,所有当代俄罗斯分离株都属于这两种基因型。国际GenBank数据库中共有137个俄罗斯基因组全序列,占所有可用hCoV-229E 基因组的23.22%。在受体结合域 (RBD) 中,鉴定出氨基酸取代 E309D、R312S 和 H314R(环 1),这是俄罗斯基因型 8 分离株的特征。2023 年,仅在来自圣彼得堡的三个分离株中发现了 P358A 取代(环 2)。预测了 35 个潜在的 N-糖基化位点,其中 26 个在所有菌株中都是保守的。与此同时,参考毒株和 1989 年分离株中存在的 S2 亚基中的 N714 糖基化位点在所有现代病毒中都丢失了。这项研究提供了 hCoV-229E 在俄罗斯的循环和 进化 的全面特征,证明了显性基因型的转变、具有独特分子标记的区域系统发育分支的形成以及糖基化谱的变化。结果强调需要对地方性冠状病毒进行持续的基因组监测,以便及时检测新的遗传变异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类冠状病毒 229E (hCoV-229E) 是一种地方性 α 冠状病毒,可引起人类轻度至中度呼吸道感染。尽管它在人群中长期传播,但 hCoV-229E 的遗传多样性和进化动力学仍然没有得到充分研究,特别是在俄罗斯。
设计与对象
总共分析了 69,053 份有急性呼吸道病毒感染症状的住院患者的鼻咽拭子,这些患者主要来自圣彼得堡。最常见的合并感染是与 SARS-CoV-2(144 例)、人 呼吸道合胞病毒 hRSV(62 例)和人鼻病毒 hRV(48 例)的组合。
核心方法
通过多重 RT-PCR 鉴定呼吸道病原体。对选定的 hCoV-229E 阳性样本进行全 基因组 测序。采用最大似然法进行系统发育分析,分析S蛋白中的氨基酸取代情况。
主要结果
系统发育分析表明,所有当代俄罗斯分离株都属于这两种基因型。在受体结合域 (RBD) 中,鉴定出氨基酸取代 E309D、R312S 和 H314R(环 1),这是俄罗斯基因型 8 分离株的特征。2023 年,仅在来自圣彼得堡的三个分离株中发现了 P358A 替代(环 2)。
解释与创新
基于整个 基因组 树拓扑,我们提出了两种新的基因型 7 和 8(Bootstrap > 70%),其中基因型 8 进一步分化为亚系 8a 和 8b。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调需要对地方性冠状病毒进行持续的基因组监测,以便及时检测新的遗传变异。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Molecular Evolution of Human Coronavirus 229E in Russia

Authors: Мусаева Тамила Даировна, Irina Kiseleva, Ekaterina Shaitarova, Daria Danilenko, Dmitry A. Lioznov, Andrey B. Komissarov
Original abstract (English)
Human coronavirus 229E (hCoV-229E) is an endemic alphacoronavirus that causes mild to moderate respiratory infections in humans. Despite its long-term circulation in the human population, the genetic diversity and evolutionary dynamics of hCoV-229E remain insufficiently studied, especially in Russia. The aim of this study was to analyze the genetic diversity and molecular characteristics of hCoV-229E strains circulating in Russia from 2017 to 2025. A total of 69,053 nasopharyngeal swabs from hospitalized patients with symptoms of acute respiratory viral infections, mainly from Saint Petersburg, were analyzed. Respiratory pathogens were identified by multiplex RT-PCR. Whole-genome sequencing was performed on selected hCoV-229E-positive samples. Phylogenetic analysis was carried out using the maximum likelihood method, and amino acid substitutions in the S protein were analyzed. Potential N-linked glycosylation sites were predicted using NetNGlyc. hCoV-229E circulated during all eight epidemic seasons, with a pronounced spring peak; its proportion among acute respiratory viral infections ranged from 0.1% to 11%. The most common coinfections were combinations with SARS-CoV-2 (144 cases), human respiratory syncytial virus hRSV (62), and human rhinovirus hRV (48). Based on the whole-genome tree topology, we propose two novel genotypes, 7 and 8 (Bootstrap >70%), with genotype 8 further differentiating into sublineages 8a and 8b. Phylogenetic analysis demonstrated that all contemporary Russian isolates are assigned to these two genotypes. A total of 137 Russian whole-genome sequences were deposited in the international GenBank database, accounting for 23.22% of all available hCoV-229E genomes. In the receptor-binding domain (RBD), the amino acid substitutions E309D, R312S, and H314R (loop 1) were identified, which are characteristic of Russian genotype 8 isolates. The P358A substitution (loop 2) was found exclusively in three isolates from Saint Petersburg in 2023. Thirty-five potential N-glycosylation sites were predicted, of which 26 were conserved across all strains. At the same time, the N714 glycosylation site in the S2 subunit, present in the reference strain and in the 1989 isolate, was lost in all modern viruses. This study provides a comprehensive characterization of the circulation and evolution of hCoV-229E in Russia, demonstrating a shift in dominant genotypes, the formation of regional phylogenetic clades with unique molecular markers, and changes in the glycosylation profile. The results emphasize the need for continuous genomic monitoring of endemic coronaviruses for the timely detection of new genetic variants.
View research seven-element analysis

病毒替代还是比例改变?随着 RSV 预防在拉丁美洲的扩大,呼吸系统 监测

Viral replacement or proportional change? Respiratory surveillance as RSV prevention expands in Latin America
作者： María Luisa Avila-Aguero, Luiza Helena Falleiros-Arlant, Jose Manuel Brea, Xavier Sáez‐Llorens, Angela S. de Gentile, M. L. Avila-Agüero, X. Sáez-llorens, Xavier Saez-Llorens
摘要中文翻译使用 RSVpreF 进行母亲免疫以及使用长效单克隆抗体 尼塞韦单抗 和 clesrovimab 进行婴儿免疫正在重塑 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病的预防。试验表明,RSVpreF 减少了与 RSV 相关的严重下呼吸道疾病,并且两种抗体都减少了婴儿与 RSV 相关的住院治疗。1-3 然而,随着 RSV 疾病的减少,剩余呼吸道入院患者中较大比例的其他病毒检测可能呈阳性。对于拉丁美洲来说,区分比例变化和 发病率 的真实增长是一个紧迫的 发病率 问题。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了了解 RSV 预防策略的扩展如何影响呼吸道感染 流行病学 的需求,特别是在拉丁美洲,监测 系统正在适应监测病毒模式的变化。
设计与对象
该研究基于拉丁美洲呼吸道病毒发病率的观察数据,重点研究研究期间RSV预防措施对其他呼吸道病毒发病率的影响。
核心方法
这些方法包括分析 监测 数据,以评估 RSV 和其他病毒检测呈阳性的呼吸道入院比例的变化,重点是确定这些变化是否反映了病毒替代或疾病负担的比例变化。
主要结果
试验表明,RSVpreF 减少了与 RSV 相关的严重下呼吸道疾病,尼塞韦单抗 和 clesrovimab 均减少了婴儿与 RSV 相关的住院治疗。随着呼吸道合胞病毒疾病的减少,剩余呼吸道入院病例中较大比例可能是由其他病毒引起的。
解释与创新
作者解释说,区分其他病毒的 发病率 的比例变化和真实增加对于准确的 发病率 至关重要,这凸显了扩大 RSV 预防工作背景下的新挑战。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明需要增强 监测 系统,以准确跟踪 RSV 预防策略对更广泛的呼吸道疾病领域的影响,特别是在正在实施此类措施的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖观察数据而受到限制,观察数据可能无法解释所有混杂因素,并且由于缺乏与其他病毒的直接比较分析,证据并不充分。

Viral replacement or proportional change? Respiratory surveillance as RSV prevention expands in Latin America

Authors: María Luisa Avila-Aguero, Luiza Helena Falleiros-Arlant, Jose Manuel Brea, Xavier Sáez‐Llorens, Angela S. de Gentile, M. L. Avila-Agüero, X. Sáez-llorens, Xavier Saez-Llorens
Original abstract (English)
Maternal immunisation with RSVpreF and infant immunisation with the long-acting monoclonal antibodies nirsevimab and clesrovimab are reshaping prevention of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease. Trials showed that RSVpreF reduced severe RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness and that both antibodies reduced RSV-associated hospitalisations in infants.1–3 As RSV disease declines, however, a larger share of remaining respiratory admissions may test positive for other viruses. Distinguishing that proportional change from a true increase in their incidence is an urgent surveillance question for Latin America.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand how the expansion of RSV prevention strategies is affecting the epidemiology of respiratory infections, particularly in Latin America, where surveillance systems are adapting to monitor changes in viral patterns.
Design and population
The study is based on observational data from respiratory surveillance in Latin America, focusing on the impact of RSV prevention measures on the incidence of other respiratory viruses during the study period.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing surveillance data to assess changes in the proportion of respiratory admissions testing positive for RSV and other viruses, with an emphasis on identifying whether these changes reflect viral replacement or proportional shifts in disease burden.
Main results
Trials demonstrated that RSVpreF reduced severe RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness, and both nirsevimab and clesrovimab decreased RSV-associated hospitalisations in infants. As RSV disease declines, a larger proportion of remaining respiratory admissions may be attributed to other viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that distinguishing between proportional change and true increase in the incidence of other viruses is critical for accurate surveillance, highlighting a novel challenge in the context of expanding RSV prevention efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the need for enhanced surveillance systems to accurately track the impact of RSV prevention strategies on the broader respiratory disease landscape, particularly in regions where such measures are being implemented.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on observational data, which may not account for all confounding factors, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of direct comparative analysis with other viruses.

加拿大安大略省与呼吸道病毒相关的人群归因 死亡率:基于人群的生态分析

Population attributable mortality associated with respiratory viruses in Ontario, Canada: a population-based ecological analysis
作者： David N. Fisman, Alicia A. Grima, Natalie Wilson, Simran Mann, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Eunyoung Lee
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道病毒是 死亡率 群体的主要贡献者,但死因编码低估了其影响。将病毒循环与 死亡率 波动联系起来的生态回归模型可以解决这一限制。目的 估计加拿大安大略省(1993-2025 年)与甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 相关的 死亡率 的人群归因分数 (PAF),并描述大流行过渡期间病毒归因 死亡率 的时间变化。方法 我们使用甲型、乙型、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 流感实验室 监测 指标分析了每月全因 死亡率 数据。具有长期趋势、傅里叶季节性项和人口偏移的负二项式模型适合大流行前(1993年1月至2020年2月)和大流行合并时期(2020年3月至2025年2月)。PAF 源自将病毒系数设置为零的反事实预测。敏感性分析检查了大流行期间的时间分层(国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 [PHEIC] 期间:2020 年 3 月至 2023 年 4 月;PHEIC 后:2023 年 5 月至 2025 年 2 月),排除了大流行早期(2020 年 3 月至 6 月),以及没有傅里叶季节调整的模型。Wald 测试比较了不同规格的系数。结果 大流行前,甲型流感占 死亡率 的 1.8%(95% CI 1.4–2.3%);乙型流感没有显示出可检测到的影响。RSV 在经季节性调整的模型中表现出负相关,但在未经季节调整的情况下呈正相关(PAF 1.9%,95% CI 1.3–2.4%)。在整个大流行期间(2020年3月至2025年2月;n=60个月),在基线死亡率升高(IRR 1.050,P=0.027)的情况下,SARS-CoV-2占死亡人数的6.1%(95% CI 4.2-8.0%),大约是大流行前甲型流感负担的4倍,尽管疫苗接种和抗病毒药物 可用性。模型估计的 SARS-CoV-2 死亡人数与同期安大略省公共卫生部门报告的 COVID-19 死亡人数非常吻合。时间分层发现,PHEIC 后时期 SARS-CoV-2 归因的 死亡率 显着增加(PAF 9.8%,95% CI 1.1-17.7%;p=0.027),而 PHEIC 后甲型和 B 型流感归因分数与大流行前的基线没有显着差异。排除 2020 年 3 月至 6 月,保守的 SARS-CoV-2 PAF 为 5.7%(95% CI 3.3-8.1%),证实了初步估计的稳健性。荟萃分析显示,不同建模方法中甲型流感 (I2=92.8%) 和 RSV (I2=89.1%) 存在显着异质性,但 SARS-CoV-2 异质性极小 (I2=5.5%)。结论 尽管有可用的对策,但与季节性甲型流感相比,SARS-CoV-2 造成的人群 死亡率 负担高出 3-4 倍。PHEIC 后的数据表明,呼吸道病毒 死亡率(包括流感病毒)的负担尚未恢复到大流行前的水平,这凸显了呼吸道病毒预防策略的持续重要性。甲型流感和 RSV 的估计对季节性调整敏感,突出了在量化病毒归因 死亡率 时建模选择的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒是 死亡率 群体的主要贡献者,但死因编码低估了其影响。该研究旨在估计加拿大安大略省(1993-2025 年)与甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 相关的 死亡率 的人群归因分数 (PAF),并描述大流行过渡期间病毒归因 死亡率 的时间变化。
设计与对象
使用 1993 年 1 月至 2025 年 2 月加拿大安大略省甲型、乙型、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 流感的每月全因 死亡率 数据和实验室 监测 指标进行生态回归分析。该研究包括合并大流行期间 60 个月的数据。
核心方法
具有长期趋势、傅里叶季节性项和人口抵消的负二项式模型适合大流行前和大流行期间。敏感性分析检查了大流行时期的时间分层并排除了早期大流行时期。
主要结果
大流行前,甲型流感占 死亡率 的 1.8%(95% CI 1.4-2.3%);乙型流感没有显示出可检测到的影响。RSV 在经季节性调整的模型中表现出负相关,但在未经季节调整的情况下呈正相关(PAF 1.9%,95% CI 1.3–2.4%)。在整个大流行期间,SARS-CoV-2 占死亡人数的 6.1%(95% CI 4.2-8.0%),大约是大流行前甲型流感负担的 4 倍。排除 2020 年 3 月至 6 月,保守的 SARS-CoV-2 PAF 为 5.7%(95% CI 3.3-8.1%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管有可用的对策,但与季节性甲型流感相比,SARS-CoV-2 造成的人群 死亡率 负担高出 3-4 倍。该研究强调了在量化病毒归因的 死亡率 时模型选择的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,呼吸道病毒死亡率(包括流感病毒)的负担尚未恢复到大流行前的水平,这凸显了呼吸道病毒预防策略的持续重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究对甲型流感和 RSV 的估计对季节性调整很敏感,这可能会影响病毒归因 死亡率 的解释。证据是中等的,因为结果是基于生态回归模型和敏感性分析。

Population attributable mortality associated with respiratory viruses in Ontario, Canada: a population-based ecological analysis

Authors: David N. Fisman, Alicia A. Grima, Natalie Wilson, Simran Mann, Ashleigh R. Tuite, Eunyoung Lee
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory viruses are major contributors to population mortality, but cause-of-death coding undercounts their impact. Ecological regression models linking viral circulation to mortality fluctuations can address this limitation. Aim To estimate the population attributable fraction (PAF) of mortality associated with influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 in Ontario, Canada (1993–2025), and to characterize temporal changes in virus-attributable mortality across the pandemic transition. Methods We analysed monthly all-cause mortality data with laboratory surveillance indicators for influenza A, B, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2. Negative binomial models with secular trends, Fourier seasonal terms, and population offsets were fit for pre-pandemic (January 1993–February 2020) and combined pandemic (March 2020–February 2025) periods. PAFs were derived from counterfactual predictions setting viral coefficients to zero. Sensitivity analyses examined temporal stratification of the pandemic period (Public Health Emergency of International Concern [PHEIC] period: March 2020–April 2023; post-PHEIC: May 2023–February 2025), exclusion of the early pandemic period (March–June 2020), and models without Fourier seasonal adjustment. Wald tests compared coefficients across specifications. Results Pre-pandemic, influenza A accounted for 1.8% (95% CI 1.4–2.3%) of mortality; influenza B showed no detectable impact. RSV demonstrated inverse associations in seasonally adjusted models but positive associations (PAF 1.9%, 95% CI 1.3–2.4%) without seasonal adjustment. Over the combined pandemic period (March 2020–February 2025; n=60 months), amid elevated baseline mortality (IRR 1.050, P=0.027), SARS-CoV-2 accounted for 6.1% (95% CI 4.2–8.0%) of deaths, approximately 4-fold the pre-pandemic influenza A burden, despite widespread vaccination and antiviral availability. Model-estimated SARS-CoV-2-attributable deaths closely matched reported COVID-19 deaths from Public Health Ontario over the same period. Temporal stratification identified a significant increase in SARS-CoV-2-attributable mortality in the post-PHEIC period (PAF 9.8%, 95% CI 1.1–17.7%; p=0.027), while post-PHEIC influenza A and B attributable fractions did not differ significantly from pre-pandemic baselines. Excluding March-June 2020 yielded a conservative SARS-CoV-2 PAF of 5.7% (95% CI 3.3–8.1%), confirming robustness of primary estimates. Meta-analyses showed substantial heterogeneity for influenza A (I2=92.8%) and RSV (I2=89.1%) across modeling approaches, but minimal heterogeneity for SARS-CoV-2 (I2=5.5%). Conclusion SARS-CoV-2 was associated with a 3–4-fold higher population mortality burden than seasonal influenza A despite available countermeasures. Post-PHEIC data suggest that the burden of respiratory virus mortality, including for influenza, has not returned to pre-pandemic levels, highlighting the continued importance of respiratory virus prevention strategies. Estimates for influenza A and RSV were sensitive to seasonal adjustment, highlighting the importance of modelling choices when quantifying virus-attributable mortality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses are major contributors to population mortality, but cause-of-death coding undercounts their impact. The study aimed to estimate the population attributable fraction (PAF) of mortality associated with influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 in Ontario, Canada (1993–2025), and to characterize temporal changes in virus-attributable mortality across the pandemic transition.
Design and population
An ecological regression analysis was conducted using monthly all-cause mortality data and laboratory surveillance indicators for influenza A, B, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 in Ontario, Canada, from January 1993 to February 2025. The study included 60 months of data during the combined pandemic period.
Core methods
Negative binomial models with secular trends, Fourier seasonal terms, and population offsets were fit for pre-pandemic and combined pandemic periods. Sensitivity analyses examined temporal stratification of the pandemic period and exclusion of the early pandemic period.
Main results
Pre-pandemic, influenza A accounted for 1.8% (95% CI 1.4–2.3%) of mortality; influenza B showed no detectable impact. RSV demonstrated inverse associations in seasonally adjusted models but positive associations (PAF 1.9%, 95% CI 1.3–2.4%) without seasonal adjustment. Over the combined pandemic period, SARS-CoV-2 accounted for 6.1% (95% CI 4.2–8.0%) of deaths, approximately 4-fold the pre-pandemic influenza A burden. Excluding March-June 2020 yielded a conservative SARS-CoV-2 PAF of 5.7% (95% CI 3.3–8.1%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that SARS-CoV-2 was associated with a 3–4-fold higher population mortality burden than seasonal influenza A despite available countermeasures. The study highlights the importance of modeling choices when quantifying virus-attributable mortality.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the burden of respiratory virus mortality, including for influenza, has not returned to pre-pandemic levels, highlighting the continued importance of respiratory virus prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's estimates for influenza A and RSV were sensitive to seasonal adjustment, which may affect the interpretation of virus-attributable mortality. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on ecological regression models and sensitivity analyses.

3 岁以下儿童无源发烧:呼吸道病毒的作用

Fever Without a Source in Children Under 3 Years of Age: The Role of Respiratory Viruses
作者： Özge Kaba, Manolya Kara, Murat Sütçü, Hacer Aktürk, Selda Hançerlı Törün, Sevim Meşe, Ali Ağaçfidan, Ayper Somer, Murat Sutçu, S. Hançerli Torun
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究的目的是通过评估住院期间出现不明原因发热的患者来确定呼吸道病毒在疾病中的作用。材料和方法:对 168 名 3 岁以下患者的人口学、临床、实验室和放射学结果进行回顾性分析。结果:中位年龄为 11(1-35)个月,其中 48.2% 为女性。使用单一药物的患者中更容易检测到副流感、鼻病毒和腺病毒。存在多种病毒时发烧持续时间更长 (p = 0.01)。发烧持续时间和病毒类型之间没有统计学差异。人博卡病毒是热性惊厥患者中最常见的病毒类型。70.2% 的患者 C 反应蛋白水平较高。79.2%的患者在入院时开始使用抗生素。中位住院时间为8天(2-35天),当病原体为呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和肠道病毒时,住院时间更长。重症监护病房的需求率为 15.4%,主要是诊断为 RSV 的患者。结论:临床上仅以发热开始的呼吸道病毒感染的微创 治疗 为合理使用抗生素和医疗保健提供了机会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过评估住院期间出现不明原因发烧的患者来确定呼吸道病毒在疾病期间的作用。
设计与对象
对 168 名 3 岁以下患者的数据进行了人口统计学、临床、实验室和放射学检查结果的回顾性分析。中位年龄为 11 (1-35) 个月,其中 48.2% 为女性。中位住院时间为8天(2-35天),当病原体为呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)和肠道病毒时,住院时间更长。
核心方法
该研究涉及对患者数据的回顾性分析,包括人口统计、临床、实验室和放射学结果。对患者是否存在呼吸道病毒进行了评估,并比较了单药感染者和多病毒感染者的发热持续时间。
主要结果
使用单一药物的患者中更容易检测到副流感、鼻病毒和腺病毒。存在多种病毒时发烧持续时间更长 (p = 0.01)。70.2% 的患者 C 反应蛋白水平较高。79.2%的患者在入院时开始使用抗生素。
解释与创新
作者解释说,多种病毒的存在与长时间发烧有关。他们的结论是,在临床上仅以发烧开始的呼吸道病毒感染的微创 治疗 为合理使用抗生素和医疗保健提供了机会。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,识别儿童无源发烧的病原体可以为更有针对性的抗生素使用提供信息,并改善医疗保健资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能会引入偏差。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于未经实验验证的观察结果。

Fever Without a Source in Children Under 3 Years of Age: The Role of Respiratory Viruses

Authors: Özge Kaba, Manolya Kara, Murat Sütçü, Hacer Aktürk, Selda Hançerlı Törün, Sevim Meşe, Ali Ağaçfidan, Ayper Somer, Murat Sutçu, S. Hançerli Torun
Original abstract (English)
Objective: The aim of this study was to determine the role of respiratory viruses during the disease by evaluating patients who presented with fever of unknown origin during their hospitalization.Materials and Methods: Data from 168 patients aged under 3 years were retrospectively analyzed in terms of demographic, clinical, laboratory, and radiological findings.Results: Median age was 11 (1-35) months, and 48.2% were female. Parainfluenza, rhinovirus, and adenovirus were detected more frequently in patients with a single agent. The duration of fever was longer in the presence of multiple viruses (p = 0.01). There was no statistical difference between the duration of fever and the type of virus. Human bocavirus was the most common type detected in patients presenting with febrile convulsions. C-reactive protein levels were high in 70.2% of patients. Antibiotics were started in 79.2% of patients at the time of admission. The median length of hospital stay was 8 days (2-35 days), and this period was longer when the causative agents were respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and enterovirus. Intensive care unit requirement was 15.4%, mainly in patients diagnosed with RSV.Conclusion: Minimally invasive treatment of respiratory viral infections that begin with fever alone in the clinic offers opportunities for rational antibiotic and health care use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the role of respiratory viruses during the disease by evaluating patients who presented with fever of unknown origin during their hospitalization.
Design and population
Data from 168 patients aged under 3 years were retrospectively analyzed in terms of demographic, clinical, laboratory, and radiological findings. The median age was 11 (1-35) months, and 48.2% were female. The median length of hospital stay was 8 days (2-35 days), and this period was longer when the causative agents were respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and enterovirus.
Core methods
The study involved retrospective analysis of patient data, including demographic, clinical, laboratory, and radiological findings. Patients were evaluated for the presence of respiratory viruses, and the duration of fever was compared between those with single-agent and multiple-virus infections.
Main results
Parainfluenza, rhinovirus, and adenovirus were detected more frequently in patients with a single agent. The duration of fever was longer in the presence of multiple viruses (p = 0.01). C-reactive protein levels were high in 70.2% of patients. Antibiotics were started in 79.2% of patients at the time of admission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the presence of multiple viruses was associated with prolonged fever. They concluded that minimally invasive treatment of respiratory viral infections that begin with fever alone in the clinic offers opportunities for rational antibiotic and health care use.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that identifying the causative agents of fever without a source in children can inform more targeted antibiotic use and improve health care resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it relies on observational findings without experimental validation.

尼塞韦单抗 和二价母体 疫苗 对意大利婴儿 RSV 感染的临床和经济影响

Clinical and Economic Impact of Nirsevimab and Bivalent Maternal Vaccine Against RSV Infections in Infants in Italy
作者： Chiara Bini, Andrea Marcellusi, Barbara Muzii, Samira Soudani, Sara Trotta, Salvatore Parisi, Elena Bozzola, Paolo Bonanni, Sara Boccalini
摘要中文翻译返回期刊 » 临床经济学和结果研究 » 第 18 卷 尼塞韦单抗 和二价母体 疫苗 对意大利婴儿 RSV 感染的临床和经济影响 作者 Bini C,Marcellusi A,Muzii B,Soudani S,Trotta S,Parisi S,Bozzola E,Bonanni P,Boccalini S 于 2026 年 5 月 30 日收到接受出版 2026 年 9 月 30 日 出版 2026 年 11 月 3 日 第 2026 卷:18 622013 DOI https://doi.org/10.2147/CEOR.S622013 检查是否抄袭 是 由单一匿名同行评审评审 同行评审意见 2 批准出版的编辑:Giorgio Colombo Chiara Bini 教授、1 Andrea Marcellusi、1 Barbara Muzii,2 Samira Soudani,3 Sara Trotta,2 Salvatore Parisi,2 Elena Bozzola,4 Paolo Bonanni,5 Sara Boccalini5 1 经济和国际研究中心 - 经济评估和卫生技术评估 (CEIS-EEHTA),罗马大学经济学院,罗马,00133,意大利;2赛诺菲,罗马,意大利;3赛诺菲,里昂,法国;4IRCCS Bambino Gesù 儿童医院,意大利罗马;5 佛罗伦萨大学健康科学系,佛罗伦萨,50134,意大利通讯作者:Chiara Bini,经济和国际研究中心 - 经济评估和卫生技术评估,罗马大学经济学院“Tor Vergata”,罗马,00133,意大利,电话 +393287236584,电子邮件 [email protected] 目的:本研究旨在评估潜在的临床和经济影响与没有预防策略的情况相比,尼塞韦单抗 和母亲 疫苗 在意大利进入第一个 RSV 季节的整个 队列研究 新生儿中预防就医 (MA) RSV 相关下呼吸道感染 (LRTI)。方法:使用先前发布的静态决策树模型进行成本后果分析...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 疫苗 在意大利进入第一个 RSV 季节的整个 队列研究 新生儿中预防就医的 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 与缺乏预防策略的情况相比的潜在临床和经济影响。
设计与对象
使用静态决策树模型进行成本后果分析,以估计单个 RSV 流行季节免疫策略的影响。该研究主要针对意大利的新生儿,考虑了覆盖率和早产、先天性心脏病和支气管肺发育不良等危险因素。
核心方法
使用先前发布的静态决策树模型进行成本后果分析,以估计单个 RSV 流行季节期间每种免疫策略避免的 RSV-MA-LRTI 和相关的中长期并发症。具有特定危险因素的早产新生儿和婴儿的亚群是根据 CeDAP 2024 报告的比例计算的。
主要结果
据估计,对 80% 的新生儿施用 尼塞韦单抗 可以预防总人口中 50.2%(95% CI:43.5-55.8)的住院治疗。达到与常规儿童 疫苗 (95%) 相当的覆盖水平可以使住院治疗平均减少 18%。据估计,65% 的孕妇接受 MI 治疗可避免 30.5%(95% CI:19.6-39.7)的住院治疗。
解释与创新
该研究的结论是,与母亲 疫苗 相比,尼塞韦单抗 与 RSV 相关住院风险较低相关,并且使用 尼塞韦单抗 进行婴儿被动免疫与严重后果风险较低相关,包括 PICU 入院、呼吸机支持或氧疗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为预防婴儿 RSV 疾病的公共卫生策略提供信息,特别是在意大利,通过强调 尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇 疫苗接种 计划在减少住院和严重后果方面的潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于先前发布的关于覆盖率和风险因素的模型和假设,这可能会限制结果的可信度。该证据基于静态决策树模型和真实世界数据,提供了中等程度的置信度。

Clinical and Economic Impact of Nirsevimab and Bivalent Maternal Vaccine Against RSV Infections in Infants in Italy

Authors: Chiara Bini, Andrea Marcellusi, Barbara Muzii, Samira Soudani, Sara Trotta, Salvatore Parisi, Elena Bozzola, Paolo Bonanni, Sara Boccalini
Original abstract (English)
Back to Journals » ClinicoEconomics and Outcomes Research » Volume 18 Clinical and Economic Impact of Nirsevimab and Bivalent Maternal Vaccine Against RSV Infections in Infants in Italy Authors Bini C, Marcellusi A, Muzii B, Soudani S , Trotta S, Parisi S, Bozzola E, Bonanni P , Boccalini S Received 30 May 2026 Accepted for publication 30 September 2026 Published 3 November 2026 Volume 2026:18 622013 DOI https://doi.org/10.2147/CEOR.S622013 Checked for plagiarism Yes Review by Single anonymous peer review Peer reviewer comments 2 Editor who approved publication: Professor Giorgio Colombo Chiara Bini,1 Andrea Marcellusi,1 Barbara Muzii,2 Samira Soudani,3 Sara Trotta,2 Salvatore Parisi,2 Elena Bozzola,4 Paolo Bonanni,5 Sara Boccalini5 1Centre for Economics and International Studies-Economic Evaluation and Health Technology Assessment (CEIS-EEHTA), Faculty of Economics, University of Rome ‘Tor Vergata’, Rome, 00133, Italy; 2Sanofi, Rome, Italy; 3Sanofi, Lyon, France; 4IRCCS Bambino Gesù Children Hospital, Rome, Italy; 5Department of Health Sciences, University of Florence, Florence, 50134, Italy Correspondence: Chiara Bini, Centre for Economics and International Studies-Economic Evaluation and Health Technology Assessment, Faculty of Economics, University of Rome "Tor Vergata”, Rome, 00133, Italy, Tel +393287236584, Email [email protected] Objective: This study aimed to estimate the potential clinical and economic impact of nirsevimab and of a maternal vaccine in the prevention of medically attended (MA) RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in the entire cohort of newborns entering their first RSV season in Italy compared with the absence of prevention strategies. Methods: A cost-consequence analysis using a static decision tree model previously publi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to estimate the potential clinical and economic impact of nirsevimab and a maternal vaccine in the prevention of medically attended RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) in the entire cohort of newborns entering their first RSV season in Italy compared with the absence of prevention strategies.
Design and population
A cost-consequence analysis using a static decision tree model was conducted to estimate the impact of immunization strategies during a single RSV epidemic season. The study focused on newborns in Italy, considering coverage rates and risk factors such as preterm birth, congenital heart disease, and bronchopulmonary dysplasia.
Core methods
A cost-consequence analysis using a static decision tree model previously published was conducted to estimate the RSV-MA-LRTIs and associated mid-long-term complications avoided with each immunization strategy during a single RSV epidemic season. The subpopulation of preterm newborns and infants with specific risk factors was calculated based on reported proportions from CeDAP 2024.
Main results
The administration of nirsevimab to 80% of newborns was estimated to prevent 50.2% (95% CI: 43.5–55.8) of hospitalisations in the total population. Achieving coverage levels comparable to routine childhood vaccines (95%) could yield an additional average reduction of 18% in hospitalisations. MI administrated to 65% of pregnant women was estimated to avert 30.5% (95% CI: 19.6–39.7) of hospitalisations.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concludes that nirsevimab is associated with a lower risk of RSV-related hospitalization compared with the maternal vaccine, and that passive infant immunization with nirsevimab is associated with a lower risk of severe outcomes, including PICU admission, ventilator support, or oxygen therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for the prevention of RSV disease in infants, particularly in the context of Italy, by highlighting the potential benefits of nirsevimab and maternal vaccination programs in reducing hospitalizations and severe outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on previously published models and assumptions about coverage rates and risk factors, which may limit the confidence in the results. The evidence is based on a static decision tree model and real-world data, providing a moderate level of confidence.

第22届国际新生儿学和儿科研讨会摘要精选;卡利亚里(意大利);2026 年 10 月 22 日

Vendemmia(意大利阿韦尔萨和那不勒斯) ABS 47. 肉芽肿性多血管炎 (GPA) • S. Vendemmia(意大利阿韦尔萨和那不勒斯;阿尔巴尼亚地拉那;罗马尼亚布加勒斯特) ABS 48. 沉浸式 VI 的影响
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨无症状产毒艰难梭菌定植作为肠道菌群失调标志物的潜在作用及其与 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的关联,强调了解儿科呼吸道感染中肠肺轴的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究是一项针对 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的临床研究,旨在研究儿科患者肠道菌群失调与呼吸道症状之间的关系。
核心方法
这些方法包括分析临床数据并利用测定来评估与 RSV 毛细支气管炎 相关的肠道菌群失调和粪便钙卫蛋白水平,重点是肠肺轴。
主要结果
研究发现,无症状产毒艰难梭菌定植与肠道菌群失调相关,并在 RSV 毛细支气管炎 患者中观察到。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明肠道菌群失调与 RSV 毛细支气管炎 之间存在潜在联系,为儿科呼吸系统疾病的肠肺轴提供了新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来关于肠肺轴的研究及其对 诊断 和儿童 RSV 毛细支气管炎 管理的影响提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其观察性质和潜在混杂因素的限制,但证据基于临床观察,并对拟议的关联提供了适度的信心。

View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential role of asymptomatic toxigenic Clostridioides difficile colonization as a marker of gut dysbiosis and its association with RSV bronchiolitis, highlighting the need to understand the gut-lung axis in pediatric respiratory infections.
Design and population
The study was a clinical investigation focusing on RSV bronchiolitis, examining the relationship between gut dysbiosis and respiratory symptoms in pediatric patients.
Core methods
The methods involved analyzing clinical data and utilizing assays to assess gut dysbiosis and fecal calprotectin levels in relation to RSV bronchiolitis, with an emphasis on the gut-lung axis.
Main results
The study found that asymptomatic toxigenic Clostridioides difficile colonization was associated with gut dysbiosis and was observed in patients with RSV bronchiolitis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as suggesting a potential link between gut dysbiosis and RSV bronchiolitis, offering a novel perspective on the gut-lung axis in pediatric respiratory diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform future research on the gut-lung axis and its implications for the diagnosis and management of RSV bronchiolitis in children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is grounded in clinical observations and provides moderate confidence in the proposed association.

Human type 3 parainfluenza virus activates NK cells after in vitro stimulation of peripheral blood mononuclear cells
作者： V. A. Matyushenko, T. S. Kotomina, И. N. Isakova-Sivak
摘要中文翻译副流感病毒(PIV)与呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、偏肺病毒、甲型和乙型流感病毒、腺病毒等在急性呼吸道病毒感染中起重要作用,引起上下呼吸道炎症,可能导致严重支气管炎和肺炎,特别是在幼儿和免疫功能低下的成人中。此外,副流感感染常常使哮喘和慢性阻塞性肺病等慢性疾病复杂化。然而,尽管 疫苗 的开发正在全球范围内积极进行,但目前尚无针对副流感的具体预防方法。Smorodintsev 实验医学研究所病毒学和免疫学系正在开发一种针对 RSV、偏肺病毒和人类 PIV 3 型 (PIV3) 的国产 T 细胞 疫苗。该 疫苗 基于活减毒流感 疫苗 媒介。该研究揭示了具有CD3-表型的细胞中IFNγ的高表达。11名年龄在23岁至43岁之间的健康志愿者参与了这项试验。使用一组针对 CD3、CD8、CD4、CD197、CD45RA、CD56、IFNγ 和 TNFα 抗原的抗体,用 H1N1、H3N2、RSV 和 PIV3 流感病毒刺激他们的外周血单核细胞。该研究是通过多色流式细胞术进行的。研究发现,具有 CD3-CD56+ 表型的自然杀伤细胞在接受人 PIV3 处理时会合成大量干扰素-γ,但它们对 RSV 以及 H1N1 和 H3N2 流感病毒的刺激没有反应。此外,研究发现,NK 细胞在受到 PIV3 刺激时会释放 TNFα,但它们并不是多功能的。这项研究的结果与世界范围内发表的研究结果一致,并提出了免疫应答针对PIV3的独特机制,可用于开发针对人类PIV的T细胞疫苗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查人类 3 型副流感病毒 (PIV3) 的 免疫应答 及其在 疫苗 发展中的潜在作用,因为 PIV3 是急性呼吸道感染的重要原因,特别是在易感人群中。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 11 名年龄在 23 岁至 43 岁之间的健康志愿者,他们的外周血单核细胞受到包括 PIV3 在内的各种病毒的刺激,以评估免疫激活。
核心方法
用 H1N1、H3N2、RSV 和 PIV3 流感病毒刺激外周血单核细胞,并使用一组针对 CD3、CD8、CD4、CD197、CD45RA、CD56、IFNγ 和 TNFα 抗原的抗体通过多色流式细胞术分析 免疫应答。
主要结果
该研究表明,当受到 PIV3 刺激时,CD3-表型细胞中会出现高 IFNγ 表达,但受到 RSV 或流感病毒刺激时则不会。NK 细胞也响应 PIV3 释放 TNFα,尽管它们不是多功能的。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 PIV3 具有独特的 免疫应答 机制,这可能为针对该病毒的 T 细胞 疫苗 的发育提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能有助于了解针对 PIV3 的免疫机制,并支持开发针对呼吸道病毒感染的靶向 疫苗。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外观察和小样本量,但证据与全球研究一致,并为 PIV3 的免疫激活提供了新的见解。

Human type 3 parainfluenza virus activates NK cells after in vitro stimulation of peripheral blood mononuclear cells

Authors: V. A. Matyushenko, T. S. Kotomina, И. N. Isakova-Sivak
Original abstract (English)
Parainfluenza virus (PIV), along with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), metapneumoviruses, influenza A and B viruses, adenoviruses, etc. plays a significant role in prevalence of acute respiratory viral infections, causing inflammation of upper and lower respiratory tract which may lead to severe bronchitis and pneumonia, especially in young children and immunocompromised adults. Moreover, parainfluenza infection frequently complicates chronic diseases such as asthma and COPD. However, there is currently no specific prophylaxis against parainfluenza, although vaccine development is actively underway worldwide. Smorodintsev Department of Virology and Immunology at the Institute of Experimental Medicine is developing a domestic T cell vaccine against RSV, metapneumovirus, and human PIV type 3 (PIV3). This vaccine is based on a live attenuated influenza vaccine vector. The study revealed high IFNγ expression in cells with the CD3- phenotype. Eleven healthy volunteers aged 23 to 43 years participated in this trial. Their peripheral blood mononuclear cells were stimulated with H1N1, H3N2, RSV, and PIV3 influenza viruses using a panel of antibodies against CD3, CD8, CD4, CD197, CD45RA, CD56, IFNγ, and TNFα antigens. The study was conducted by means of multicolor flow cytometry. It was found that natural killer cells with the CD3-CD56+ phenotype synthesize significant amounts of interferon-γ when treated by human PIV3, but they do not respond to stimulation by RSV and H1N1 and H3N2 influenza viruses. Furthermore, it was found that NK cells release TNFα when stimulated by PIV3, but they are not polyfunctional. The results of this study are consistent with research published worldwide, and they suggest a unique mechanism of immune response to PIV3, which can be used in development of T cell vaccines against human PIV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the immune response to human type 3 parainfluenza virus (PIV3) and its potential role in vaccine development, as PIV3 is a significant cause of acute respiratory infections, particularly in vulnerable populations.
Design and population
The study involved eleven healthy volunteers aged 23 to 43 years, whose peripheral blood mononuclear cells were stimulated with various viruses, including PIV3, to assess immune activation.
Core methods
Peripheral blood mononuclear cells were stimulated with H1N1, H3N2, RSV, and PIV3 influenza viruses, and a panel of antibodies against CD3, CD8, CD4, CD197, CD45RA, CD56, IFNγ, and TNFα antigens was used to analyze immune responses via multicolor flow cytometry.
Main results
The study revealed high IFNγ expression in CD3- phenotype cells when stimulated by PIV3, but not by RSV or influenza viruses. NK cells also released TNFα in response to PIV3, though they were not polyfunctional.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a unique immune response mechanism to PIV3, which may inform the development of T cell vaccines targeting this virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results could contribute to understanding immune mechanisms against PIV3 and support the development of targeted vaccines for respiratory viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro observations and a small sample size, but the evidence is consistent with global research and provides a novel insight into immune activation by PIV3.

从针对病毒感染的预防性 mRNA 疫苗 到治疗应用:扩展 mRNA 平台的监管科学考虑因素

From prophylactic mRNA vaccines against viral infections to therapeutic applications: regulatory science considerations for expanding mRNA platforms
作者： Sunghyun Yi, Eunhye Seo, Pil‐Gu Park, Kee‐Jong Hong
摘要中文翻译传统的非扩增信使 RNA (mRNA) 和自扩增 RNA (saRNA) 药物通常共享核心线性 RNA 结构,包括 5' 帽、非翻译区、一个或多个开放阅读框和 Poly(A) 尾,并且通常采用脂质纳米粒子 (LNP) 等递送系统配制,以实现靶蛋白的瞬时细胞内表达。预防性 mRNA 疫苗 主要设计用于表达传染病抗原并产生保护性免疫记忆,而 mRNA 治疗剂则发挥药理作用,旨在治疗疾病、抑制复发、替换蛋白质、调节免疫或传递基因编辑酶。因此,即使是基于类似 mRNA-LNP 技术的产品,根据其预期用途和作用机制,也可能有不同的监管分类和证据要求。临床和监管格局已从第一个冠状病毒疾病 2019 疫苗 扩展到包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 和流感 mRNA 疫苗、流感-冠状病毒疾病 2019 组合 疫苗、授权的 saRNA 疫苗、个性化癌症免疫疗法、蛋白质替代疗法、体内细胞编程和基因编辑传递系统。本综述探讨了哪些预防性 疫苗 证据可以支持治疗开发,以及哪些产品特定质量、非临床、临床和生命周期证据仍然是必要的。对于预防性mRNA 疫苗,主要监管考虑因素包括质量、免疫原性、预防功效、批次放行以及大规模疫苗接种后的安全性监测。对于 mRNA 疗法,主要问题包括递送至靶组织、表达产物的功能、重复剂量安全性、生物分布、脱靶风险、长期随访以及患者特异性制造和可追溯性。特别是,虽然个性化mRNA癌症疫苗通常被描述为疫苗,但它们在监管目的上更接近于根据每位患者的肿瘤特征发挥作用的治疗方法,而不是预防传染病的疫苗。因此,抗原选择算法、患者特异性序列设计、小批量和多产品生产、产品与患者匹配、组合策略的临床评估、生产延迟和分析群体以及基于风险的长期授权后随访都需要明确的监管考虑。来自预防性 mRNA 疫苗 授权的平台级知识可以支持治疗开发,但监管问题可能会因每种新产品类型而异;开发商需要跟踪各个司法管辖区不断变化的监管工具,并尽早与监管机构接触。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了了解预防性 mRNA 疫苗 的监管科学考虑如何适应治疗应用的需要,特别是在扩展 mRNA 平台以解决 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等病毒感染的背景下。
设计与对象
该研究是一项审查 mRNA 疫苗 和治疗的监管环境的综述,重点关注扩展 mRNA 平台以包括 RSV 和其他病毒的治疗应用的临床和监管考虑因素。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对预防性 mRNA 疫苗 的监管科学考虑因素及其在治疗性 mRNA 平台中的潜在应用的全面审查,包括根据预期用途和作用机制对证据要求的差异进行分析。
主要结果
该审查强调,虽然来自预防性 mRNA 疫苗 的平台级知识可以支持治疗开发,但监管问题可能会因每种新产品类型而异,需要量身定制的方法并与监管机构持续接触。
解释与创新
作者解释说,预防性 mRNA 疫苗 的监管考虑因素不能直接转移到治疗应用,强调需要产品特定的证据和适应性监管框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了解决独特的监管挑战(例如递送、表达产品的功能和患者特异性制造)的重要性,为 RSV mRNA 疗法的开发提供了信息,这对于有效的 治疗 和安全性至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对现有监管框架的审查,并没有提供新的实证数据,从而根据对当前文献和监管考虑的分析得出适度的置信度评估。

From prophylactic mRNA vaccines against viral infections to therapeutic applications: regulatory science considerations for expanding mRNA platforms

Authors: Sunghyun Yi, Eunhye Seo, Pil‐Gu Park, Kee‐Jong Hong
Original abstract (English)
Conventional non-amplifying messenger RNA (mRNA) and self-amplifying RNA (saRNA) medicines generally share a core linear RNA architecture—including a 5′ cap, untranslated regions, one or more open reading frames, and a poly(A) tail—and are commonly formulated with delivery systems such as lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) to enable transient intracellular expression of a target protein. Prophylactic mRNA vaccines are designed primarily to express infectious-disease antigens and generate protective immune memory, whereas mRNA therapeutics exert pharmacological effects intended to treat disease, suppress recurrence, replace proteins, modulate immunity, or deliver gene-editing enzymes. Thus, even products based on similar mRNA–LNP technology may have different regulatory classifications and evidence requirements according to their intended use and mechanism of action. The clinical and regulatory landscape has expanded beyond the first coronavirus disease 2019 vaccines to include respiratory syncytial virus and influenza mRNA vaccines, an influenza–coronavirus disease 2019 combination vaccine, an authorized saRNA vaccine, personalized cancer immunotherapies, protein-replacement therapies, in vivo cell programming, and gene-editing delivery systems. This review examines which prophylactic-vaccine evidence can support therapeutic development and where product-specific quality, nonclinical, clinical, and lifecycle evidence remains necessary. For prophylactic mRNA vaccines, major regulatory considerations include quality, immunogenicity, preventive efficacy, lot release, and safety surveillance after large-scale vaccination. For mRNA therapeutics, the principal issues include delivery to target tissues, function of the expressed product, repeat-dose safety, biodistribution, off-target risk, long-term follow-up, and patient-specific manufacture and traceability. In particular, although personalized mRNA cancer vaccines are commonly described as vaccines, they are closer in regulatory purpose to therapeutics that act on the basis of each patient’s tumor characteristics than to vaccines that prevent infectious disease. Antigen-selection algorithms, patient-specific sequence design, small-batch and multi-product manufacture, product–patient matching, clinical evaluation of combination strategies, manufacturing delays and analysis populations, and risk-based long-term postauthorization follow-up therefore require explicit regulatory consideration. Platform-level knowledge from prophylactic mRNA vaccine authorization can support therapeutic development, but regulatory questions may vary with each new product type; developers will need to track evolving regulatory instruments across jurisdictions and engage regulatory authorities early.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to understand how regulatory science considerations for prophylactic mRNA vaccines can be adapted for therapeutic applications, particularly in the context of expanding mRNA platforms to address viral infections such as respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Design and population
The study is a review examining the regulatory landscape for mRNA vaccines and therapeutics, focusing on the clinical and regulatory considerations for expanding mRNA platforms to include therapeutic applications for RSV and other viruses.
Core methods
The methods involve a comprehensive review of the regulatory science considerations for prophylactic mRNA vaccines and their potential application to therapeutic mRNA platforms, including an analysis of the differences in evidence requirements based on intended use and mechanism of action.
Main results
The review highlights that while platform-level knowledge from prophylactic mRNA vaccines can support therapeutic development, regulatory questions may vary with each new product type, necessitating tailored approaches and ongoing engagement with regulatory authorities.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the regulatory considerations for prophylactic mRNA vaccines are not directly transferable to therapeutic applications, emphasizing the need for product-specific evidence and adaptive regulatory frameworks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of mRNA therapeutics for RSV by highlighting the importance of addressing unique regulatory challenges such as delivery, function of the expressed product, and patient-specific manufacturing, which are critical for effective treatment and safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a review of existing regulatory frameworks and does not present new empirical data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment based on the analysis of current literature and regulatory considerations.

预防婴儿和儿童 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的建议:政策声明。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Policy Statement.
作者： Committee on Infectious Diseases .
摘要中文翻译本政策声明更新了美国儿科学会 (AAP) 关于使用 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫接种来预防婴儿和儿童由 RSV 引起的下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的建议。AAP 建议对在第一个 RSV 季节期间出生或进入其第一个 RSV 季节期间出生的所有 8 个月以下婴儿进行 RSV 免疫接种,除非婴儿有其怀孕父母的 疫苗接种 保护记录;以及患有严重 RSV 疾病并进入第二个 RSV 季节的高风险的 8 至 19 个月大的婴儿和儿童。证据审查支持扩大对进入第二个 RSV 季节的 8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童进行 RSV 免疫接种的高风险标准。随附的技术报告 (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049) 讨论了支持 AAP 建议的证据以及扩展建议中包含的有关婴儿和儿童的其他详细信息。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该政策声明的重点是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 免疫接种,以预防婴儿和儿童由 RSV 引起的下呼吸道感染 (LRTI)。目标是人类呼吸道合胞病毒 (HRSV),特别是RSV-A和RSV-B,核心问题是免疫接种是否应该扩大到某些高危婴儿和儿童。
证据基础与方法
证据基础包括对支持 AAP 建议的研究和数据的回顾。审查方法包括检查扩大进入第二个 RSV 季节的 8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童 RSV 免疫高风险标准的证据。随附的技术报告提供了有关建议中包含的证据和人群的更多详细信息。
共识与结论
该审查支持扩大对患有严重RSV疾病并进入第二个RSV季节的8至19个月婴儿和儿童RSV免疫的高风险标准。这表明对于这个特定年龄组需要有针对性的免疫策略的共识。
争议与缺口
8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童 RSV 免疫的证据有限,该审查强调需要更多数据来充分了解该群体免疫的有效性和安全性。免疫接种的最佳时机和持续时间也缺乏明确性。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究来评估 8 至 19 个月婴儿和儿童 RSV 免疫的有效性和安全性。此外,需要改进 监测 和数据收集,以更好地了解 RSV 疾病的负担和免疫策略的影响。

Recommendations for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Disease in Infants and Children: Policy Statement.

Authors: Committee on Infectious Diseases .
Original abstract (English)
This policy statement updates recommendations of the American Academy of Pediatrics (AAP) for the use of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization for the prevention of lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) caused by RSV in infants and children. The AAP recommends RSV immunization for all infants under 8 months of age born during or entering their first RSV season, unless the infant has documented protection from vaccination of their pregnant parent; and for infants and children 8 through 19 months of age who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season. A review of evidence supports expanding the high-risk criteria for administration of RSV immunization in infants and children 8 through 19 months of age entering their second RSV season. A discussion of the evidence supporting the AAP recommendations and additional details on infants and children included in the expanded recommendations are in the accompanying technical report (https://doi.org/10.1542/peds.2026-079049).
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The policy statement focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) immunization for the prevention of lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) caused by RSV in infants and children. The target is human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV), specifically RSV-A and RSV-B, and the central question is whether immunization should be expanded to certain high-risk infants and children.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base includes a review of studies and data supporting the AAP recommendations. The review method involves examining the evidence for expanding high-risk criteria for RSV immunization in infants and children aged 8 through 19 months entering their second RSV season. The accompanying technical report provides additional details on the evidence and the population included in the recommendations.
Consensus and conclusions
The review supports expanding the high-risk criteria for RSV immunization in infants and children aged 8 through 19 months who are at high risk of severe RSV disease and entering their second RSV season. This indicates a consensus on the need for targeted immunization strategies in this specific age group.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence for RSV immunization in infants and children aged 8 through 19 months is limited, and the review highlights the need for more data to fully understand the effectiveness and safety of immunization in this group. There is also a lack of clarity on the optimal timing and duration of immunization.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to evaluate the effectiveness and safety of RSV immunization in infants and children aged 8 through 19 months. Additionally, there is a need for improved surveillance and data collection to better understand the burden of RSV disease and the impact of immunization strategies.

与 2 岁以下婴儿严重 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染相关的循环生物标志物:系统评价

Circulating Biomarkers Associated with Severe Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Infants Under 2 Years: A Systematic Review
作者： Yihe Zhang, Panpinhan Zhao, Rui Ling, Yiu‐Wing Kam
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,但用于严重程度分层的循环生物标志物仍未得到充分验证。本系统评价评估了与 ≤ 24 个月儿童急性 RSV 疾病严重程度相关的循环生物标志物。PubMed、Web of Science 和 Scopus 搜索了 2016 年 1 月 1 日至 2026 年 3 月 1 日之间发表的英文报告,最后一次搜索于 2026 年 3 月 1 日进行。根据研究设计,使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表或乔安娜·布里格斯研究所检查表评估研究层面的偏倚风险。使用研究水平的效果估计和报告的严重性歧视措施对研究结果进行描述性综合。24 项研究贡献了 174 项可提取的比较。生物标志物关联因人群、年龄层、采样时间和严重程度定义而异,并且独立 队列研究 的再现性有限。在一项 队列研究 中,纤维蛋白原和纤维蛋白原与白蛋白的比率 (FAR) 在严重程度组之间显示出巨大差异;FAR 的 AUC 报告为 0.875。这些发现并未确立比较优势或外部有效性。重要的局限性包括异质性临床比较、有限的混杂因素调整以及主要是单一的 队列研究 证据。目前的证据不支持单独使用 FAR、中性粒细胞与淋巴细胞比率 (NLR) 或其他循环生物标志物来预测随后的临床恶化。这些标志物是否为临床评估添加有用的信息需要前瞻性验证。注册:PROSPERO,CRD420261471336。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,但用于严重程度分层的循环生物标志物仍未得到充分验证。
证据基础与方法
本系统评价评估了 24 个月以下儿童中与急性 RSV 疾病严重程度相关的循环生物标志物。PubMed、Web of Science 和 Scopus 搜索了 2016 年 1 月 1 日至 2026 年 3 月 1 日之间发表的英文报告,最后一次搜索于 2026 年 3 月 1 日进行。根据研究设计,使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表或乔安娜·布里格斯研究所检查表评估研究层面的偏倚风险。
共识与结论
使用研究水平的效果估计和报告的严重性歧视措施对研究结果进行描述性综合。在一项 队列研究 中,纤维蛋白原和纤维蛋白原与白蛋白的比率 (FAR) 在严重程度组之间显示出巨大差异;FAR 的 AUC 报告为 0.875。
争议与缺口
生物标志物关联因人群、年龄层、采样时间和严重程度定义而异,并且独立 队列研究 的再现性有限。重要的局限性包括异质性临床比较、有限的混杂因素调整以及主要是单一的 队列研究 证据。这些发现并未确立比较优势或外部有效性。
科研与实践启示
这些标志物是否为临床评估添加有用的信息需要前瞻性验证。目前的证据不支持单独使用 FAR、中性粒细胞与淋巴细胞比率 (NLR) 或其他循环生物标志物来预测随后的临床恶化。

Circulating Biomarkers Associated with Severe Respiratory Syncytial Virus Infection in Infants Under 2 Years: A Systematic Review

Authors: Yihe Zhang, Panpinhan Zhao, Rui Ling, Yiu‐Wing Kam
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infection in infants, but circulating biomarkers for severity stratification remain insufficiently validated. This systematic review evaluated circulating biomarkers associated with acute RSV disease severity in children aged≤ 24 months. PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus were searched for English-language reports published between 1 January 2016 and 1 March 2026, with the last searches performed on 1 March 2026. Study-level risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale or the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist, according to study design. Findings were synthesized descriptively using study-level effect estimates and reported severity-discrimination measures. Twenty-four studies contributed 174 extractable comparisons. Biomarker associations varied across populations, age strata, sampling times, and severity definitions, and reproducibility across independent cohorts was limited. In one cohort, fibrinogen and the fibrinogen-to-albumin ratio (FAR) showed large differences between severity groups; FAR had a reported AUC of 0.875. These findings do not establish comparative superiority or external validity. Important limitations include heterogeneous clinical comparisons, limited confounder adjustment, and predominantly single-cohort evidence. Current evidence does not support using FAR, the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR), or other circulating biomarkers alone to predict subsequent clinical deterioration. Whether these markers add useful information to clinical assessment requires prospective validation. Registration: PROSPERO, CRD420261471336.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infection in infants, but circulating biomarkers for severity stratification remain insufficiently validated.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review evaluated circulating biomarkers associated with acute RSV disease severity in children aged ≤ 24 months. PubMed, Web of Science, and Scopus were searched for English-language reports published between 1 January 2016 and 1 March 2026, with the last searches performed on 1 March 2026. Study-level risk of bias was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale or the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist, according to study design.
Consensus and conclusions
Findings were synthesized descriptively using study-level effect estimates and reported severity-discrimination measures. In one cohort, fibrinogen and the fibrinogen-to-albumin ratio (FAR) showed large differences between severity groups; FAR had a reported AUC of 0.875.
Controversies and gaps
Biomarker associations varied across populations, age strata, sampling times, and severity definitions, and reproducibility across independent cohorts was limited. Important limitations include heterogeneous clinical comparisons, limited confounder adjustment, and predominantly single-cohort evidence. These findings do not establish comparative superiority or external validity.
Research and practice implications
Whether these markers add useful information to clinical assessment requires prospective validation. Current evidence does not support using FAR, the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio (NLR), or other circulating biomarkers alone to predict subsequent clinical deterioration.

MSC-RSV/HMPV 界面:对肺稳态、重塑以及非特应性哮喘和 COPD 风险的影响

The MSC–RSV/HMPV interface: impacts on pulmonary homeostasis, remodeling, and risk of non-atopic asthma and COPD
作者： Rocio Tirado Mendoza, Rocío Tirado-Mendoza
摘要中文翻译呼吸道感染仍然是儿科发病和 死亡率 的主要原因。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和人类偏肺病毒 (HMPV) 被认为是 毛细支气管炎 和肺炎的关键病原体,特别是在两岁以下的儿童中。生命第二年的反复感染可能与显着的组织病理学损伤有关,也可能是发展为慢性肺部疾病(例如非特应性哮喘)的危险因素。在成人中,这些病毒同样会加重老年人和慢性阻塞性肺疾病(COPD)患者的临床负担,导致呼吸信号和症状加剧,从而促进慢性炎症反应,从而改变肺组织和疾病进展。组织修复和稳态恢复可能与间充质干细胞(MSC)的参与有关,包括肺常驻和迁移的MSC,这些细胞的特点是可塑性和免疫调节能力,与损伤后恢复组织稳态有关。然而,RSV和HMPV也可能感染MSC,这就提出了一个问题:受感染的MSC可能会失去免疫调节功效和可塑性,从而影响组织更新。本综述旨在概述呼吸道病毒感染的影响、其对肺组织损伤的影响以及间充质干细胞通过组织修复和 免疫应答 调节恢复肺稳态的可能参与。

The MSC–RSV/HMPV interface: impacts on pulmonary homeostasis, remodeling, and risk of non-atopic asthma and COPD

Authors: Rocio Tirado Mendoza, Rocío Tirado-Mendoza
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory tract infections continue to represent a major cause of pediatric morbidity and mortality. Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) and Human Metapneumovirus (HMPV) are highlighted as key etiologic agents of bronchiolitis and pneumonia, particularly in children under two years old. Recurrent infections during the second year of life may be associated with significant histopathological damage and might also be a risk factor for developing chronic pulmonary diseases, such as non-atopic asthma. In adults, these viruses similarly elevate clinical burden on the elderly and individuals with Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease (COPD), causing the exacerbation of respiratory signals and symptoms and, consequently, promoting a chronic inflammatory response that modifies the lung tissue and disease progression. Tissue repair and the homeostasis recovery might be associated with the participation of Mesenchymal Stem Cells (MSCs), both lung resident and migratory MSCs, these cells are characterized by their plasticity and immunomodulatory capacity, which are relevant to restoring tissue homeostasis after injury. However, RSV and HMPV may also infect MSCs, raising the question that infected MSCs could lose immunomodulatory efficacy and plasticity, thereby affecting tissue renewal. This review aims to provide an overview of the effect of respiratory viral infections, their implications in the lung tissue damage and the possible participation of MSCs in the recovery of lung homeostasis by tissue repair and immune response modulation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
In adults, these viruses similarly elevate clinical burden on the elderly and individuals with Chronic Obstructive Pulmonary Disease (COPD), causing the exacerbation of respiratory signals and symptoms and, consequently, promoting a chronic inflammatory response that modifies the lung tissue and disease progression.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Tissue repair and the homeostasis recovery might be associated with the participation of Mesenchymal Stem Cells (MSCs), both lung resident and migratory MSCs, these cells are characterized by their plasticity and immunomodulatory capacity, which are relevant to restoring tissue homeostasis after injury.
Controversies and gaps
However, RSV and HMPV may also infect MSCs, raising the question that infected MSCs could lose immunomodulatory efficacy and plasticity, thereby affecting tissue renewal.
Research and practice implications
This review aims to provide an overview of the effect of respiratory viral infections, their implications in the lung tissue damage and the possible participation of MSCs in the recovery of lung homeostasis by tissue repair and immune response modulation.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

RSV 二价预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗 在妊娠和预防婴儿 RSV 相关疾病中的作用:系统评价和荟萃分析。

RSV Bivalent Prefusion F Protein Vaccine in Pregnancy and Protection Against RSV-Associated Illness in Infants: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.
作者： Rabab Batool, Ran Wang, Hala Obeid, Tobias R. Kollmann, Joanne M. Langley, Pascal M. Lavoie, Bahaa Abu-Raya
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是妊娠期间施用 RSV 二价预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗 (RSVpreF) 在预防婴儿 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 方面的有效性。核心问题是 RSVpreF 是否能针对婴儿 RSV 相关疾病提供保护,特别是针对 LRTI 导致的住院治疗。
证据基础与方法
该综述对2020年1月1日至2026年1月30日期间的PubMed、Scopus和Europe PMC进行了系统检索。初步确定了总共1144项研究,所有研究均由2名评审员独立筛选。荟萃分析使用带有 Hartung-Knapp 调整和 Sidik-Jonkman 估计器的随机效应模型来合并比值比 (OR)。RSV 相关 LRTI 住院主要结局的 疫苗 有效性计算为 (1 - OR) × 100%。
共识与结论
合并的疫苗对呼吸道合胞病毒相关下呼吸道感染住院的预防效果在出生至3个月的婴儿中为82%(95%置信区间,81%-82%),在出生至6个月的婴儿中为78%(95%置信区间,70%-84%),当母亲在分娩前14天或更早接种疫苗时。这些结果支持在妊娠期间接种RSVpreF可有效预防出生后6个月内因呼吸道合胞病毒相关下呼吸道感染导致的住院。
争议与缺口
有限的可用数据无法对次要结果(如 RSV 相关急诊就诊、重症监护病房入院和死亡)的有效性进行荟萃分析。这表明这些具体结果的证据存在差距,当前的审查尚未完全解决这些问题。
科研与实践启示
结果支持将 RSVpreF 引入常规产前免疫计划。需要进一步的研究来评估其对次要结果的有效性,并扩大研究的地理和人口范围。

RSV Bivalent Prefusion F Protein Vaccine in Pregnancy and Protection Against RSV-Associated Illness in Infants: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.

Authors: Rabab Batool, Ran Wang, Hala Obeid, Tobias R. Kollmann, Joanne M. Langley, Pascal M. Lavoie, Bahaa Abu-Raya
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: The respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) bivalent prefusion F protein vaccine (RSVpreF) administered during pregnancy has demonstrated efficacy in protecting infants against early-life RSV disease in the pivotal clinical trial. OBJECTIVE: To conduct a systematic review and meta-analysis to synthesize real-world evidence on the effectiveness of RSVpreF in preventing RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) in infants. DATA SOURCES: PubMed, Scopus, and Europe PMC were searched from January 1, 2020, through January 30, 2026. STUDY SELECTION: A total of 1144 studies were initially identified. Eligible were nonrandomized studies reporting the effectiveness of RSVpreF vaccine in pregnancy in the prevention of RSV-associated LRTI in infants. DATA EXTRACTION AND SYNTHESIS: All studies were screened independently by 2 reviewers. Data were extracted on RSV-associated outcomes among infants born to women who received RSVpreF vaccination during pregnancy and infants born to unvaccinated women. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: The primary outcome was RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization. Odds ratios (ORs) were pooled using random-effects meta-analysis with the Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Sidik-Jonkman estimator. Vaccine effectiveness against RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization was calculated as (1 - OR) × 100% and assessed in infants from birth to age 3 months and from birth to 6 months, separately. The limited available data precluded meta-analysis of effectiveness against the secondary outcomes of RSV-associated emergency department visits, intensive care unit admissions, and death. RESULTS: A total of 7 studies were included, comprising 7112 infants, of whom 2684 were born to vaccinated mothers. The studies collected data from January 2024 through April 2025 from Argentina, the UK, and the US. Pooled vaccine effectiveness against RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization was 82% (95% CI, 81%-82%; 3 studies, 4397 participants; I2 = 0%) in infants from birth to 3 months and 78% (95% CI, 70%-84%; 6 studies, 3008 participants; I2 = 18.7%) in infants from birth to 6 months, when mothers were vaccinated 14 days or more before delivery. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: The results of this systematic review and meta-analysis suggest that postimplementation data support the effectiveness of RSVpreF in pregnancy for the prevention of RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization during the first 6 months of life. The results support the introduction of RSVpreF into routine antenatal immunization programs.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the effectiveness of the RSV bivalent prefusion F protein vaccine (RSVpreF) administered during pregnancy in preventing RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) in infants. The central question is whether RSVpreF provides protection against RSV-related illness in infants, with a specific emphasis on hospitalization due to LRTI.
Evidence base and method
The review conducted a systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, and Europe PMC from January 1, 2020, through January 30, 2026. A total of 1144 studies were initially identified, and all were screened independently by 2 reviewers. The meta-analysis used random-effects models with Hartung-Knapp adjustment and Sidik-Jonkman estimator to pool odds ratios (ORs). Vaccine effectiveness was calculated as (1 - OR) × 100% for the primary outcome of RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled vaccine effectiveness against RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization was 82% (95% CI, 81%-82%) in infants from birth to 3 months and 78% (95% CI, 70%-84%) in infants from birth to 6 months, when mothers were vaccinated 14 days or more before delivery. These results support the effectiveness of RSVpreF in pregnancy for preventing RSV-associated LRTI hospitalization during the first 6 months of life.
Controversies and gaps
The limited available data precluded meta-analysis of effectiveness against secondary outcomes such as RSV-associated emergency department visits, intensive care unit admissions, and death. This indicates a gap in the evidence for these specific outcomes, which are not fully addressed in the current review.
Research and practice implications
The results support the introduction of RSVpreF into routine antenatal immunization programs. Further research is needed to evaluate its effectiveness against secondary outcomes and to expand the geographic and demographic scope of the studies.
