[针对老年人的 疫苗接种 更新]。

[Update on vaccination for older adults].
作者： Anja Kwetkat, Anne-Sophie Endres, Hans Jürgen Heppner
摘要中文翻译老年人非常容易受到感染,其中一些感染是可以预防的。因此,疫苗接种 (STIKO) 常务委员会也定期更新其针对老年人的 疫苗接种 建议。自2025/26赛季起,除了高剂量流感疫苗外,还建议60岁及以上人群使用佐剂流感疫苗。此外,秋季针对呼吸道合胞病毒的一次性疫苗接种已被添加为75岁老年人的常规疫苗接种和60岁及以上患有严重基础疾病的老年人的基于适应症的疫苗接种。迄今为止,很少有人注意到 疫苗接种 针对带状疱疹、肺炎球菌疾病、流感和 RSV 的额外益处,即超出主要治疗益处并对健康老龄化做出重大贡献的效果。这些包括减少心肌和脑血管事件,以及对神经退行性过程的保护作用。特别是,已经证明流感疫苗在心肌梗塞的二级预防中与他汀类药物一样有效。这些发现可能会导致 疫苗接种 的接受度增加,从而导致 疫苗接种 费率的增加,但目前的比率仍然不足。未来为了继续提高疫苗接种的效益,疫苗需要进一步开发以实现更大的功效,这也有助于提高疫苗接种的接受度。目前正在进行多项新开发,特别是针对肺炎球菌 疫苗。自 2025 年 3 月起,一种用于成人的 21 价肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 已在欧洲获得许可,专门针对导致成人 IPD 的血清型。目前正在使用新技术开发其他涵盖多达 31 种血清型的肺炎球菌 疫苗 候选药物,并正在进行 2 期或 3 期试验。

证据基础与方法
疫苗接种 (STIKO) 常务委员会也会定期更新其针对老年人的 疫苗接种 建议。
共识与结论
特别是,已经证明流感疫苗在心肌梗塞的二级预防中与他汀类药物一样有效。
争议与缺口
这些发现可能会导致 疫苗接种 的接受度增加,从而导致 疫苗接种 费率的增加,但目前的比率仍然不足。
科研与实践启示
未来为了继续提高疫苗接种的效益,疫苗需要进一步开发以实现更大的功效,这也有助于提高疫苗接种的接受度。

[Update on vaccination for older adults].

Authors: Anja Kwetkat, Anne-Sophie Endres, Hans Jürgen Heppner
Original abstract (English)
Older adults are highly susceptible to infections, some of which are vaccine preventable. Therefore, the Standing Committee on Vaccination (STIKO) regularly updates its vaccination recommendations for older adults as well. Since the 2025/26 season, the adjuvanted influenza vaccine is recommended for people aged 60 and older, in addition to the high-dose influenza vaccine. In addition, a one-time vaccination against respiratory syncytial virus in autumn has been added as a routine vaccination for 75-year-olds and as an indication-based vaccination for those aged 60 and older with severe underlying medical conditions. Little attention has been paid so far to the now well documented additional benefits of vaccinations against shingles, pneumococcal disease, influenza, and RSV - that is, effects that go beyond the primary therapeutic benefit and make a significant contribution to healthy aging. These include a reduction in myocardial and cerebrovascular events, as well as a protective effect against neurodegenerative processes. In particular it has been demonstrated that the influenza vaccine is as effective as statins in the secondary prevention of myocardial infarction. These findings could lead to an increased acceptance of vaccination and, consequently, to an increase in vaccination rates, which remain insufficient. To continue improving the benefits of vaccination in the future, vaccines need to be further developed to achieve greater efficacy, which could also help increase acceptance of vaccination. Several new developments are currently in the pipeline, particularly for the pneumococcal vaccine. Since March 2025, a 21-valent pneumococcal conjugate vaccine for adults has been licensed in Europe, specifically targeting the serotypes that cause IPD in adults. Additional pneumococcal vaccine candidates covering up to 31 serotypes are currently in development using new technologies and are undergoing Phase 2 or 3 trials.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Older adults are highly susceptible to infections, some of which are vaccine preventable.
Evidence base and method
The Standing Committee on Vaccination (STIKO) regularly updates its vaccination recommendations for older adults as well.
Consensus and conclusions
In particular it has been demonstrated that the influenza vaccine is as effective as statins in the secondary prevention of myocardial infarction.
Controversies and gaps
These findings could lead to an increased acceptance of vaccination and, consequently, to an increase in vaccination rates, which remain insufficient.
Research and practice implications
To continue improving the benefits of vaccination in the future, vaccines need to be further developed to achieve greater efficacy, which could also help increase acceptance of vaccination.

新兴市场国家成人 RSV 疾病负担的系统回顾和建模分析

RSV Disease Burden in Adults in Emerging Market Countries, a Systematic Review and Modelling Analysis
作者： Sebastien Kenmoe, Bhanu Wahi-Singh, Pia Wahi-Singh, Cai-Hua Liang, Julia Regazzini Spinardi, Reiko Sato, Shu-Yue Deng, Elizabeth Begier, You Li, Harish Nair
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
摘要 背景 以往大多数关于成人 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 负担的研究都集中在高收入环境 (HIC)。
证据基础与方法
方法 我们进行了系统评价 (PROSEPRO: CRD420251137828),以确定报告 EMC 中 18 岁以上成人任何护理环境中 RSV 负担估计值的研究。
共识与结论
结果 我们纳入了 70 项研究,其中大多数来自中国 (30/70),PCR 是主要的诊断方法 (59/70)。考虑临床抽样和诊断测试后,调整后的 RSV 疾病 发病率 率(比未调整的高约 2.5 倍)范围为 18-49 岁成人中每 100,000 人中有 2,544 例(95% CI 1,352-4,787),到≥65 岁成人中每 100,000 人中有 2,392 例(1,618-3,534)。调整后的住院率随着年龄的增长而增加,从 18-49 岁的每 10 万人中 20 例(8-51 例)增加到 65 岁以上成人中的 74 例(3-2,027 例),调整后大约增加了 2.5 倍。无论设置或临床病例定义如何,调整后的 RSV 阳性比例相对稳定,范围为 1.8% (0.4-8.5) 至 9.7% (6.3-14.6)。
争议与缺口
The unadjusted in-hospital case fatality ratio (hCFR) was 6.0% (2.9-11.9) in adults ≥65 years vs 3.1 (2.0-5.0) in those 18-49 years. In-hospital CFR are comparable to or higher than in HIC.
科研与实践启示
随着最近批准用于成人的 RSV 疫苗,将流行病学认识扩展到新兴市场国家 (EMC) 对于制定预防策略至关重要。

RSV Disease Burden in Adults in Emerging Market Countries, a Systematic Review and Modelling Analysis

Authors: Sebastien Kenmoe, Bhanu Wahi-Singh, Pia Wahi-Singh, Cai-Hua Liang, Julia Regazzini Spinardi, Reiko Sato, Shu-Yue Deng, Elizabeth Begier, You Li, Harish Nair
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Most previous studies on adult respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) burden focus on high-income settings (HIC). With the recent approval of RSV vaccines for adults, extending epidemiological understanding to emerging market countries (EMC) is critical for informing prevention strategies. We aimed to characterize the burden of RSV among adults ≥18 years in EMC. Methods We conducted a systematic review (PROSEPRO: CRD420251137828) to identify studies reporting estimates of RSV burden from any care setting in adults>18 years in EMC. For each individual study included, we followed a two-step statistical approach to adjust for RSV case under-ascertainment related to the use of clinical specimen and diagnostic test. Random-effects meta-analysis was conducted to estimate RSV incidence rate, proportion positive, hospitalisation, and mortality burden. Findings We included 70 studies of which the majority were from China (30/70) and PCR was the predominant diagnostic method (59/70). Adjusted RSV disease incidence rate (approximately 2.5 times higher than unadjusted) ranged from 2,544 (95% CI 1,352-4,787) per 100,000 in adults aged 18-49 years to 2,392 (1,618-3,534) in adults ≥65 years after accounting for clinical sampling and diagnostic testing. Adjusted hospitalisation rates increased with age, from 20 (8-51) per 100,000 in 18-49 years to 74 (3-2,027) in adults ≥65 years, representing an approximate 2.5-fold increase after adjustment. The adjusted RSV proportion positive was relatively stable, ranging from 1.8% (0.4-8.5) to 9.7% (6.3-14.6), regardless of settings or clinical case definitions. The unadjusted in-hospital case fatality ratio (hCFR) was 6.0% (2.9-11.9) in adults ≥65 years vs 3.1 (2.0-5.0) in those 18-49 years. Among hospitalized RSV-positive adults, 95.8% (84.2-99.0) had at least one comorbidity, most commonly hypertension, diabetes, and COPD. Conclusion The estimated incidence and proportion positive of RSV disease in EMC is substantially lower than HIC. Narrow case definitions requiring fever, less sensitive testing such as direct fluorescent antibody testing, reduced healthcare access, and incomplete surveillance may be contributing to these lower estimates. In-hospital CFR are comparable to or higher than in HIC.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question

Evidence base and method
Methods We conducted a systematic review (PROSEPRO: CRD420251137828) to identify studies reporting estimates of RSV burden from any care setting in adults>18 years in EMC.

产前 MRI 诊断 和孤立性髓质被盖帽发育不良的良好神经发育结果:病例报告 和文献综述

Prenatal MRI Diagnosis and Favorable Neurodevelopmental Outcome in Isolated Medullary Tegmental Cap Dysplasia: A Case Report and Literature Review
作者： Julie Dobbs Sauveur, Éléonore Blondiaux, Mathilde Casson-Masselin, Timothée de Saint Denis, Lucie Guilbaud, Delphine Héron, Marie De Stefano, Lydie Bürglen, Stéphanie Valence, E. Blondiaux
摘要中文翻译髓质被盖发育不良(MTCD)是一种极其罕见的先天性脑干畸形,迄今为止报道的病例很少。我们报告了一名因枕部脑膜膨出而转诊的胎儿,妊娠 27 周时的胎儿 MRI 发现延髓背侧突出,与 MTCD 一致。未检测到幕上、幕下或脑外异常。包括染色体微阵列和三重外显子组测序在内的产前检查均无贡献。产后磁共振成像证实孤立的 MTCD 伴有枕部脑膜膨出,并通过弥散纤维束成像保存了脑桥横向纤维。除了呼吸道合胞病毒感染导致的短暂喂养困难和阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停之外,新生儿评估并无异常。16 个月大时,孩子表现出正常的神经发育,包括独立行走、语言和社交互动。该病例扩大了 MTCD 的表型谱,并表明当 MTCD 被分离且遗传研究呈阴性时,结果良好。胎儿影像学的识别对于产前咨询和产后管理至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注髓质被盖帽发育不良 (MTCD) 的产前 MRI 诊断 及其与良好的神经发育结局的关系。核心问题是,在没有其他异常的情况下,孤立的 MTCD 是否会导致积极的预后。
证据基础与方法
该综述是病例报告与文献综述相结合的综述。证据包括单个案例研究和文献中其他案例的参考。这些方法包括产前 MRI、染色体微阵列和三重全外显子组测序来调查病情。
共识与结论
遗传检查呈阴性的孤立 MTCD 可能会产生有利的神经发育结果。产后 MRI 证实 MTCD 存在,无其他异常,孩子在 16 个月时发育正常。
争议与缺口
证据仅限于文献中的单个 病例报告 和其他几个案例,可能无法提供足够的数据来得出广泛的结论。审查指出,产前检查没有贡献,而且缺乏对 MTCD 长期结果的全面研究。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来更好地了解 MTCD 的长期结果,并探索产前 MRI 在诊断和治疗此类病例中的作用。改进的基因检测和更详细的案例研究可以增强这种罕见疾病的证据基础。

Prenatal MRI Diagnosis and Favorable Neurodevelopmental Outcome in Isolated Medullary Tegmental Cap Dysplasia: A Case Report and Literature Review

Authors: Julie Dobbs Sauveur, Éléonore Blondiaux, Mathilde Casson-Masselin, Timothée de Saint Denis, Lucie Guilbaud, Delphine Héron, Marie De Stefano, Lydie Bürglen, Stéphanie Valence, E. Blondiaux
Original abstract (English)
Medullary tegmental cap dysplasia (MTCD) is an extremely rare congenital brainstem malformation, with few cases reported to date. We report a fetus referred for an occipital meningocele, in whom fetal MRI at 27 weeks’ gestation identified a dorsal protrusion of the medulla oblongata consistent with MTCD. No supratentorial, infratentorial, or extracerebral anomalies were detected. Prenatal investigations, including chromosomal microarray and trio whole-exome sequencing, were noncontributory. Postnatal magnetic resonance imaging confirmed isolated MTCD with an occipital meningocele and preserved transverse pontine fibers on diffusion tractography. Neonatal evaluation was unremarkable apart from transient feeding difficulties and obstructive sleep apnea in the context of RSV infection. At 16 months, the child showed normal neurodevelopment, including independent walking, language, and social interaction. This case expands the phenotypic spectrum of MTCD and suggests a favorable outcome when MTCD is isolated and genetic investigations are negative. Recognition on fetal imaging is essential for prenatal counseling and postnatal management.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the prenatal MRI diagnosis of Medullary Tegmental Cap Dysplasia (MTCD) and its association with favorable neurodevelopmental outcomes. The central question is whether isolated MTCD, in the absence of other anomalies, leads to a positive prognosis.
Evidence base and method
The review is a case report combined with a literature review. The evidence includes a single case study and references to other cases in the literature. The methods involved prenatal MRI, chromosomal microarray, and trio whole-exome sequencing to investigate the condition.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that isolated MTCD with a negative genetic investigation may result in a favorable neurodevelopmental outcome. Postnatal MRI confirmed the presence of MTCD without other anomalies, and the child showed normal development at 16 months.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence is limited to a single case report and a few other cases in the literature, which may not provide sufficient data to draw broad conclusions. The review notes that prenatal investigations were noncontributory, and there is a lack of comprehensive studies on the long-term outcomes of MTCD.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to better understand the long-term outcomes of MTCD and to explore the role of prenatal MRI in diagnosing and managing such cases. Improved genetic testing and more detailed case studies could enhance the evidence base for this rare condition.

怀孕和 疫苗 接受:犹豫驱动因素和孕产妇免疫策略的全球回顾

Pregnancy and vaccine acceptance: A global review of hesitancy drivers and strategies for maternal immunization
作者： Courage Chandipwisa, Agness Shimilimo, Amos M'yisa Makelele, Million Teklay Solomon
摘要中文翻译怀孕期间,女性由于生理状态和免疫应答的变化,更容易受到COVID-19、流感、百日咳、Tdap、呼吸道合胞病毒等病毒感染。各种研究表明,疫苗接种 对严重疾病和并发症具有强大的保护作用。本研究的目的是确定影响 疫苗 犹豫 (VH) 的因素,并提供可以改善母亲免疫的解决方案。本综述使用叙述综合方法来检查母亲 疫苗 的接受和犹豫。文献检索自 2015 年至 2025 年期间的 PubMed、Scopus 和 Google Scholar 等来源以及全球卫生机构 [疾病控制中心 (CDC)、联合国国际儿童紧急基金、世界卫生组织 (WHO) 和国家卫生服务部门]。提取并按主题合成有关 患病率、决定因素和干预措施的主要数据,以确定全球趋势、公平差距和改善孕产妇状况的综合策略疫苗 吸收。与接种疫苗的女性相比,感染 SARS-COV-2 或流感的未接种疫苗的女性面临严重疾病、住院、并发症和 死亡率 的风险更高。一项研究报告称,疫苗 在完整治疗方案中对严重 COVID-19 并发症的有效性为 48%(95% 置信区间 [CI]:22-65),在加强治疗后升至 76%(95% CI:47-89)。怀孕期间感染 疫苗接种 流感可使婴儿确诊病例减少 63%,并预防母亲和婴儿约三分之一的发热呼吸道疾病。孕产妇免疫对于保护母亲和婴儿至关重要,但 VH 在世界范围内持续存在。安全担忧、错误信息、低感知风险和机构不信任阻碍了人们的接受,尤其是在弱势群体中。加强提供者沟通、将 疫苗 纳入产前护理以及根据文化量身定制的干预措施对于确保公平获取和改善结果至关重要。本次审查不仅总结了犹豫不决的因素,还明确审查了孕产妇免疫政策中的规范与操作分歧,对比高收入和低收入和中等收入国家(LMIC)的情况,以确定实施差距和公平挑战。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 及其对孕妇的影响,研究影响 疫苗 犹豫的因素以及改善孕产妇免疫的策略。核心问题是确定 疫苗 犹豫的驱动因素,并提出提高母亲对 疫苗 吸收的解决方案。
证据基础与方法
该综述采用叙述综合方法来分析母亲 疫苗 的接受和犹豫。文献来源于 2015 年至 2025 年期间的 PubMed、Scopus 和 Google Scholar,以及 CDC、WHO 和 NHS 等全球卫生机构。按主题提取和综合了 患病率、决定因素和干预措施的数据。
共识与结论
孕产妇免疫对于保护母亲和婴儿免受严重 RSV 相关并发症至关重要。有证据表明,疫苗接种 显着降低孕妇及其婴儿患严重疾病、住院和 死亡率 的风险。该审查强调了解决安全问题、错误信息和机构不信任的重要性,以提高 疫苗 的采用率。
争议与缺口
关于特定人群(特别是低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC))孕产妇 RSV 免疫接种的有效性的证据有限。该审查指出了在了解 RSV 孕产妇免疫计划的实施挑战和公平问题方面存在的差距。
科研与实践启示
为了加强孕产妇对 RSV 的免疫,需要加强提供者沟通、将 疫苗 纳入产前护理以及根据文化定制干预措施。此外,还需要进一步研究来评估 RSV 疫苗 在中低收入国家中的有效性,并解决这些地区接受 疫苗 的障碍。

Pregnancy and vaccine acceptance: A global review of hesitancy drivers and strategies for maternal immunization

Authors: Courage Chandipwisa, Agness Shimilimo, Amos M'yisa Makelele, Million Teklay Solomon
Original abstract (English)
During pregnancy, women are more susceptible to viral infections such as COVID-19, influenza, pertussis, Tdap, and respiratory syncytial virus due to changes in the physiological state and immune responses. Various research illustrates that vaccination confers strong protection against severe diseases and complications. The aim of this research is to identify the factors that influence vaccine hesitancy (VH) and proffer solutions that can improve maternal immunization. This review used a narrative synthesis approach to examine maternal vaccine acceptance and hesitancy. Literature was retrieved from sources such as PubMed, Scopus, and Google Scholar, and also from global health agencies [Centers for Disease Control (CDC), United Nations International Children’s Emergency Fund, World Health Organization (WHO), and National Health Services] from 2015 to 2025. Major data on prevalence, determinants, and interventions were extracted and synthesized thematically to identify global trends, equity gaps, and integrated strategies for improving maternal vaccine uptake. Unvaccinated women who contract SARS-COV-2 or influenza face a higher risk of severe illness, hospitalization, complications, and mortality in comparison to those vaccinated. One study reported vaccine effectiveness against severe COVID-19 complications at 48% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 22-65) with a full regimen, rising to 76% (95% CI: 47-89) after a booster. Influenza vaccination during pregnancy reduced confirmed infant cases by 63% and prevented roughly one-third of febrile respiratory illness in mothers and infants. Maternal immunization is vital for protecting mothers and infants, yet VH persists worldwide. Safety concerns, misinformation, low perceived risk, and institutional distrust hinder uptake, especially among vulnerable groups. Strengthening provider communication, integrating vaccines into antenatal care, and culturally tailored interventions are essential to ensure equitable access and improved outcomes. This review goes beyond summarizing hesitancy drivers by explicitly examining the normative-operational divide in maternal immunization policy, contrasting high-income and low- and middle-income countries (LMIC) contexts to identify implementation gaps and equity challenges.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and its impact on pregnant women, examining factors influencing vaccine hesitancy and strategies for improving maternal immunization. The central question is to identify the drivers of vaccine hesitancy and propose solutions to enhance maternal vaccine uptake.
Evidence base and method
The review employed a narrative synthesis approach to analyze maternal vaccine acceptance and hesitancy. Literature was sourced from PubMed, Scopus, and Google Scholar, as well as global health agencies like the CDC, WHO, and NHS, between 2015 and 2025. Data on prevalence, determinants, and interventions were extracted and synthesized thematically.
Consensus and conclusions
Maternal immunization is crucial for protecting both mothers and infants from severe RSV-related complications. Evidence indicates that vaccination significantly reduces the risk of severe illness, hospitalization, and mortality in pregnant women and their infants. The review highlights the importance of addressing safety concerns, misinformation, and institutional distrust to improve vaccine uptake.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited evidence on the effectiveness of maternal immunization against RSV in specific populations, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). The review notes gaps in understanding the implementation challenges and equity issues in maternal immunization programs for RSV.
Research and practice implications
To enhance maternal immunization against RSV, there is a need for stronger provider communication, integration of vaccines into antenatal care, and culturally tailored interventions. Additionally, further research is required to evaluate the effectiveness of RSV vaccines in LMICs and to address the barriers to vaccine acceptance in these regions.

A high priorityPublic-health news

Illness Levels Over Time | Respiratory Illnesses - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) published an article on October 30, 2026, titled 'Illness Levels Over Time | Respiratory Illnesses,' which discusses respiratory illnesses, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). The article highlights RSV bronchiolitis and RSV infection as key health topics but does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers. It serves as a general overview of respiratory illnesses and their trends, without detailing investigation, testing, or prevention measures related to RSV. The CDC emphasizes the importance of understanding and addressing these illnesses, but the information is not specific to any outbreak or confirmed event. The article is part of the CDC's broader efforts to translate scientific findings into practical public health guidance.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

患有 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)(又称婴儿病)的患者中有一半年龄为 65 岁 - 매일경제

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报该新闻报道强调了韩国 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 住院患者的人口特征发生了重大变化,其中一半患者年龄在 65 岁或以上。据韩国疾病管理本部称,因RSV住院的老年人人数从2022年的433人急剧增加到2024年的2,032人,截至第40周,今年达到3,300人,是去年总数的1.7倍。这些老年患者中,56.8%伴有肺炎,25%转入重症监护室,住院死亡率率为10.6%。专家强调,需要改进针对老年人的监测系统和 疫苗接种 计划,因为目前的国家系统侧重于婴儿和产后护理中心的卫生管理。尽管肺炎和基础疾病恶化等严重并发症的风险很高,但政府尚未实施针对老年人 RSV 的国家 疫苗接种 计划,从而在公共卫生应对方面留下了空白。
查看新闻五要素
时间
批评人士指出,老年人迫切需要国家级的预防管理措施,因为一旦感染,很可能导致肺炎等致命的严重并发症或基础疾病恶化。
地点与对象
韩国65岁以上老年人口。
事件
据韩国疾病控制和预防中心报告,老年人口 RSV 住院人数增加,患者年龄构成发生显着变化。
规模、影响与风险
截至第40周,今年有3,300名患者因RSV住院,是去年患者总数(1,906人)的1.7倍。65岁以上住院患者占49.2%,占住院患者总数的一半。这些老年患者中有56.8%患有肺炎,25%被转入重症监护室。住院死亡率率达到10.6%,其中33%的60岁以上患者出院后6个月丧失日常生活能力。
应对、状态与不确定性
韩国疾病管理本部报告说,住院人数呈上升趋势。专家警告老年人患严重疾病的高风险,并呼吁改进监测系统和 疫苗接种 计划。政府目前重点关注婴儿和产后护理中心的卫生管理,但国家针对呼吸道合胞病毒的传染病管理体系存在空白。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Korean)
Half of the patients with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), known as infant disease, were 65 years - 매일경제
News Brief
The news report highlights a significant shift in the demographic profile of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) hospitalizations in South Korea, with half of the patients aged 65 or older. According to the Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, the number of elderly hospitalized with RSV has sharply increased from 433 in 2022 to 2,032 in 2024, and as of week 40, reached 3,300 this year, 1.7 times the previous year's total. Among these elderly patients, 56.8% were accompanied by pneumonia, and 25% were transferred to intensive care units, with an in-hospital mortality rate of 10.6%. Experts emphasize the need for improved monitoring systems and vaccination programs for the elderly, as the current national system focuses on hygiene management for infants and postpartum care centers. Despite the high risk of severe complications, such as pneumonia and worsening of underlying diseases, the government has not yet implemented a national vaccination program for RSV in the elderly, leaving a gap in public health response.
Original source text (Korean)

Critics point out that national-level preventive management measures are urgently needed for the elderly as there is a high risk of leading to fatal serious complications such as pneumonia or worsening of underlying diseases in case of infection. According to the Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention on the 3rd, the number of people aged 65 or older hospitalized with RSV infection in Korea has continued to increase sharply every year from 433 in 2022 to 1,130 in 2023 and 2032 in 2024. In particular, as of the 3rd of last month (week 40), when the full-fledged epidemic began, the number of patients has already reached 3,300 this year, 1.7 times the total number of patients (1906) last year. The number of patients as well as changes in composition ratio by age are clear. Of the total inpatients (6710) this year, 49.2% of those aged 65 or older accounted for half. Considering that it was only 5.2% in 2022, it shows that the burden of disease from RSV infection has been rapidly shifting from infants to the elderly in recent years. Experts warn that RSV infection in the elderly is at high risk of being directly linked to severe disease. This is because the virus' defense has declined due to immune aging that occurs with age. In addition, lung function and weakened respiratory muscles reduce the ability to spit out airway secretions such as phlegm, making them more vulnerable to infection. According to a study published in the international journal Scientific Report by a team of professors at Korea University Ansan Hospital, 56.8% of RSV hospitalized patients aged 65 or older in Korea were accompanied by pneumonia, and 25% were transferred to intensive care units. Their in-hospital mortality rate reached 10.6%. It is even more fatal for the elderly with underlying diseases. According to an analysis of RSV hospitalized patients aged 60 or older published in the international journal Journal of Infection Digits, 80% of COPD patients, 38% of heart failure patients, and 50% of asthma patients had worsened symptoms of the existing disease during inpatient treatment. In a study of 471 inpatients in the United States, new cardiovascular adverse reactions such as heart failure, atrial fibrillation, and myocardial infarction were identified in 37%. "Korea has limited adult RSV diagnostic tests and relies on a sample monitoring system, so it is easy to underestimate the actual disease burden of high-risk groups," said Park Jin-joo, a professor of circulatory medicine at Seoul National University Bundang Hospital. "We need to upgrade the monitoring system so that we can accurately grasp the overall disease burden and actively inform patients and their guardians of the risk of infection and the need for vaccination." The burden of disease continues for a long time even after acute treatment is over. According to the international academic journal 'Influenza and Arthur's Respiracy Virus', 33% of RSV hospitalized patients aged 60 or older lost their ability to perform daily life such as eating and bathing even six months after discharge, and 38.3% were readmitted within six months. 14% of discharged patients required a higher level of care and care than before admission. RSV, along with flu and COVID-19, is classified as a level 4 legal infectious disease that can be prevented by vaccines. However, the national infectious disease management system still has a wide gap. While the government provides free national vaccination (NIP) for the elderly aged 65 or older in the case of influenza and COVID-19, RSV still focuses only on hygiene management centered on infants and postpartum care centers and childcare facilities. Medical guidelines already specify vaccinations for the elderly. Major domestic and foreign institutions, including the Korean Infectious Society and the U.S. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), recommend a single dose of RSV vaccine for all adults over the age of 75 and high-risk groups aged 50 to 74 who suffer from chronic diseases or immunocompromised diseases. On the other hand, discussions on introducing domestic NIPs to ensure their access to vaccines have not even taken the first step. RSV infection in the elderly also leads to enormous socioeconomic losses. The international journal 'Journal of Virology' estimated that the total annual medical expenses related to RSV for the elderly exceed 1.48 trillion won. Another academic journal, "Pharmaco Economics Open," also analyzed that an average of 9.37 million won and 10.4 days of hospitalization for RSV infection are required. Experts point out that the burden of such diseases can be substantially relieved through vaccination. According to the international journal "Human Vaccine and ImmunoCeraputics", if people aged 60 or older receive RSV vaccines, direct and indirect costs such as treatment costs and productivity losses are expected to be reduced by a total of 20.675 trillion won. The loss of QALY, an indicator that reflects the deterioration of quality of life due to disease, can also be reduced by 244,424 years. Major overseas countries tend to recognize the cost-effectiveness of vaccines and quickly provide public support. Following the recommendation of vaccination for senior citizens aged 75 to 79, the UK has gradually expanded the scope of support to residents of nursing homes aged 80 or older and those aged 65 to 74 with underlying diseases this year. Australia also provides NIP to those aged 75 or older in consideration of the risk of hospitalization by age, and provides vaccination to indigenous Australians who have a relatively early risk of disease burden from the age of 60. In addition, Canada, Germany, and the United States are continuing to support vaccination for high-risk groups through public health insurance or state programs. Professor Park said, "It is time for Korea to discuss the introduction of NIP by comprehensively evaluating the burden of RSV disease, prevention effect, and cost-effectiveness of vaccines due to aging." Time
Critics point out that national-level preventive management measures are urgently needed for the elderly as there is a high risk of leading to fatal serious complications such as pneumonia or worsening of underlying diseases in case of infection.
Location and population
South Korea, elderly population aged 65 years or older.
Event
An increase in RSV hospitalizations among the elderly population, with a significant shift in the age composition of patients, reported by the Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention.
Scale, impact and risk
As of week 40, 3,300 patients were hospitalized with RSV this year, 1.7 times the total number of patients (1,906) last year. 49.2% of hospitalized patients aged 65 or older accounted for half of the total inpatients. 56.8% of these elderly patients had pneumonia, and 25% were transferred to intensive care units. The in-hospital mortality rate reached 10.6%, and 33% of patients aged 60 or older lost their ability to perform daily life six months after discharge.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention reported the increasing trend in hospitalizations. Experts warn of the high risk of severe disease in the elderly and call for improved monitoring systems and vaccination programs. The government currently focuses on hygiene management for infants and postpartum care centers, but there is a gap in the national infectious disease management system for RSV.

呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)
新闻简报呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是一个重大的全球健康威胁,特别是影响婴儿和老年人。婴儿期早期 RSV 感染会增加儿童哮喘的风险,尤其是有过敏或哮喘家族史的儿童。对于老年人来说,RSV 会导致严重的后果和心脏并发症,疫苗 可以有效地将 60 岁及以上成年人的住院率减少 80% 以上。孕产妇 疫苗接种 和 尼塞韦单抗 在新生儿风险最高的月份提供补充保护。世界卫生组织发布了第一份关于婴儿 RSV 免疫的立场文件,强调需要全年获得免疫接种,以尽量减少季节性 暴发。尽管取得了这些进步,但仍需要采取额外的干预措施,因为仅 HEPA 净化器并不能完全减少教室的病毒暴露。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于 2026-08-02。证据中没有报告具体的事件日期。
地点与对象
全球范围,特别关注婴儿、老年人以及早产儿、患有基础疾病的儿童、白血病患者等弱势群体。
事件
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是五岁以下儿童急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,对全世界儿童健康构成持续威胁。婴儿早期感染 RSV 会显着增加患儿童哮喘的风险,尤其是有过敏或哮喘家族史的儿童。RSV 还会导致老年人出现严重后果和心脏并发症。
规模、影响与风险
RSV 导致成人住院率较高,丹麦的一项大型真实世界随机试验发现,RSVpreF 疫苗 将 60 岁及以上成人与 RSV 相关的住院率降低了 80% 以上。单剂量的 RSV 疫苗 可以为老年人提供 58% 的住院保护,但对于免疫功能低下的个体来说,效果有所不同。全年接种 RSV 疫苗可能会减少全国主要季节性 暴发 的发生。
应对、状态与不确定性
儿科专家正在为呼吸道病毒季节期间 RSV 病例的预期增加做好准备。孕产妇 疫苗接种 和 尼塞韦单抗 共同提供补充策略,在新生儿风险最高的月份提供保护。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 发布了第一份关于保护婴儿免受 RSV 感染的免疫产品的立场文件。然而,HEPA 净化器并不能完全减少教室病毒暴露,这表明需要采取额外的干预措施。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV)

News Brief
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) remains a significant global health threat, particularly affecting infants and older adults. Early RSV infection in infancy increases the risk of childhood asthma, especially in children with a family history of allergy or asthma. For older adults, RSV causes severe outcomes and heart complications, with vaccines showing effectiveness in reducing hospitalizations by over 80% in adults aged 60 and over. Maternal vaccination and nirsevimab provide complementary protection for newborns during their highest-risk months. The WHO has issued its first position paper on RSV immunization for infants, emphasizing the need for year-round access to immunization to minimize seasonal outbreaks. Despite these advances, additional interventions are needed as HEPA purifiers alone do not fully reduce classroom viral exposure.
Original source text (English)
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Nirsevimab is a long-acting monoclonal antibody that provides season-long protection against RSV in infants, reducing medically attended infections and hospitalizations. Maternal vaccination and nirsevimab together offer complementary strategies to protect newborns during their highest-risk months. News article (Korean original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Korean)
Some postpartum care centers require respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccination, and admission i.. - 매일경제
News Brief
In September 2026, a postpartum care center in Jeju, Korea, experienced an RSV outbreak, infecting over 20 individuals, including newborns and staff, leading to a temporary closure. The Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention noted a record high of 22 RSV group infections in postpartum care centers last year, with 15 cases reported in the first half of 2026. RSV can cause severe respiratory diseases in infants and the elderly. In response, Pfizer plans to launch its RSV vaccine 'Avrisvo' in Korea in early December 2026, aiming to prevent infections in newborns and high-risk adults. This vaccine is expected to compete with existing options like Sanofi's 'Vaipotus' and GSK's 'Alexbee.' The situation highlights growing concerns about RSV in postpartum care settings and the increasing market competition for preventive measures.

a notice from a postpartum care center. In September, at a postpartum care center in Jeju, more than 20 people, including newborns and facility workers, were infected with RSV and the facility was temporarily closed. According to the Korea Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, the number of RSV group infections in postpartum care centers reached a record high of 22 last year, and 15 cases were reported in the first half of this year alone. RSV causes cold-like symptoms in healthy adults, but in infants and the elderly, it can lead to severe lower respiratory diseases such as capillary bronchitis and pneumonia. As infection concerns grow and prevention options increase, competition among pharmaceutical companies over the domestic market is also expanding. According to the pharmaceutical industry on the 3rd, Pfizer Pharmaceutical Korea is planning to launch its RSV vaccine "Avrisvo" in Korea in early December. It has been about four months since it received domestic product approval in August. By delivering antibodies produced by pregnancy inoculation to the fetus, Sanofi 'Vaipotus', which directly administers preventive antibodies to babies, competes for infant RSV prevention needs. It can also be used for the elderly and high-risk adults, so it will also enter the adult market dominated by GSK's "Alexbee." Existing domestic RSV preventive drugs are classified according to the target. AstraZeneca's "Synagis" is a preventive antibody used in high-risk children such as premature babies. Vapotus is a long-term preventive antibody that is also administered to general newborns and infants who are in their first RSV epidemic season. AlexB is an adult vaccine that induces an immune response in the body of an inoculated person. According to IQVIA, a pharmaceutical market research company, domestic sales of these three types reached 14.6 billion won in the first half of this year, up 109 percent year-on-year. Last year's annual sales were 25.2 billion won. 对于患有基础疾病的儿童来说,RSV 相关的医院费用很高

RSV-related hospital costs high in kids with underlying medical conditions
新闻简报CIDRAP 于 2026 年 11 月 3 日发表的一项研究强调,与没有 UMC 的儿童相比,因 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 住院的患有基础疾病 (UMC) 的儿童面临的医疗费用要高得多。该研究基于 2016 年 12 月 1 日至 2019 年 10 月 31 日期间收集的美国六个儿科医疗中心的数据,发现 UMC 中的神经系统或神经肌肉疾病导致的费用比没有 UMC 的费用高 4.4 倍。这些儿童的住院时间也更长,更有可能需要机械通气。作者认为,对 UMC 儿童进行 RSV 预防的成本效益可能比之前预测的还要高。该研究没有报告任何新的 暴发 或病例,但提供了有关 RSV 住院治疗对弱势群体的经济影响的见解。
查看新闻五要素
时间
本研究于2016年12月1日至2019年10月31日进行,成本以2025年美元调整。发表日期为2026-11-03。
地点与对象
本研究包括在美国六家儿科医疗中心住院的年龄小于2岁的儿童。
事件
CIDRAP 发表的一项研究发现,与没有 UMC 的儿童相比,患有 RSV 住院的患有基础疾病 (UMC) 的儿童的医疗费用明显更高。该研究描述了在这些人群中使用单克隆抗体的成本效益。
规模、影响与风险
在 6 至 11 个月大的患有 UMC 的儿童中,最昂贵的疾病是神经系统或神经肌肉疾病,导致费用比没有 UMC 的儿童高 4.4 倍。患有这些疾病的儿童住院时间也更长,并且更有可能需要机械通气。作者得出的结论是,对 UMC 儿童进行 RSV 预防的成本效益可能高于预期。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究基于 监测 数据,包括实验室确诊的 RSV 病例。该调查结果基于现有数据,不受正在进行的调查的影响。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV-related hospital costs high in kids with underlying medical conditions

News Brief
A study published on November 3, 2026, by CIDRAP highlights that children with underlying medical conditions (UMCs) hospitalized with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) face significantly higher medical costs compared to those without UMCs. The research, based on data from six U.S. pediatric medical centers collected between December 1, 2016, and October 31, 2019, found that neurologic or neuromuscular disorders among UMCs led to costs 4.4 times higher than those without UMCs. These children also had longer hospital stays and were more likely to require mechanical ventilation. The authors suggest that the cost-benefit of RSV prophylaxis in children with UMCs may be even greater than previously predicted. The study does not report any new outbreaks or cases but provides insights into the economic impact of RSV hospitalizations in vulnerable populations.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Main navigation News Antimicrobial Resistance COVID-19 Chronic Wasting Disease Influenza Public Health Alerts All Topics A-Z Coronavirus Vaccines Roadmap Influenza Vaccines Roadmap Roadmaps for Priority Pathogens Vaccine Integrity Project CIDRAP Leadership Forum . . Topics & Projects Antimicrobial Stewardship Chronic Wasting Disease Osterholm Update Podcasts Newsletters About CIDRAP CIDRAP in the News Our Staff Our Director Osterholm in the Press Shop Merchandise Contact Us About Search CLOSE Search Support Menu RSV-related hospital costs high in kids with underlying medical conditions News brief Stephanie Soucheray, MA Topics Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Share Copied to clipboard herjua / iStock Long-lasting monoclonal antibodies are recommended to children ages 8 to 19 months old during their second respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) season if they have underlying medical conditions (UMCs). A recent study in Pediatrics described the medical costs of RSV-associated hospitalizations for children with or without specific UMCs, providing in greater detail the cost-benefits of monoclonal antibody use in these populations. The study was based on surveillance from the New Vaccine Surveillance Network and included children aged younger than 2 years hospitalized at six pediatric medical centers with laboratory-confirmed RSV during December 1, 2016, to October 31, 2019. Direct medical costs were adjusted to 2025 dollars for the study. Those with neurologic, neuromuscular conditions see highest costs A total of 1,715 children with RSV were hospitalized, of which 67.5% had no UMC or history of prematurity. Among those with UMCs aged 6 to 11 months, the costliest conditions were neurologic or neuromuscular disorders, which resulted in costs 4.4 times those of kids hospitalized without UMCs. Children with neurologic or neuromuscular conditions also had the longest hospital stays (averaging 6.5 days), and were more likely to require mechanical ventilation. Among children in their second year of life, median hospital costs were highest among children with chronic lung disease, the authors said, but total costs were still highest among those with neurologic or neuromuscular conditions. “The cost-benefit for RSV prophylaxis in children with UMCs, including those beyond current recommendations, may be even greater than predicted,” the authors concluded. Quick takes: Clade 1 mpox in California, H9N2 avian flu cases in China, more Ebola deaths News brief Stephanie Soucheray, MA Topics Mpox Avian Influenza (Bird Flu) Ebola Viral Hemorrhagic Fever Officials in Stanislaus County, California, said four recent mpox cases were or may have been caused by clade 1, the deadlier strain of the virus. The strain typically circulates in Africa and is usually only seen in the United States in connection with international travel. Two of the cases are confirmed clade 1, two are suspected pending analysis, and three of the four case-patients reported not leaving Stanislaus County in the past year. The fourth patient’s travel history is still being investigated. Clade 1 has a case-fatality rate of 1% to 10%, compared with 1% to 4% for the more common clade 2. Both clades commonly spread via close contact, often from sexual contact or household transmission. For the first time since August, Hong Kong's Centre for Health Protection (CHP) is reporting human H9N2 avian flu cases, one each in Hubei and Jiangxi provinces. The Hubei patient is a 2-year-old boy with symptom onset on September 22, and the Jiangxi province case occurred in a 77-year-old woman with a September 16 symptom onset. There have been 22 other H9N2 cases reported on mainland China in the past six months, and a single case in Hong Kong. Since 2015, a total of 164 cases of human infection with H9N2 avian flu have been confirmed, including two deaths, both in people with underlying conditions. The Ebola outbreak centered in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) has now killed 5,376 people, including 5,372 in the DRC and two each in Kenya and Uganda. A total of 10,444 people have been sickened in this outbreak, which is entering its seventh month. The case-fatality rate is now over 50%, at 51.6%, as contact tracing remains limited and as there are no approved vaccines or therapeutics for the Bundibugyo strain of the virus. Both Ituri and North Kivu provinces remain the epicenter of the outbreak. The study included children aged younger than 2 years hospitalized at six pediatric medical centers in the United States.
Event
A study published by CIDRAP found that children with underlying medical conditions (UMCs) hospitalized with RSV had significantly higher medical costs compared to those without UMCs. The study described the cost-benefits of monoclonal antibody use in these populations.
Scale, impact and risk
Among children with UMCs aged 6 to 11 months, the costliest conditions were neurologic or neuromuscular disorders, resulting in costs 4.4 times higher than those without UMCs. Children with these conditions also had longer hospital stays and were more likely to require mechanical ventilation. The authors concluded that the cost-benefit for RSV prophylaxis in children with UMCs may be greater than predicted.
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was based on surveillance data and included laboratory-confirmed RSV cases. The findings are based on existing data and are not subject to ongoing investigation.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

早期流感,晚期呼吸道合胞病毒:感染预防专家在这个呼吸道季节应注意什么 - 今日感染控制

Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season - Infection Control Today
新闻简报Infection Control Today 于 2026 年 11 月 2 日发表的文章《早期流感,晚期 RSV:感染预防专家在这个呼吸道季节应该注意什么》强调了早期流感季节和晚期 RSV 季节的双重挑战,敦促医疗保健专业人员保持警惕。它讨论了感染预防策略的重要性,包括使用实时定位系统 (RTLS) 来改善手部卫生和工作流程监控。这篇文章还提到了马拉维的艾滋病毒项目,该项目通过协调护理和随访,显着降低了暴露婴儿的艾滋病毒阳性率。此外,它还注意到法国报告的首例沙蒙达病毒人类病例,引发了对 传播 和弱势群体的担忧。该文章强调了解决无菌处理中人为因素的必要性以及员工参与在可持续改进中的作用。它还涉及登革热预防方法的不确定性以及 基因组监测 和感染控制实践中持续存在的挑战。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布于2026-11-02。文章讨论了过去一个月的事件,包括2026年9月25日关于埃博拉病毒、莱姆病检测、器械处理倦怠以及更多内容的报告,以及2026年9月21日关于实时定位系统(RTLS)和手卫生的播客。
地点与对象
这篇文章主要关注美国,特别是医疗机构和感染预防专业人员。它还引用了马拉维 2026 年艾滋病会议和法国首次报告的人类沙蒙达病毒感染等国际事件。
事件
该文章重点介绍了早期流感季节和晚期呼吸道合胞病毒季节,强调感染预防人员需要监测这些趋势。它还讨论了各种与医疗保健相关的主题,包括使用实时定位系统 (RTLS) 进行手部卫生、马拉维艾滋病毒计划以及法国首例沙蒙达病毒人类病例。
规模、影响与风险
这篇文章报道了正在发生的流感和呼吸道合胞病毒季节,这是重大的公共卫生问题。它还提到了共享设备和缺失表面对抗菌素耐药性的影响,以及解决无菌处理中人为因素的重要性。马拉维艾滋病毒项目将暴露婴儿的艾滋病毒阳性率降低了 10 倍,法国首例沙蒙达病毒人类病例表明脆弱患者存在潜在风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
本文讨论了正在进行的调查和对各种公共卫生挑战的应对措施,包括使用 RTLS 进行手部卫生、铅围裙的标准化清洁,以及改善感染预防方面的沟通和参与的必要性。它还指出了预防登革热的蚊虫控制方法的不确定性以及员工参与对可持续改进的重要性。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season - Infection Control Today

News Brief
The article 'Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season' published on November 2, 2026, by Infection Control Today highlights the dual challenges of early flu season and late RSV season, urging healthcare professionals to remain vigilant. It discusses the importance of infection prevention strategies, including the use of real-time location systems (RTLS) to improve hand hygiene and workflow monitoring. The piece also references a Malawi HIV program that significantly reduced HIV positivity among exposed infants through coordinated care and follow-up. Additionally, it notes the first reported human case of Shamonda virus in France, raising concerns about transmission and vulnerable populations. The article emphasizes the need for addressing human factors in sterile processing and the role of staff engagement in sustainable improvements. It also touches on uncertainties in dengue prevention methods and the ongoing challenges in genomic surveillance and infection control practices.

早期流感,晚期呼吸道合胞病毒:感染预防专家在这个呼吸道季节应注意什么

Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season
新闻简报本文发表于 2026 年 11 月 2 日,讨论了由突变的 H1N1 毒株驱动的早期流感浪潮以及呼吸道季节期间 RSV 的延迟活动。它强调了新的mRNA流感病毒疫苗,预计它对老年人比传统的疫苗更有效,但可能会引起更多副作用,例如身体疼痛和低烧。CDC 报告称,截至 10 月 17 日的一周内,有近 5,000 人因流感住院,这是自 2010-2011 年以来的最高流感季节住院率。疫苗 的功效预计将于 12 月初进行评估。该文章还强调了 疫苗接种 对于高危人群(包括免疫功能低下个体和 65 岁及以上成年人)的重要性,并指出流感和 COVID-19 疫苗 可以一起服用。疫苗 的 监测 正在进行中,被动和主动监控均已到位。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于 2026 年 11 月 2 日。提到的活动日期是 10 月 3 日,监测 和讨论持续到 10 月 29 日。
地点与对象
文章讨论美国,重点关注西部和南部地区。它针对的是普通人群,特别是老年人和免疫功能低下的个体。
事件
该文章报道了由突变的 H1N1 病毒株引发的早期流感浪潮,并提到了 RSV 的延迟活性。它还讨论了新的 mRNA 流感 疫苗 及其潜在的副作用和功效。
规模、影响与风险
CDC 报告称,截至 10 月 17 日的一周内,有近 5,000 例经实验室确诊的流感住院病例,这是自 2010-2011 年季节以来 10 月中旬流感季节初的最高住院率。H1N1变种有新的突变,但初步数据表明疫苗可以识别它。mRNA 疫苗 可能比传统注射产生更多副作用,但其安全性很强,有望为严重疾病提供更好的保护。
应对、状态与不确定性
文章指出,mRNA 疫苗 受被动和主动安全 监测 的约束,包括 疫苗 不良事件报告系统。它还提到,早期疗效估计应在 12 月初提供。专家们讨论了 疫苗接种 对于高风险群体的重要性以及在赛季开始前更新 疫苗 的必要性。

Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season

News Brief
This article, published on November 2, 2026, discusses the early wave of influenza driven by a mutated H1N1 strain and the delayed activity of RSV during the respiratory season. It highlights the new mRNA influenza vaccine, which is expected to be more effective than traditional vaccines for older adults but may cause more side effects, such as body aches and low-grade fever. The CDC reported nearly 5,000 influenza hospitalizations during the week ending October 17, the highest early-season rate since 2010-2011. The vaccine’s efficacy is anticipated to be evaluated by early December. The article also emphasizes the importance of vaccination for high-risk groups, including immunocompromised individuals and adults 65 and older, and notes that flu and COVID-19 vaccines can be administered together. The surveillance of the vaccine is ongoing, with both passive and active monitoring in place.

News Media ICViewExpert PerspectivesMedical World NewsPathogen PlaybookPeer ExchangeShortsVideosWebinars Conferences Conference CoverageConference ListingAPIC Chapters Publication Infection Control TodaySupplements And Featured Publications More Resources CME/CEEditorial Advisory BoardJob BoardPartnersSponsoredWhitepapers Educator of the Year Educator of the Year Official Rules2025 Educator of the Year Winner2024 Educator of the Year Winner2023 Educator of the Year WinnerEducator of the Year Subscribe My Account News Media Conferences Publication Resources Subscribe Educator of the Year Advanced Technology Infection Intel Product Locator Bug of the Month COVID-19 Dental Infection Prevention The Clean Bite Environmental Services Clean Hospitals Corner HAIs Hand Hygiene IC Trends ICTalk Long-Term Care Long-Term Care Chronicles Operating Room Personal Protective Equipment Mask Respirator Policy Prevention IPLifeline Pathogen Pulse Contagious Conversations IPC Career Puzzles Sterile Processing Sterile Processing Perspectives Surface Disinfection Vascular Access Veterinary Infection Prevention Choose Topic Spotlight - Peer Exchange| Expert Perspectives| Bug of the Month| Contagious Conversations| Featured Articles| IC Trends| Pathogen Playbook Advertisement News|Articles|November 2, 2026 Early Flu, Late RSV: What Infection Preventionists Should Watch This Respiratory Season Author(s)Richard Payerchin Fact checked by: Tori Whitacre Martonicz, MA Two experts give an update ahead of the first season with a new mRNA vaccine against flu. Advertisement An early wave of influenza is being driven by a mutated H1N1 strain that only partly escapes population immunity, and this season’s vaccine still appears to recognize it, an influenza virologist said. Meanwhile, the new messenger RNA (mRNA) influenza vaccine will be watched through the same passive and active safety surveillance that covers every vaccine, said a vaccine researcher. Recipients may notice more side effects than with traditional shots. “However, data suggests this year’s influenza vaccine can recognize this variant, so the immune escape is only partial or rather incomplete,” said Andrew Pekosz, PhD, professor and vice chair of the W. Harry Feinstone Department of Molecular Microbiology and Immunology at the Johns Hopkins Bloomberg School of Public Health and co-director of the Johns Hopkins Center of Excellence in Influenza Research and Response. Pekosz spoke during an Oct. 29 media briefing hosted by the Bloomberg School with Anna Durbin, MD, professor in its Department of International Health and director of the Johns Hopkins Center for Immunization Research. The experts answered questions from multiple news outlets, including Infection Control Today. What do surveillance data show? The CDC reported almost 5,000 laboratory-confirmed influenza hospitalizations nationwide during the week ending Oct. 17, the highest early-season rate for mid-October since the 2010-2011 season, Durbin said. Activity is low but increasing, particularly in the West and South, Pekosz said. The H1N1 variant carries new mutations compared with last year’s virus, so some mismatch with the vaccine is expected, but preliminary CDC data show antibodies generated by the current vaccine recognize it. H1N1 accounted for 125 of 127 subtyped influenza A viruses in CDC surveillance for the week ending October 3. The vaccine’s other 2 components look like good matches for strains expected later, and early efficacy estimates should come in early December, Pekosz said. How will the mRNA vaccine be monitored? Every vaccine is subject to passive surveillance, in which anyone can report an adverse event to the Vaccine Adverse Event Reporting System, Durbin said. Health care systems and other networks also run active surveillance, searching for cases of adverse events. “So there’s not necessarily anything special for this new mRNA vaccine, these surveillance activities are going on all the time, but it is carefully looked at,” Durbin said. The mRNA vaccine can cause body aches and pains and a low-grade fever, and it probably has more side effects than traditional influenza vaccines, she said. Licensed for people 50 and older, it outperformed traditional vaccines in clinical trials in that age group. “Its safety profile is really strong, and it is an option that’s available for people that, at least the preliminary data suggests, could be better in terms of protecting you from severe disease,” Pekosz said. Who needs a protective shield? Immunocompromised people and adults 65 and older should get vaccinated, and older adults have higher-dose vaccines, Durbin said. Health systems have options there: a large real-world study found comparable effectiveness between adjuvanted and high-dose vaccines in older adults, Infection Control Today reported. Many older adults are infected by grandchildren and other children, so vaccinating the people around high-risk patients matters, Durbin said. “That’s really sort of giving a nice shield around the people to help prevent them from getting influenza,” Durbin said. How can patients tell the viruses apart? Trouble breathing suggests infection is moving deeper into the lungs and is probably not a cold, Pekosz said. Home tests can identify influenza, COVID-19 and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), which matters for high-risk patients because influenza and COVID-19 antivirals work only if started within a few days of symptom onset. Which vaccines should be reviewed now? Asked by Infection Control Today how often younger adults need pneumococcal vaccination, Pekosz said pharmacies can provide the recommendations. His broader advice was to ask about every eligible respiratory vaccine at once. “This is the time of year where all of those vaccines against respiratory infections should be looked at, and you should think about getting updated for them if you’re eligible to provide the most protection just before the season starts,” Pekosz said. COVID-19 activity is low but steady, with a surge expected in December and January. RSV has been unusually quiet, though Pekosz expects it within weeks. Flu and COVID-19 vaccines can be given together, Durbin said. Amid shifting federal guidance, Pekosz closed with a reminder. “Vaccines are the best protection, and getting vaccines early ensures you get the most protection out of those vaccines,” Pekosz said. RSV病毒威胁幼儿的健康;注意呼吸衰竭的风险。- 越南.vn

RSV virus threatens the health of young children; be aware of the risk of respiratory failure. - Vietnam.vn
新闻简报The article from Vietnam.vn, published on 2026-11-02, highlights the threat of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) to young children, particularly those under 2 years old, infants, and those with underlying health conditions. RSV can cause severe respiratory illnesses such as bronchiolitis and pneumonia, leading to respiratory failure. The Thanh Son Regional Health Center in Phu Tho province reports an increase in RSV cases, with some requiring intensive care. Globally, RSV is a leading cause of hospitalizations and deaths in children under 5, with WHO estimating over 3.6 million hospitalizations and 100,000 deaths annually. In Vietnam, the number of RSV cases has slightly increased compared to the previous year. The article emphasizes the importance of early detection, monitoring symptoms like rapid breathing, chest retraction, and wheezing, and seeking medical attention promptly. It also notes that there are no routine antiviral treatments for RSV, and supportive care is the primary approach. The article warns against self-medicating with antibiotics or other treatments without a doctor's guidance. The information is based on a syndicated summary, and the full original article body could not be extracted.

文章提到了越南的胡志明市和富寿省。指幼儿,特别是2岁以下的幼儿、婴儿、早产儿以及患有先天性心脏病、慢性肺病、免疫缺陷等基础疾病的儿童。
事件
文章报道,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对幼儿的健康构成重大威胁,导致呼吸衰竭和其他严重并发症。Thanh Son 地区卫生中心表示,已证实 RSV 可导致易感儿童出现 毛细支气管炎、肺炎和呼吸衰竭。
规模、影响与风险
RSV is noted as a common cause of respiratory infections in children, with an estimated 3.6 million hospitalizations and 100,000 deaths in children under 5 globally each year. In Vietnam, the number of RSV-related hospitalizations has increased slightly compared to the same period last year, with some cases requiring intensive care. The risk of severe complications is particularly high for children under 6 months old, premature babies, and those with underlying health conditions.

RSV virus threatens the health of young children; be aware of the risk of respiratory failure. - Vietnam.vn

Original source text (English)
English Login Homepage Topic News Political System Destination Event Travel Happy Vietnam Enterprise Product Heritage Museum Figure Multimedia Data Home Culture – Society Y tế RSV virus threatens the health of young children; be aware of the risk of respiratory failure. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) can cause bronchiolitis, pneumonia, and respiratory failure in young children, especially infants, premature babies, or those with underlying medical conditions. Báo Đầu tư•02/11/2026 Follow Vietnam.vn on Google Add Vietnam.vn on Google 0 During seasonal changes, respiratory illnesses in young children tend to increase, with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection being one of the notable causes. Although most cases can be cured with appropriate care, RSV still poses a risk of lower respiratory tract damage, requiring hospitalization and even intensive respiratory support. Children with RSV are being treated at a healthcare facility. Information from the Thanh Son Regional Health Center ( Phu Tho province ) indicates that the Pediatrics Department has recently received many cases of children infected with RSV. This virus is a common cause of respiratory infections in children, especially those under 2 years old, with the ability to spread rapidly through respiratory droplets, direct contact with infected individuals, or through hands and objects contaminated with the virus. Towards the end of the year, hospitals in Ho Chi Minh City are recording an increase in the number of patients suffering from respiratory illnesses. This trend typically occurs between October and December each year, when the rainy weather, high humidity, and fluctuating temperatures create favorable conditions for the spread of respiratory pathogens. At the Respiratory Department of Children's Hospital 1 (Ho Chi Minh City), there are currently about 300 children under treatment, with an average of 60-80 new patients admitted each day. Compared to the same period last year, the number of cases has increased slightly, and no unusual developments have been recorded. However, the worrying aspect is that many of these children are very young, with underdeveloped immune systems, making them at risk of serious complications if they contract lower respiratory tract infections. The 2019 Global Burden of Disease report, presented at a recent conference, revealed that RSV claims the lives of approximately 66,300 children under one year old each year. It is also one of the leading causes of death from lower respiratory tract infections, including pneumonia and bronchiolitis, in children under 12 months of age. In Vietnam, studies on the burden of RSV have been conducted since 2010. Each year, tens of thousands to hundreds of thousands of cases require hospitalization due to RSV-related illnesses, including children who need treatment in intensive care units. Tìm hiểu lịch sử The burden extends beyond just children's health, as the increasing number of pediatric patients risks overwhelming the healthcare system, impacting its ability to accommodate and care for other children. In severe cases requiring prolonged intensive care, the costs for families can be substantial. RSV can circulate year-round, with varying rates depending on weather conditions and local epidemiological characteristics. Worryingly, the initial symptoms often resemble those of a common cold, leading parents to underestimate the disease or delay bringing their children to medical facilities. In the early stages, children infected with RSV typically present with a runny nose, nasal congestion, sneezing, coughing, fever, fussiness, poor feeding, or reduced appetite. Some cases may show signs of fatigue and restless sleep, while infants may not have a fever or may only experience subtle changes in feeding ability and activity level. However, the infection can worsen in the following days if the virus damages the lower respiratory tract. Children may develop symptoms such as frequent coughing, rapid breathing, wheezing, shortness of breath, respiratory muscle retraction, or chest wall retraction. These manifestations may be related to bronchiolitis, pneumonia, and the risk of respiratory failure. In particular, children under 6 months old, premature babies, children with congenital heart disease, chronic lung disease, or immunodeficiency are at high risk of serious complications. Their underdeveloped respiratory systems and limited immune response make them vulnerable to inflammation, edema, and increased mucus production in the airways. In newborns, the progression of the disease is sometimes more difficult to recognize because respiratory symptoms are not yet clear. Infants may feed less, be less active, react slowly, or experience episodes of apnea. If not detected and treated promptly, respiratory failure can be life-threatening. You may also like The severity of RSV is also reflected in the global burden of disease. The World Health Organization (WHO) estimates that this virus causes more than 3.6 million hospitalizations and approximately 100,000 deaths in children under 5 years old worldwide each year. These figures show that RSV is not simply a cause of the common cold. While many children can recover on their own, the risk of severe complications in young children and those with underlying health conditions requires families to be especially cautious in monitoring their health. Medical experts advise that close monitoring of the disease is crucial for early detection of cases requiring medical intervention. Parents should observe their child's breathing rate, ability to feed or eat, level of alertness, and any unusual changes in their daily activities. If a child is breathing rapidly, has difficulty breathing, chest retraction, progressively worsening wheezing, significantly poor feeding, or shows signs of dehydration, the family should take the child to a medical facility for examination. Symptoms such as cyanosis of the lips, cessation of breathing, lethargy, difficulty waking up, or clear respiratory distress are emergency signs requiring immediate treatment. A key concern is that there are currently no routinely used antiviral drugs specifically for treating RSV infection. Treatment primarily focuses on supportive care, ensuring adequate fluid intake, controlling symptoms, and providing respiratory support depending on the severity of the illness. Severe cases may require oxygen therapy, respiratory support, or treatment in an intensive care unit. Parents should not self-medicate with antibiotics, cough medicine, or other treatments without a doctor's prescription. Antibiotics do not kill RSV and should only be considered in cases where there is evidence or suspicion of a co-existing bacterial infection requiring treatment. Besides early detection, proactive prevention plays a crucial role because children who have previously been infected with RSV are still susceptible to reinfection. Immunity acquired after an illness does not guarantee long-term protection, and subsequent infections can still cause illness, especially in high-risk children. To limit the spread of the virus, families should maintain a habit of frequent handwashing with soap, sanitize toys and frequently touched surfaces, and limit children's close contact with people who are coughing, have a fever, or have respiratory symptoms. Caregivers should pay attention to hand hygiene before holding, feeding, or performing daily care activities. The living environment needs to be kept well-ventilated and clean, free from cigarette smoke and other factors that can irritate the respiratory tract. For infants, premature babies, or those with underlying medical conditions, limiting exposure to sources of infection is even more crucial, especially in crowded places. Along with hygiene measures and infection control, medicine now has additional solutions for preventing RSV. The WHO recommends the use of appropriate RSV vaccines for pregnant women to deliver antibodies to protect the child in the first months of life, or the use of long-acting monoclonal antibodies for children in the indicated group. These measures aim to reduce the risk of serious lower respiratory tract infections caused by RSV, especially during the early and vulnerable stages of childhood. However, the choice of preventive solution should be based on age, health status, maternal vaccination history, access to products, and current professional guidance. Parents should consult directly with their doctor to receive advice on appropriate protection options, avoiding confusion between vaccines used for pregnant women and monoclonal antibodies intended for young children. You may also like Previously, regarding the RSV vaccine, according to Dr. Phan Phong Ky of the Long Chau Pharmacy and Vaccination System, the RSV vaccine stimulates the immune system to produce antibodies against the F protein of the virus – a protein that helps RSV attach to and invade respiratory tract cells. The specific antibodies produced help reduce the risk of severe respiratory illness caused by RSV. One of the RSV vaccines currently available is Abrysvo. For eligible pregnant women, vaccination aims to immunize the mother and transfer antibodies through the placenta to protect the baby in the first few months after birth. Besides maternal vaccines, monoclonal antibodies are another preventive option. Unlike vaccines that stimulate the body to create its own immunity, this method directly delivers antibodies to the child, thereby providing protection without depending on the child's own immune response. Experts emphasize that the choice of preventive measures should be based on age, health status, pregnancy, and professional recommendations, rather than making decisions on one's own. RSV is so common that most children are likely to have been infected at some point in their early years, but the consequences of the disease are not the same for every child. In children under 1 year old, especially in the first few months after birth, the time from cold-like symptoms to bronchiolitis, pneumonia, and respiratory distress can be very short. Therefore, in addition to early recognition of dangerous signs and timely medical examination for children, proactive prevention and access to appropriate preventive measures are becoming an important part of the strategy to reduce hospitalizations, complications, and deaths from RSV in young children. View five news elements
Time
The article was published on 2026-11-02. The event date is not explicitly stated, but the trend of increased RSV cases is noted during the period of October to December, with a mention of the current season being towards the end of the year.
Location and population
The article mentions Ho Chi Minh City and Phu Tho province in Vietnam. It refers to young children, especially those under 2 years old, infants, premature babies, and children with underlying medical conditions such as congenital heart disease, chronic lung disease, or immunodeficiency.
Event
The article reports that respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a significant threat to young children's health, causing respiratory failure and other severe complications. It is confirmed that RSV can lead to bronchiolitis, pneumonia, and respiratory failure in vulnerable children, as stated by the Thanh Son Regional Health Center.
Scale, impact and risk
RSV is noted as a common cause of respiratory infections in children, with an estimated 3.6 million hospitalizations and 100,000 deaths in children under 5 globally each year. In Vietnam, the number of RSV-related hospitalizations has increased slightly compared to the same period last year, with some cases requiring intensive care. The risk of severe complications is particularly high for children under 6 months old, premature babies, and those with underlying health conditions.
Response, status and uncertainty
The response includes recommendations for early detection, monitoring symptoms, and seeking medical attention promptly. It also highlights the lack of routine antiviral drugs for RSV and the importance of supportive care. The article is a syndicated summary, and the full original article body could not be extracted.

住院成人中超过一半与 RSV 相关的死亡发生在出院后 - Pharmacy Times

Over Half of RSV-Related Deaths in Hospitalized Adults Occur After Discharge - Pharmacy Times
新闻简报CDC 最近的一份报告强调,住院成人中与 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 相关的死亡中有一半以上发生在出院后。该研究基于 监测 数据,发现大约 10% 因 RSV 住院的成年人在住院期间或出院后 60 天内死亡。这一信息强调了对 RSV 患者持续监测和出院后护理的重要性。该研究结果由《药房时报》于 2026 年 10 月 30 日发表,强调医疗保健专业人员在处理医院以外的 RSV 相关并发症时需要保持警惕。该报告没有提供病例数量或确切风险水平的具体细节,但表明即使患者出院后,风险仍然很大。该研究是了解和减轻 RSV 对成年人群影响的更广泛努力的一部分。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Over Half of RSV-Related Deaths in Hospitalized Adults Occur After Discharge - Pharmacy Times

News Brief
A recent report from the CDC highlights that over half of the deaths related to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in hospitalized adults occur after discharge. The study, based on surveillance data, found that approximately 10% of adults hospitalized with RSV died either during their hospitalization or within 60 days of being discharged. This information underscores the importance of continued monitoring and post-discharge care for RSV patients. The findings were published on October 30th, 2026, by Pharmacy Times, emphasizing the need for healthcare professionals to remain vigilant in managing RSV-related complications beyond the hospital setting. The report does not provide specific details on the number of cases or the exact risk level, but it suggests that the risk remains significant even after patients leave the hospital. The study is part of broader efforts to understand and mitigate the impact of RSV on adult populations.

B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV-related hospital costs high in kids with underlying medical conditions - CIDRAP

News Brief
A recent CIDRAP news brief draws attention to the financial burden of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) hospitalizations in children who already have underlying medical conditions. While the piece underscores that costs are notably high for this vulnerable group, it does not supply exact cost amounts, case numbers, or mortality figures. The brief does not identify a geographic location, nor does it describe any ongoing investigations, treatment protocols, or public‐health interventions related to these expenses. Consequently, the observation remains limited to a cost‐related concern, with no further data or response actions detailed. This highlights a gap in publicly available information on the economic impact of RSV among high‐risk pediatric patients.

像瓦斯无声无息! 一旦感染RSV病毒,恐陷3大风险

Original source text (Chinese)
新闻 体育 汽车 房产 旅游 教育 时尚 科技 财经 娱乐 像瓦斯无声无息! 一旦感染RSV病毒,恐陷3大风险 2026-11-04 00:00 来源: 王大爷的招呼 发布于:山西省 秋冬呼吸道疾病盛行,呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的威胁常被忽略,如瓦斯般无声无息,看似平静,却可能迅速演变成健康危机。初期症状可能类似感冒,但对年长者、慢性病患者、养老机构住民及婴幼儿而言,感染后可能进一步造成 肺炎、原有慢性病恶化、甚至失能与死亡。 呼吸道合胞病毒是一种常见的、具有高度传染性的呼吸道病毒,主要的传染途径是飞沫传播和接触传播。RSV有A、B两型流行,一年四季都可能出现病例,且可能终身反复感染。 老年人感染RSV后就诊、住院及死亡风险增加,此外还可诱发原有慢性基础疾病加重,导致不良临床结局。我国人口老龄化进程在加快,但目前尚未有针对老年RSV感染的特效治疗手段,也缺乏有效的预防性疫苗,使得RSV疾病负担进一步加重。RSV是引发老年人肺炎的重要病因之一,每10名因肺炎住院的老年人中,就有1名感染RSV,且感染RSV造成肺炎的几率为流感的2.7倍。 RSV传染性强,1人可传给3人,且对高龄人群而言,RSV感染的冲击不一定随出院或痊愈就消失。50岁以上RSV住院患者中,约2成会发生急性心脏事件,部分并发症甚至持续到出院后180天,甚至约3成长者在住院6个月后日常生活仍受影响,且四分之一在住院的6到12个月仍感到呼吸困难。 RSV对慢性病患者的威胁也不容低估。国际研究显示,慢性病患者感染RSV后的住院风险较健康同龄者高出3至10倍。以慢性阻塞性肺疾病(COPD)患者为例,感染RSV后住院风险较一般人增加9.7倍、发生重症,如入住监护病房或住院死亡风险上升1.9倍,且与流感新冠相比,COPD患者感染RSV更容易因病情恶化就医、住院,甚至需要使用呼吸器。 相较于流感、新冠,RSV没有专一性病毒治疗药物,因此主动预防至关重要。一旦老年人感染RSV住院,平均住院天数高达32天,并对家庭造成沉重的医疗负担。 RSV也可能在家中造成群聚感染,研究数据显示,约有4成RSV感染者没有症状,可能在不知不觉间将病毒传播给周遭的人,当家庭同时有幼儿、老年人或慢性病患者时,病毒可能在家户间传播,形成「一人感染、全家承担」的健康与照护风险。 面对不同的呼吸道疾病,民众应建立「完整防护」的观念,医师提醒,除了流感、新冠、肺炎链球菌与百日咳疫苗,民众也应依年龄及个人健康风险与医师讨论是否需要接种RSV疫苗,逐一盘点、补齐适合自己的「五重防护」。 (中时)返回搜狐,查看更多 阅读 () 平台声明 该文观点仅代表作者本人,搜狐号系信息发布平台,搜狐仅提供信息存储空间服务。

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

RSV 疫苗 导致医院婴儿数量大幅减少:您需要了解的 4 件事

RSV vaccines are leading to far fewer babies in hospitals: 4 things you need to know
新闻简报母体 RSV 疫苗接种 在减少婴儿因 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染住院方面显示出显着的益处。最近的一项研究发现,在英格兰,2024 年 9 月至 2026 年 3 月期间,三个月以下婴儿的 毛细支气管炎 入院率比预期低 62.6%,即使平均 毛细支气管炎 率为 55%。在布宜诺斯艾利斯,也出现了类似的下降,住院人数下降了 33.6%。发表在《柳叶刀区域健康 - 欧洲》上的对 21 项研究的系统回顾和荟萃分析显示,孕产妇 疫苗接种 可将住院风险降低 73.6%。这些研究结果表明,RSV 疫苗 正在有效减轻医院的负担并保护弱势婴儿。然而,这些研究考察了不同的年龄组并使用了不同的方法,使得直接比较具有挑战性。没有重大安全问题的报告,在超过 16,000 次怀孕中没有观察到早产风险增加。这些证据基于真实世界的数据,是确保 疫苗 安全的持续监测工作的一部分。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026年11月2日(发布日期)、2024年9月至2026年3月(活动期间)。
地点与对象
英格兰、布宜诺斯艾利斯以及美国、英国、阿根廷和法国等其他地区。目标实体:呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)及其相关毒株(RSV-A、RSV-B)。
事件
母亲 RSV 疫苗接种 与婴儿因 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染而入院的人数减少有关。现实世界的研究以及系统评价和荟萃分析证实了这一点。Linda Geddes、Gavi.org 和 Eurosurveillance 报道了这一事件。
规模、影响与风险
在英格兰,2024 年 9 月至 2026 年 3 月期间,三个月以下婴儿因 RSV 相关 毛细支气管炎 住院人数比预期减少了 62.6%。毛细支气管炎 重症监护入院人数减少了 41%。在布宜诺斯艾利斯,入院人数下降了 33.6%。明确指出,因 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染住院的风险降低了 73.6%。
应对、状态与不确定性
现实世界的研究正在进行中,研究结果基于多项研究的汇总数据。这些研究使用了不同的年龄组和方法,因此很难进行直接比较。这些证据得到了系统评价和荟萃分析的支持,并且没有报告重大的安全问题。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV vaccines are leading to far fewer babies in hospitals: 4 things you need to know

News Brief
Maternal RSV vaccination is showing significant benefits in reducing hospital admissions for RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections in babies. A recent study found that in England, admissions for bronchiolitis among infants under three months were 62.6% lower than expected between September 2024 and March 2026, even with an average vaccination rate of 55%. In Buenos Aires, similar reductions were observed, with a 33.6% drop in hospitalisations. A systematic review and meta-analysis of 21 studies, published in The Lancet Regional Health – Europe, revealed that maternal vaccination reduces the risk of hospitalisation by 73.6%. These findings suggest that RSV vaccines are effectively lowering the burden on hospitals and protecting vulnerable infants. However, the studies examined different age groups and used varying methods, making direct comparisons challenging. No significant safety concerns were reported, with no increased risk of premature birth observed in over 16,000 pregnancies. The evidence is based on real-world data and is part of ongoing monitoring efforts to ensure vaccine safety.
Original source text (English)
News from the lab RSV RSV vaccines are leading to far fewer babies in hospitals: 4 things you need to know From protecting individual babies to reducing hospital admissions, studies from around the world are revealing the benefits of RSV vaccination. 2 November 2026 4 min read by Linda Geddes Republish this article Disclaimer If you would like to republish this article, please follow these steps: use the HTML below; do not edit the text; include the author’s byline; credit VaccinesWork as the original source; and include the page view counter script. <article> <h1> <span>RSV vaccines are leading to far fewer babies in hospitals: 4 things you need to know</span> </h1> <div> <div><p>From protecting individual babies to reducing hospital admissions, studies from around the world are revealing the benefits of RSV vaccination. </p></div> </div> <ul> <li> <b>2 November 2026</b> </li> <li> <b class="me-2">by</b> <span> <a href="https://www.gavi.org/vaccineswork/authors/linda-geddes" hreflang="en">Linda Geddes</a> </span> </li> </ul> <div> <div> <div> <div> <div> <p> </p><div><h4>At a glance</h4><ul><li>A recent study found that maternal RSV vaccination reduced babies’ risk of being hospitalised with RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections by 73.6%.</li><li>Countries that have introduced maternal RSV vaccination programmes are also seeing reductions in hospitalisations at a population level. Maternal vaccination cuts babies’ risk of being hospitalised with RSV by nearly three quarters.</h3><p>RSV is a common virus that affects the nose, throat and lungs. Although most infections are mild, it is a leading cause of lung infections and hospitalisation in young children worldwide. </p><p>Babies are particularly vulnerable because their airways are small and their immune systems are still developing. </p><p>Clinical trials established that maternal vaccination could protect babies against serious RSV illness during their first months of life, by allowing protective antibodies to pass from mother to baby across the placenta.</p><p>Now, real-world studies are revealing how well this protection holds up across larger and more diverse populations.</p><p>A recent systematic review and meta-analysis published in<em> </em><a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S2666776226002590"><em>The Lancet Regional Health – Europe</em></a>, combined evidence from 21 real-world studies of maternal RSV vaccination published between July 2024 and September 2025. </p><p>Most of the studies came from the US, with others from the UK, Argentina and France. </p><p>When the researchers pooled data from four of these studies that specifically assessed vaccine effectiveness against hospitalisation in babies under six months old, they found that RSV vaccines reduced babies’ risk of being hospitalised with RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections by 73.6%. </p><h3>2. benefits at a population level, with substantial reductions in hospital admissions for RSV-related illness among young infants. </p><p>In England, where maternal RSV vaccination was introduced in infection that can cause breathing difficulties) among babies younger than three months were 62.6% lower than expected between September 2024 and March 2026, even though maternal vaccination only averaged around 55% during this period. </p><blockquote><h2>RSV vaccines reduced babies’ risk of being hospitalised with RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections by 73.6%. </h2></blockquote><p>In Argentina, researchers examined admissions for RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections among babies younger than six months at hospitals in <a href="https://www.thelancet.com/journals/lanam/article/PIIS2667-193X(25)00307-2/fulltext">Buenos Aires</a>. </p><p>They estimated that admissions were 33.6% lower than expected following the introduction of maternal vaccination in 2024. </p><p>Although the reductions reported in England and Argentina differed, the studies examined different age groups and used different methods, making direct comparisons difficult. </p><p>Other factors, including differences in RSV circulation between seasons may also have influenced hospital admission rates. </p><p>Even so, the findings suggest
